17. **Avatar URL cache churn (RESOLVED in V10 round 3, Decision W):** every response signs a fresh `avatar_url` (Decision R), so a browser refetches avatar images when a board, comment list, or member list is loaded. The owner accepted the recommendation: no change now; if a board with many authors feels slow, give avatar signatures a stable `exp` per 15-minute bucket (a later, additive change). Recorded in the design under "Accepted risks recorded in round 3".
18. **Avatars beside Activity_Log rows and Notifications (RESOLVED in V10 round 3, Decision W):** Decision U lists Comments, Board members, candidates, and Card assignees. Activity rows and Notifications carry `actor_name` only and no avatar field, so any avatar the Frontend draws beside them is initials. The owner accepted the recommendation: leave as is; no avatar field is added to activity rows or Notifications.
19. **Logo files are raster, not vector (V11, open):** both SVG files wrap a PNG (Glossary: Embedded_Raster), so they cannot be recoloured with CSS, will look soft above their `viewBox` size and on screens above 1.33 device pixels per CSS pixel, and ignore the dark-mode and design-token rules. Recommendation: ask the owner for true vector SVGs with a transparent background; the files can be replaced in place with the same names and `viewBox` and no code change (Req 21, criterion 16).
20. **Other icon sizes (V11, open):** only `favicon.ico` is provided. No `icon.png`, `apple-icon.png` (180 by 180, `jpg` or `png` only in Next.js), or web app manifest icons (192 and 512) exist. A 180 pixel icon could be cut from the Embedded_Raster, but that would be a derived, opaque image the owner has not approved. Recommendation: ask the owner for a square 512 pixel PNG and add `app/icon.png`, `app/apple-icon.png`, and `app/manifest.ts` in a follow-up.
21. **Opaque logo background on the brand panel (V11, open):** the Embedded_Raster has no alpha, so on the navy gradient of the login and register brand panel the logo appears as a rectangle. V11 places it on a `bg-surface-raised` tile (Req 21, criterion 9). Recommendation: ask for a transparent (or white-on-transparent) variant for dark backgrounds.
22. **Board owner removing members and assignments (V12, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V12):** the Board_Owner keeps the right to remove a Board_Member, as written in Requirement 22 criterion 10 and Requirement 3 criteria 12 and 14; the removal clears that user's assignments on the Board. Removal is reversible through a new request. The owner row in the Sidebar has no remove action (Requirement 22 criteria 19 and 20).
23. **Owner as a Board member (V12, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V12):** the Sidebar Team members list shows the Board_Owner first, tagged "Owner", with no remove action on that row (Requirement 3 criterion 24; Requirement 22 criteria 13, 19, and 20). The members endpoint returns the owner with `is_owner`; the member search never lists the owner (ACC-09); the assignee popover lists the owner first and lets staff assign them (Requirement 4 criterion 33); tests are Requirement 15 criteria 36 and 37.
24. **Join_Request spam and Email for approvers (V12, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V12):** (a) no cooldown after a rejection, and no rate limit on Join_Request creation (Requirement 24 criterion 12 stands); (b) the pending join-request list shows each requester's Email together with the full name and the Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar, to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin only. This reverses the earlier "no Email in the pending list" choice and amends Decisions M and S: the Email now has three staff-only places (candidates, assignee popover, pending join-request list). Requirement 24 criteria 13, 19, and 27; Requirement 3 criterion 31; Requirement 15 criteria 36 and 37.
25. **Comment creation activity (V12, RESOLVED BY DEFAULT in round 2 within V12; the owner did not answer, so the recommendation written here applies):** Requirement 25 criterion 12 gives Subtasks and Attachments their own Activity_Log rows and Requirement 26 adds `comment.deleted`, but a created Comment writes no `comment.created` row; the Comment thread is its own history. No requirement changes. The owner can reverse this by asking for a `comment.created` action.
26. **Role change cleanup (V12, RESOLVED BY DEFAULT in round 2 within V12; the owner did not answer, so the recommendation written here applies):** Requirement 22 criterion 6 deletes memberships and pending requests when an Admin makes a user a Manager or an Admin. Demoting a Manager to Member leaves them with no Board access until a Manager or an Admin adds them or a Join_Request is approved; this is accepted as a rare case. No requirement changes.
27. **Rejected requester destination (V12, RESOLVED BY DEFAULT in round 2 within V12; the owner did not answer, so the decision already written applies):** the brief said board notifications open `/boards/{id}`; for `board_join_rejected` the requester has no access, so the row opens `/boards` instead (Requirement 28, criterion 5). The owner can reverse this.
28. **Old Share_Link links (V12, resolved by Decision Z; unchanged in round 2 within V12):** the owner of a Board who enabled a link in V10 loses the right to manage it (Decision Z); the data was reset, so no existing link is affected.
31. **Server type (V14, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** the current server is an unprivileged container that cannot run containers, so it runs in bare mode (Gunicorn, Next.js, and the scheduler under `supervisord`, no Docker). Docker mode stays the target for a VM or AWS EC2. Requirement 33 criteria 1, 3, and 8 apply to Docker mode; for bare mode criterion 8 is met by supervisord, which the server container starts at boot.
32. **Reset migrations (V14, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2):** Decision AN is approved and implemented.
33. **Nginx port (V14, RESOLVED by inspection in round 2):** Nginx listens on port 80 inside the server and public port 8082 is forwarded to it (inferred: nothing inside listens on 8082). Port 8000 on loopback is already used by another process and must not be reused.
34. **Uploads and backups (V14, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2):** `/var/lib/revoboard/uploads`, and a `pg_dump` before each deploy with the last 7 kept. Off-server backup is not specified.
35. **Request body limit (V14, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2):** 3 MB, which also caps attachments at about 3 MB.
36. **Landing page (V14, RESOLVED in V16 by Requirement 41; the redirect of signed-in visitors is withdrawn):** proposed a public route group in the same Next.js app at `/`, with signed-in visitors sent to `/boards`. It gets its own version when its content is known.
37. **Directory layout (V14, open):** proposed the whole monorepo at `/srv/revoboard/app`, configuration at `/srv/revoboard/.env`, and only mutable data under `/var`.
38. **Domain names (V16, open, not blocking the spec):** the planned domain in DEP-13 is `revoboard.belajardeploy.web.id`. V16 assumes the Site_Host is that name and the App_Host is `app.` followed by it. Both come from environment variables (`SITE_URL`, `APP_URL`), so the owner can change them without a code change. The certificate must cover both names.
39. **Rail and Top_Nav carry the same destinations (V16, default applied):** the prototype draws both. V16 keeps both because the owner chose the prototype shell: the rail is the icon-only shortcut and the Top_Nav is the labelled one. The owner may drop one later; only SHELL-01 to SHELL-08 change.
40. **Team destination (V16, default applied):** for a Member, Supervisor, or Manager the Team item opens the Team_View for the active Board. For an Admin it opens `/admin/users`, because an Admin has no Board access by default (Decision B). The owner may change this.
41. **Plus Jakarta Sans (V16, default applied):** adopted for headings in the whole product, not only the landing page, so the system stays one set. The owner may revert to Inter-only by changing DS-10; the scale stays the same.
42. **`done_delta` meaning (V16, default applied):** switching `done_delta` from `updated_at` to `completed_at` changes the number a Supervisor or Manager sees for edits made after completion. The owner may keep the old definition for `done_delta` and use `completed_at` only for the throughput; THR-04 is the only criterion that changes.
43. **Pill-shaped nav on the marketing page (V16, default applied):** the landing header uses the same Top_Nav pill component as the app, with anchor links instead of routes.
44. **Filled fields and small controls (V16, default applied, accessibility risk):** the prototype draws form fields without a border (a tinted fill) and pill buttons 24 to 36 pixels high. V16 copies both because the owner asked for the same visual. A fill-only field has about 1.1 to 1 contrast against white, below the 3 to 1 that WCAG 2.1 asks for a control boundary; the focus ring, the label, and the fill are the cue. Controls below 44 pixels keep a 44 pixel hit area on small screens. The owner may switch to a bordered field by adding a `--border-input` token and changing the Input row of DS-18.
45. **Rail Home circle and announcement bar (V16, default applied):** the rail's first item is the teal Home circle to `/boards`, and a "Board" item appears while a Board is open; the landing page keeps the prototype's navy announcement bar with true copy. The owner may remove either.
46. **Old Board slug (RESOLVED by the owner):** not redirected; it returns 404 with the Not_Found_Page.
47. **Restored archived Card (RESOLVED by the owner):** goes to the first Column by position.
48. **Sidebar toggle (RESOLVED by the owner):** stored per browser; default expanded, labels shown.
49. **Dashboard date range (RESOLVED by the owner):** last 30 days by default.
50. **Due job (RESOLVED by the owner):** every 15 minutes, using `APP_TIMEZONE`.
51. **Scope of ConfirmDialog (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** the brief says "every create and every delete". FIX-07 lists the creates that open a dialog: Board, Column, Category, Card, user, Invite_Link, and Public_Link. A comment, a reply, a Subtask, an attachment, and a Join_Request are one-step actions with their own submit button, so a second dialog would only slow them (Requirement 25 saves them at once). Every delete, remove, disable, and regenerate opens a ConfirmDialog. The owner confirmed: no ConfirmDialog for creating a comment, a reply, a Subtask, an attachment, or a Join_Request.
52. **403 versus 404 for a slug (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V17):** the default stands: `GET /api/boards/slug/{slug}` returns 403 to a signed-in user without Board_Access, like the UUID route. The probing risk (a name can be tested) is accepted.
53. **`cards.board_id` (V17, applied by necessity):** a Card reaches its Board only through its Column. With `column_id` null (BC), an archived Card would lose its Board and the Archived view could not list it. V17 adds `cards.board_id` (not null, backfilled). The brief's migration 2 names only `column_id`; this column is added to it.
54. **Dashboard date range (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V17):** the Board and Member filters apply to every widget; the date range applies to Overdue, Finished on time, Finished overdue, and the throughput chart (by their dates); Overview and Top 5 priority are not limited by it; **Due tomorrow has its own "Due on" date filter**, default tomorrow, independent of the range (DASH-15).
55. **Who manages Columns (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in rounds 2 and 3):** the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin (COL-01). A Supervisor who is only a BoardMember cannot. A Board with a null `owner_id` is managed by a Manager or an Admin, so no Board is left without a manager.
56. **`deadline` notifications and the daily job (V17, default applied):** `due_today` replaces `deadline`. The daily job and its scheduler are removed; new `deadline` rows are no longer created, and old rows still display. The V12 card dedupe index covered every card type, so a second comment on the same day would have been refused; migration 4 narrows it to `assigned`, `deadline`, `due_today`, and `due_tomorrow`. Cron may not run in the unprivileged server container (Open Question 31); the deploy scripts fall back to a `supervisord` loop that runs the same command every 15 minutes.
57. **`completed_at` and archiving (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V17):** a Card in `Done` cannot be archived (COL-14, `CARD_IN_DONE`), so finished work stays visible in `Done` and throughput (THR-06) keeps counting it. A Card archived before V17 keeps its `completed_at`. Restoring treats the Card as entering the first Column: `completed_at` is cleared, or set to now when that Column is the Done_Column (a `Done` Column may be reordered to first).
58. **Status chip in the Card modal (V17, default applied):** V15 excluded a card `status` dropdown. The chip row of the new reference shows the Column as a read-only chip.
59. **An Admin cannot deactivate themselves (V17, default applied):** refused with HTTP 409 `CANNOT_DEACTIVATE_SELF`, so the last Admin cannot lock everyone out. Not in the brief.
60. **Deactivation and "no revocation list" (V17, default applied):** Requirement 1 says tokens are stateless with no revocation list. Deactivation takes effect at once because the Backend reads `is_active` from the user row on every authenticated request (the row is already loaded to check the role). No list of revoked tokens exists.
61. **Admin and Members (V17, default applied):** SHELL-21 sent an Admin's Team item to `/admin/users`. In V17 Members is a Top_Menu item shown while a Board is open, and an Admin has Board_Access (Requirement 22), so it opens the Members view like for everyone. The Users page is in the Sidebar.
62. **Toggles and the Update button (V17, default applied):** in the Users table a toggle or role change is staged in the row; the row's Update button opens a ConfirmDialog that lists each change and saves them in one `PATCH` on confirm. A user's own availability toggle on the Profile opens a ConfirmDialog at once.
63. **Screens without a reference image (V17, open):** Profile, Not_Found_Page, List, Calendar, Archived, My Tasks, and the Users table at 360 pixels have no image in `design/reference/v2/`. They use the Component_Recipes and are checked in the owner's visual review (`41-VERIFICATION.md`).
64. **Gmail SMTP limits (V17, open, risk):** Gmail needs 2-step verification and an app password, rewrites a `From` that is not the account, and limits a personal account to about 500 messages a day. This is enough for a demo. The transport is behind `mail.py`, so a service such as SES can replace it without a spec change.
65. **Old `/boards/{id}` links (V17, default applied):** permanent redirects cannot turn a UUID into a slug in the proxy. `/boards/{id}` redirects to `/app/boards/{id}`, a redirect-only route that reads the Board and redirects to `/app/b/{slug}` (Not_Found_Page when it does not exist).
66. **Slug normalisation (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V17):** the default stands: characters outside `a-z0-9` are removed, not folded ("Café Board" gives `caf-board`).
67. **Robots (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 2 within V17):** the default stands: `robots.txt` does not disallow `/app`, so the `noindex` header can be read; the sitemap lists `/` only.
68. **Unverified accounts (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** an account that never confirms its email is not purged; it keeps its username and email, and the owner can resend the link (ACT-04) or an Admin can review it. The risk of a reserved username or address is accepted.
69. **Destination through activation (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** `next` is kept through register, verification, and sign-in in the same browser (ACT-12), so an invite link still leads to its Join_Request.
70. **Inbox All and Unread switch (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** built as NAV-13, with the Backend filter `unread=true`.
71. **My Tasks and the Admin (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** an Admin does not see My Tasks (NAV-01, TASK-01).
72. **Two account flags (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** `email_verified_at` (activation) and `is_active` (Admin switch) stay separate, with `ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVATED` and `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` (ACT-01, ADM-07).
73. **Session cookie path (V17, RESOLVED by the owner in round 3):** `Path=/app` (PATH-06) is accepted; every existing session ends at the V17 deploy and users sign in again. `deploy/README.md` SHALL say so in the release notes.
74. **Old subtasks and attachments without a creator (V18, default applied):** rows created before V18 get `created_by_id` null. A Member cannot edit or delete them (only check a subtask); staff can. Backfilling from the activity log is possible but not exact, so it is not done.
75. **Description only (V18, default applied):** a Member's `PATCH /api/cards/{id}` may carry `description` only; title, priority, dates, category, and assignee are refused with 403. The Member still moves the Card through the move route.
76. **Comment editing (V18, default applied):** comments have no edit route today; V18 adds none. "Own comments" means delete.
77. **Admin and comments (V18, default applied):** Decision B stands; an Admin reads comments and never writes them.
78. **Page size of activity (V18, default applied):** the owner set 5 for comments; activity uses the same 5 so the two panes match.
79. **Board list sort default (V18, default applied):** `updated_at` descending (the most recently changed Board first).
80. **Toast and ConfirmDialog (V18, default applied):** an error raised by a ConfirmDialog action stays in the dialog's error area (FIX-07, COL-05) because the dialog is still open; a success closes the dialog and shows a toast.
81. **Back arrow parents (V18, default applied):** the table in NAVB-05. The Card modal has a close control, not a back arrow.

## Requirements

### Requirement 1: Authentication and Accounts

**User Story:** As a visitor, I want to register and sign in with an email or username and password, so that I can access boards according to my role.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a visitor submits a registration with full name, Username, Email, and password, THE Backend SHALL create a user account with a hashed password and return HTTP 201 with `{ "status": true, "message": "User registered", "data": { "user": { id, full_name, username, email, avatar_url }, "token": "..." } }` (Decisions G, M, and N; reworded in V10).  (V17: superseded by ACT-02, Requirement 59: register returns `token: null` until the email is verified.)
2. WHEN a registered user submits a valid `identifier` (a Username or an Email) and password to login, THE Backend SHALL return the same `user` and `token` shape as registration, matching the identifier case-insensitively (Decisions M and N; reworded in V10).  (V17: superseded by ACT-01 and ADM-07: login also refuses an unverified or inactive account.)
3. IF the `identifier` matches no account or the password is wrong, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 with the same error Envelope and the same message in both cases (Decision N; reworded in V10).
4. IF a registration uses a Username or an Email that already exists when compared case-insensitively, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `CONFLICT` and an `error.details` object that names each duplicate field (`username`, `email`, or both) with a list of message strings (Decisions M and O; reworded in V10).
5. IF a registration omits full name, Username, Email, or password, or a field fails the format rules of criteria 47 and 48, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for each failing field (Decision M; reworded in V10).
6. THE Backend SHALL store the user's Avatar_Image reference as a nullable `avatar_path` and SHALL expose it as `avatar_url` in the `user` payload and in the payloads of criterion 34 of Requirement 19 (Decisions R and U; reworded in V10 rounds 2 and 3).
7. WHEN an Admin creates, edits, or assigns a role to a user account, THE Backend SHALL apply the change and record it in the Activity_Log.
8. IF an authenticated user who is not an Admin requests user account management, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present.
9. THE Frontend SHALL present the login and register pages as a split-panel layout with a brand panel beside an elevated rounded form card on desktop widths.
10. WHILE the viewport is at mobile width, THE Frontend SHALL hide the brand panel on the login and register pages and show only the form.
11. THE System SHALL exclude Google OAuth login until Username-or-Email and password authentication is verified end-to-end.
12. IF a registration password is shorter than 8 characters, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
13. THE Backend SHALL hash passwords with bcrypt or argon2.
14. WHEN the Backend issues a Token, THE Backend SHALL set a fixed expiry of 24 hours and keep no server-side revocation list.
15. WHEN a user calls `POST /api/auth/logout`, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 200 with `{ "status": true, "message": "Logged out", "data": null }` (Decision G).
16. WHEN a user logs out, THE Frontend SHALL discard the stored Token, and the issued Token SHALL remain valid until its expiry.
17. [Withdrawn in V10, Decision M] The provisional "no format validation" rule for `login_identifier` no longer applies; criteria 47 and 48 define the formats.
18. [Superseded in V10, Decision O] Rate limiting is no longer deferred; Requirement 20 defines it.

The criteria below specify the Playwright tests of the E2E_Auth_Suite (V9, updated in V10 to the new login form, the header sign-out, the route guard, and the rate limit). They add no product behaviour: each one verifies behaviour specified in criteria 1.2, 1.3, 1.5, 1.9, 1.10, 1.15, 1.16, 1.44 to 1.46, 1.51, Requirements 13, 19, and 20, and Decisions L, N, O, and P. No criterion is pending in V10. Assumptions: the Test_Account exists and is a Member; the specs run against the deployed Frontend and Backend; the login form posts through `lib/api.ts`; each test starts in a fresh browser context with no Session_Cookie; the suite shows "signed in" by the User_Menu_Trigger. Each criterion has a stable ID, and the matching test title begins with that ID.

**Sign-in, happy cases**

19. [AUTH-E2E-SI-01] WHEN a visitor submits the Test_Account's Username or Email and password on `/login`, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to `/boards` and show the User_Menu_Trigger.
20. [AUTH-E2E-SI-02] WHEN a visitor submits the Test_Account's Username or Email with every letter upper-cased and the correct password, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to `/boards` and show the User_Menu_Trigger (criterion 2, Decision M).
21. [AUTH-E2E-SI-03] WHILE a Test_Account is signed in, WHEN the visitor reloads `/boards`, THE Frontend SHALL stay on `/boards` and show the User_Menu_Trigger.
22. [AUTH-E2E-SI-04] WHEN a Test_Account signs in successfully, THE Frontend SHALL set the Session_Cookie.
23. [AUTH-E2E-SI-05] WHILE the viewport is 360 pixels wide, WHEN a visitor opens `/login`, THE Frontend SHALL show the login form with the single "Username or email" field and hide the brand panel (criterion 10).

**Sign-in, negative cases**

24. [AUTH-E2E-SI-06] IF a visitor submits the Test_Account's Username or Email with a wrong password, THEN THE Frontend SHALL stay on `/login`, show the Generic_Login_Error, and set no Session_Cookie (criterion 3).
25. [AUTH-E2E-SI-07] IF a visitor submits a Username-shaped Identifier that belongs to no account (a Throwaway_Identifier), THEN THE Frontend SHALL stay on `/login`, show the same Generic_Login_Error text as criterion 24, and set no Session_Cookie.
26. [AUTH-E2E-SI-08] IF a visitor submits the login form with an empty "Username or email" field and an empty password, THEN THE Frontend SHALL stay on `/login`, show a validation message beside each of the two fields, send no request, and set no Session_Cookie (criterion 5).
27. [AUTH-E2E-SI-09] IF a visitor submits an Identifier of 256 characters without `@`, THEN THE Frontend SHALL stay on `/login`, show a username-shape validation message beside the "Username or email" field, send no request, and set no Session_Cookie (criterion 51).
28. [AUTH-E2E-SI-10] WHEN a visitor submits the login form, THE Frontend SHALL keep the password out of the page URL and out of the visible page text, and SHALL render the password field with `type="password"`.

**Sign-out, happy cases**

29. [AUTH-E2E-SO-01] WHEN a signed-in Test_Account opens the User_Menu and activates the Sign_Out_Control, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to `/login` (criterion 16).
30. [AUTH-E2E-SO-02] WHEN a signed-in Test_Account opens the User_Menu and activates the Sign_Out_Control, THE Frontend SHALL remove the Session_Cookie and send `POST /api/auth/logout` once (criteria 15 and 16).
31. [AUTH-E2E-SO-03] WHEN a Test_Account has signed out and the visitor opens a Protected_Route, THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/login` (Decision P). (V12: `/invite/{token}` is a Protected_Route too.)
32. [AUTH-E2E-SO-04] WHEN a Test_Account has signed out and the visitor navigates back in the browser history, THE Frontend SHALL end on `/login` and show no User_Menu_Trigger and no signed-in user name (Decision P).

**Sign-out and session, negative cases**

33. [AUTH-E2E-SO-05] IF a visitor without a Session_Cookie opens a Protected_Route, THEN THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/login` before the page renders (Decision P). (V12: `/invite/{token}` is a Protected_Route too; the redirect carries the Safe_Return_Path.)
34. [AUTH-E2E-SO-06] IF a request made by a signed-in Frontend returns HTTP 401 for an expired or invalid Token, THEN THE Frontend SHALL remove the Session_Cookie and navigate to `/login` (design: `lib/api.ts`; server renders: criteria 57 and 58, Decision T).
35. [AUTH-E2E-SO-07] IF `POST /api/auth/logout` fails with a network error or HTTP 500, THEN THE Frontend SHALL still remove the Session_Cookie and navigate to `/login` (criterion 16).
36. [AUTH-E2E-SO-08] IF `POST /api/auth/logout` returns HTTP 401 because the Token has expired, THEN THE Frontend SHALL remove the Session_Cookie and end on `/login` without a redirect loop.

**Traceability**

37. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL give every test a title that begins with the stable ID of the criterion it verifies, and SHALL contain exactly one test per ID in criteria 19 to 36, 39 to 42, 55, and 56. (V12: criteria 65 to 71 add tests; see criterion 72.)
38. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL contain no test for registration, role permissions, or Google OAuth.

**V10 additions to the suite**

39. [AUTH-E2E-SI-11] IF a visitor submits a Throwaway_Identifier with a wrong password until the Rate_Limiter blocks the pair, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show the wait time, disable the submit button, send no further login request while the button is disabled, and set no Session_Cookie (Requirement 20, Decision O). The test makes at most 10 attempts (Requirement 15, criterion 19) and never uses the Test_Account.
40. [AUTH-E2E-SI-12] IF a visitor submits an Identifier that contains `@` but is not a valid Email, THEN THE Frontend SHALL stay on `/login`, show an email-shape validation message beside the "Username or email" field, send no request, and set no Session_Cookie (criterion 51).
41. [AUTH-E2E-SO-09] WHEN a signed-in Test_Account opens the User_Menu, THE Frontend SHALL list the Profile item above the Sign_Out_Control (criterion 2 of Requirement 19, Decision L).
42. [AUTH-E2E-SO-10] WHILE a Test_Account is signed in, THE Frontend SHALL show no Log out item and no Profile entry in the Sidebar (Decision L).

**Account identity (V10)**

43. [USR-01] THE Backend SHALL store a User `username` and a User `email`, each unique when compared case-insensitively through a unique index on the lower-cased value, and SHALL have no `login_identifier` column after the V10 migration (Decision M).
44. [USR-02] WHEN a login request carries an `identifier` that contains `@` and has a valid Email shape, THE Backend SHALL look the user up by `email`, case-insensitively (Decision N).
45. [USR-03] WHEN a login request carries an `identifier` without `@` that matches the Username format, THE Backend SHALL look the user up by `username`, case-insensitively (Decision N).
46. [USR-04] IF a login `identifier` is empty, contains `@` without a valid Email shape, or has no `@` and does not match the Username format, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `identifier`, count no attempt in the Rate_Limiter, and look up no user (Decision N).
47. [USR-05] WHEN the Backend receives a Username, THE Backend SHALL trim it, lower-case it, and accept it only if it has 3 to 30 characters, each one of `a-z`, `0-9`, `_`, or `.`; otherwise THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `username` (Decision M).
48. [USR-06] WHEN the Backend receives an Email, THE Backend SHALL trim it, lower-case it, and accept it only if it is at most 255 characters and has a valid Email shape (one `@`, a non-empty local part, a domain with at least one dot, no spaces; no DNS lookup); otherwise THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `email` (Decision M).
49. [USR-07] WHEN a visitor calls `GET /api/auth/availability` with `username`, `email`, or both, THE Backend SHALL return `{ "username": { "available": boolean }, "email": { "available": boolean } }` with an entry for each supplied parameter, and SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` when neither is supplied or a supplied value fails criterion 47 or 48 (Decision M).
50. [USR-08] WHEN a visitor leaves the Username or Email field of the register form, THE Frontend SHALL call the availability endpoint and show the result beside that field; WHEN the Backend answers 409 on submit, THE Frontend SHALL show each `error.details` message beside its field, and the 409 SHALL stay authoritative over an earlier "available" result (Decision M).
51. [USR-09] THE Frontend SHALL show the login form as one "Username or email" field and one password field; WHEN the value contains `@`, THE Frontend SHALL validate the Email shape, and otherwise THE Frontend SHALL validate the Username shape, and SHALL send a request only when the value is valid (Decision N).
52. [USR-10] [Withdrawn in V10 round 3, Decision V] The legacy `login_identifier` backfill and the sanitized-Username rule no longer apply, because the application is not deployed and the migration resets local development data (criterion 63). The ID stays so that references do not move.
53. [USR-11] [Withdrawn in V10 round 3, Decision V] No account has a null Email, so the Frontend shows no Email_Prompt. The ID stays so that references do not move.
54. [USR-12] WHEN an Admin creates or edits a user account through `POST /api/users` or `PATCH /api/users/{id}`, THE Backend SHALL accept and validate `username` and `email` by criteria 47 and 48, apply the uniqueness rule of criterion 4, and return the `user` shape of criterion 1 (Decision M).

**Invalid or expired Token on any request (V10 round 2, Decision T)**

55. [AUTH-E2E-SO-11] IF a visitor whose Session_Cookie holds a value that is not a decodable Token (for example the text `bogus`) opens `/boards`, THEN THE Frontend SHALL end on the login page and remove the Session_Cookie (criterion 57).
56. [AUTH-E2E-SO-12] IF a visitor whose Session_Cookie holds a well-formed Token with a future expiry and an invalid signature opens `/boards`, THEN THE Frontend SHALL end on the login page, remove the Session_Cookie, and make no further request to a Protected_Route after the redirect (criterion 58).
57. [TOK-01] WHEN a request for a Protected_Route carries a Session_Cookie whose value is not three dot-separated base64url segments, whose payload is not a JSON object with a string `sub`, a string `role`, and a numeric `exp`, or whose `exp` is not later than the current time, THE Frontend SHALL answer through `frontend/proxy.ts` with a redirect to `/login` that carries the Safe_Return_Path as `next` and SHALL remove the Session_Cookie in that same response; the proxy SHALL NOT verify the signature, because only the Backend holds the signing secret.
58. [TOK-02] IF the Backend answers a server render of a Protected_Route with HTTP 401 while a Session_Cookie is present, THEN THE Frontend SHALL redirect to the route handler `/session-expired` with the Safe_Return_Path, and that handler SHALL remove the Session_Cookie and redirect to `/login` with the same `next`; a server render that gets HTTP 403 SHALL NOT remove the Session_Cookie.
59. [TOK-03] WHEN login succeeds, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to the Safe_Return_Path given in `next`, and to `/boards` when `next` is absent or is not a Safe_Return_Path. (V12: a Safe_Return_Path may also start with `/invite/`.)
60. [TOK-04] WHILE the visitor is on `/login`, `/register`, or `/shared/{token}`, THE Frontend SHALL ignore a stale Session_Cookie: no redirect and no cookie removal on arrival (Requirement 18, criterion 24).
61. [TOK-05] THE route handler `/session-expired` SHALL redirect only to `/login`, and `/login` SHALL NOT redirect to a Protected_Route without a user action, so that an invalid Token cannot cause a redirect loop.
62. [TOK-06] WHERE criteria 31, 33, 34, 55, and 56 say the visitor ends on `/login`, THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL compare the URL path only, so that a `next` query parameter does not fail the test.
63. [USR-13] WHEN the V10 migration runs and `APP_ENV` is `development` or `testing`, THE Backend SHALL delete every row of `users` and of each table that references it (Boards, BoardMembers, Cards with their Subtasks and Attachments, Comments, Notifications, Activity_Log rows, and rate-limit rows), add `username` and `email` as NOT NULL columns with the unique lower-case indexes of criterion 43, add `boards.share_token` and `rate_limit_attempts`, replace `avatar_url` with `avatar_path`, drop `login_identifier`, and print one line saying that local data was reset and that `flask seed-users` or `flask seed-data` recreates it, without backfilling any legacy value (Decision V); IF `APP_ENV` has any other value or is unset, THEN THE migration SHALL abort before it changes anything, with a message that names `APP_ENV`. Uploaded files under `UPLOAD_DIR` are not touched. (V14, amended by Decision AN: outside development and testing the migration may run on an empty `users` table and never truncates; Requirement 37.)
64. [USR-14] WHEN the seed commands `flask seed-users` and `flask seed-data` create a user, THE Backend SHALL set `username`, `email`, and `full_name` on it for every seeded user: the Member, Supervisor, Manager, and Admin of `seed-users`, and `member2` and `supervisor2` of `seed-data`, using the Username `<key>`, the Email `<key>@example.com`, and a two-word full name such as "Member One"; the Member `member` is the Test_Account of the E2E_Auth_Suite (`E2E_USER_IDENTIFIER=member`). Running a seed command twice SHALL create no duplicate (Decision V). (V12: `flask seed-data` also seeds the Boards, Board_Members, and Card of Requirement 22, criterion 18.)

**V12 additions to the suite**
65. [AUTH-E2E-V12-01] IF a visitor without a Session_Cookie opens `/invite/e2e-unknown-token`, THEN THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/login` and carry `/invite/e2e-unknown-token` in the `next` query parameter (Requirement 24, criterion 22; Decision P as amended in V12).
66. [AUTH-E2E-V12-02] WHEN a visitor arrives on `/login?next=/invite/e2e-unknown-token` and signs in as the Test_Account, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to `/invite/e2e-unknown-token` and show the message that the invite link is not valid (Requirement 24, criteria 22 and 25; this uses an unknown token, so the suite creates no data).
67. [AUTH-E2E-V12-03] WHEN any visitor opens `/shared/e2e-unknown-token`, THE Frontend SHALL show the page that says the link is not valid and offers a Sign in link (Requirement 18, criterion 23, Public_Link of Requirement 23).
68. [AUTH-E2E-V12-04] WHILE a Test_Account is signed in, THE Frontend SHALL show on `/boards` no lock icon and no element whose accessible name contains "private" or "public" (Requirement 22, criterion 14).
69. [AUTH-E2E-V12-05] WHILE a Test_Account (a Member who is a BoardMember of a seeded Board) is on that Board's page, THE Frontend SHALL show no "Invite" button, no "Public link" button, and no add-member button in the Sidebar Team members heading (Requirement 22, criterion 16; Requirement 23, criterion 9; Requirement 24, criterion 19).
70. [AUTH-E2E-V12-06] WHILE a Test_Account has a Card modal open on a seeded Board, THE Frontend SHALL show no button named "Save changes" and, because a Member may not archive or delete a Card, neither Archive nor Delete (Requirement 25, criterion 1; Requirement 4, criterion 7).
71. [AUTH-E2E-V12-07] WHILE the viewport is 1280 pixels wide, THE Frontend SHALL show the Profile_Page avatar card and change-password card side by side (the change-password card's left edge is right of the avatar card's right edge and their tops are within 8 pixels); WHILE the viewport is 360 pixels wide, THE Frontend SHALL stack them (Requirement 29, criteria 1 and 2).
72. [AUTH-E2E-V12-08] THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL give each test of criteria 65 to 71 a title that begins with its ID, SHALL contain exactly one test per ID, SHALL create no data and change no account (criterion 15), and SHALL treat criteria 69 and 70 as skipped with a named reason when the Test_Account's Home lists no Board, because `flask seed-data` makes the Test_Account `member` a BoardMember of a seeded Board with a Card (Requirement 22, criterion 18).

### Requirement 2: Roles and Permissions

**User Story:** As a system owner, I want strictly layered roles enforced on the server, so that each user can reach only the actions their role allows.

Note (Decision B): Admin is a separate administration track and is deliberately not a superset of Manager. The Admin exclusions in criteria 5 and in Requirement 10 criterion 4 are intentional and must not be changed as a "fix".

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. THE Backend SHALL support exactly five roles: guest, member, supervisor, manager, and admin.
2. THE Backend SHALL treat Manager permissions as a superset of Supervisor permissions, Supervisor permissions as a superset of Member permissions, and Member view access as a superset of Guest view access.
3. THE Backend SHALL treat Admin as a separate administration track with the permissions listed in criteria 4 to 6.
4. THE Backend SHALL permit an Admin to view all Boards, Cards, Comments, and Activity_Log entries, manage user accounts, create and assign Boards, archive and Soft_Delete Boards, set Board visibility, manage Categories, and move and edit any Card (Decision 5). (V12: "set Board visibility" no longer exists; Decision Y.)
5. THE Backend SHALL deny an Admin the actions of creating a Comment and flagging a Comment as priority (Decision B).
6. THE Backend SHALL permit an Admin to create Cards, delete Cards, archive Cards, and create Columns.  (V17: superseded by COL-01 for creating Columns, Requirement 49.)
7. WHEN any mutating endpoint is called, THE Backend SHALL determine the acting role from the Token and ignore any role value in the request body.
8. IF a mutating request has no valid Token, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 with an error Envelope.
9. IF an authenticated user calls a mutating endpoint that the user's role or Board_Access does not permit, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 with an error Envelope.
10. THE Backend SHALL re-validate the caller's role on 100 percent of mutating endpoints.

### Requirement 3: Boards and Columns

**User Story:** As a Manager or Admin, I want to create boards and assign them to supervisors, so that each project has an owner and a consistent workflow.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a Manager or Admin creates a Board, THE Backend SHALL insert the Board and the five Default_Columns with `order` values 0 to 4 in that sequence, in a single transaction.
2. WHEN a Manager or Admin assigns a Board to a Supervisor, THE Backend SHALL store that Supervisor's id in the Board's nullable `owner_id` (Decision 1).
3. IF an authenticated Member or Supervisor attempts to create a Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present.
4. WHEN a Manager or Admin, or a Supervisor who is the Board_Owner or a BoardMember of the Board, creates a Column on that Board, THE Backend SHALL append the Column with the next `order` value (Decision A).  (V17: superseded by COL-01, Requirement 49: only the Board_Owner manages Columns.)
5. IF an authenticated Member, or a Supervisor without Board_Access as owner or BoardMember, attempts to create a Column, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision A).  (V17: superseded by COL-01, Requirement 49.)
6. WHEN a Manager or Admin soft-deletes a Board, THE Backend SHALL set the Board's `deleted_at` and exclude the Board from default queries (Decision 4).
7. IF an authenticated Member or Supervisor attempts to soft-delete a Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision 4).
8. THE Backend SHALL never hard-delete a row from the Board table.
9. THE Frontend SHALL list the Boards the viewer may see in the Sidebar and use that list as the Board switcher. (V12: the list is the Boards where the viewer is owner or Board_Member, and every Board for a Manager and an Admin; Requirement 22, criterion 4.) (V16: superseded by Requirement 39, SHELL-12 to SHELL-15: the Board_Switcher replaces the Sidebar Boards list.)
10. WHILE a Board is open, THE Frontend SHALL render the Board's Columns and Cards in the main area.
11. WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin adds a user to a Board through `POST /api/boards/{id}/members`, THE Backend SHALL create a BoardMember for that Board and user (Decision A). (V12, Decision Y: only a Manager or an Admin adds directly, and only Member and Supervisor accounts; the Board_Owner adds people through Join_Requests; Requirement 22, criteria 5, 7, and 8, and Requirement 24.)
12. IF an authenticated user who is not the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin attempts to add or remove a BoardMember, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision A). (V12: adding is for a Manager or an Admin only; the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin may still remove, Requirement 22, criterion 10.)
13. IF a BoardMember add targets a user who is already a BoardMember of that Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with an error Envelope (Decision A).
14. WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin removes a BoardMember through `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/members/{user_id}`, THE Backend SHALL delete that BoardMember record (Decision A). (V12: the removal also clears the removed user's assignments on the Board, Requirement 22, criterion 10.)
15. WHEN a BoardMember is added or removed, THE Backend SHALL write one Activity_Log row for the change in the same transaction (Decision A).
16. WHEN a Manager or Admin reassigns a Board's `owner_id` to a user who has the supervisor role, THE Backend SHALL store the new `owner_id` (Decision 1). (V12: also notifies the new owner and clears the previous owner's assignments, Requirement 22, criterion 12.)
17. IF a Board `owner_id` reassignment names a user who does not have the supervisor role, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
18. WHEN a Manager or Admin clears a Board's `owner_id`, THE Backend SHALL set `owner_id` to null.
19. WHEN a Board's `owner_id` is reassigned or cleared, THE Backend SHALL write one Activity_Log row in the same transaction.
20. THE Frontend SHALL show each Board's visibility in the Sidebar Boards list and in the Board header as an icon with an accessible label and tooltip: a closed lock for `private` and an open lock for `public` (Decision I). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22, criterion 14: no visibility icon exists; Home lists the Boards of Requirement 22, criterion 4.)**
21. THE Frontend SHALL wrap a long Board name onto further lines in the Sidebar and in the Board header instead of cutting it off, and SHALL show the full name as a tooltip in the Sidebar (Decision I). (V16: the name wraps in the Board_Switcher and the Board_Toolbar, Requirement 39, SHELL-14 and SHELL-18.)
22. WHEN a signed-in user opens Home, THE Frontend SHALL show a page at `/boards` that lists every Board the viewer may see, each with its visibility icon, and SHALL make Home the first Sidebar entry and the default destination after sign-in (Decision I). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22, criterion 14: no visibility icon exists; Home lists the Boards of Requirement 22, criterion 4.)** (V16: the Home circle of the Icon_Rail and the Boards item of the Top_Nav, Requirement 39, SHELL-01 and SHELL-03.)
23. THE Frontend SHALL show the Board header in two rows: the first is the App_Header (Requirement 19) and holds the visibility icon and Board name and, for the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin, the Share button (Requirement 18) on the left, and the Inbox bell and the User_Menu trigger on the right (reworded in V10); the second holds three bordered sections, progress ("12 of 18 done", the percent, and a thin bar) on the left, the Category filter chips in the middle as one strip that scrolls sideways with left and right arrow buttons shown only when more chips are hidden, and the search field on the right; the header SHALL have no Add Member button and no settings icon, and below 960 pixels the search field SHALL collapse to an icon that opens the search in a modal (Decision I). **(Amended in V12: the visibility icon is removed; the "Share" button is the "Public link" button for a Manager and an Admin only (Requirement 23, criterion 9); the "Invite" button is added for the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin (Requirement 24, criterion 19).)** (V16: superseded by Requirement 39, SHELL-09, SHELL-12, and SHELL-17: the page context moves to the Board_Toolbar.)
24. THE Frontend SHALL keep one Team members section in the Sidebar, listing the Board's members, each with an Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar (Decision U), with no separate Members navigation entry; WHERE the viewer is the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin, the section heading SHALL carry an add button that opens a member search (a popover, or a modal when the viewport is too narrow) and each member SHALL carry a remove button that asks for confirmation (Decision I). **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 22, criterion 16: the add button is shown to a Manager and an Admin only. Amended in V12 round 2 by Requirement 22, criteria 19 and 20: the list starts with the Board_Owner, tagged "Owner", and the owner row carries no remove button.)** (V16: superseded by Requirement 39, SHELL-19 to SHELL-21: the Team_View of the Side_Panel.)
25. WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin requests `GET /api/boards/{id}/candidates?search=<text>` with a `search` of at least 2 characters after trimming, THE Backend SHALL return at most 20 users who are not yet BoardMembers of that Board, matched case-insensitively on `full_name`, on `username` by prefix, or on `email` by exact match only (never by prefix or substring), and ordered by name, each with `id`, `full_name`, `username`, `email`, and `avatar_url` (Decisions M, I, S, U, and W; reworded in V10 and again in rounds 2 and 3); IF `search` is missing or has fewer than 2 characters after trimming, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `search` and SHALL query no users; IF another signed-in user calls it, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, and with no Token HTTP 401 (Decision I). **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 22, criteria 8 and 9: Manager and Admin only, Member and Supervisor accounts only.)**
26. WHEN a user requests `GET /api/boards`, THE Backend SHALL include on each Board `card_count` (Cards not soft-deleted and not archived), `done_count` (of those, Cards in the `Done` Column), `overdue_count` (of those, Overdue_Cards), and `member_count` (BoardMembers), computed in a fixed number of queries whatever the number of Boards (Decision I).
27. THE Backend SHALL store a nullable Board `description` of at most 500 characters, accepted on `POST /api/boards` and `PATCH /api/boards/{id}` under the same role rules as the Board name, returned on every Board response, and SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` for a longer value (Decision I).
28. THE Frontend SHALL offer an optional Description field in the new-board modal, sending it only when it is not empty (Decision I).
29. THE Frontend SHALL show each Board on Home as a plain bordered tile (no cover band or image) with the visibility icon and name, up to two lines of the description, a progress bar with the percent from `done_count` and `card_count`, the counts of Cards, Overdue_Cards, and members, and up to three member avatars followed by "+N" (Decision I). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22, criterion 14: no visibility icon exists; Home lists the Boards of Requirement 22, criterion 4.)**
30. [EML-01] WHEN a signed-in user requests `GET /api/boards/{id}/members` without `include`, THE Backend SHALL return each member with `id`, `full_name`, `username`, and `avatar_url` and no `email`; WHEN the request carries `include=email` and the viewer has Board_Staff_Access, THE Backend SHALL add `email` to each member; IF the request carries `include=email` and the viewer lacks Board_Staff_Access, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, and with no Token HTTP 401 (Decision S). (V12: the response also carries the Board_Owner first and `is_owner`, Requirement 22, criterion 13.)
31. [EML-02] THE Frontend SHALL show a user's Email only in the candidate results of the member search (Board_Owner, Manager, Admin), in the assignee popover (Board_Staff_Access), and, from V12 round 2, in the pending join-request list of the Invite modal (Board_Owner, Manager, Admin; Requirement 24, criterion 13), and THE Backend SHALL return another user's Email in no other Board-scoped payload, including Comments, Activity_Log rows, Notifications, the Shared_View, the Sidebar member rows, and any list a Member can read (Decision S). A user's own Email in the `user` shape and the Admin user list are unchanged. (V12: the member search is for a Manager and an Admin only.)
32. [EML-03] WHEN the member search shows a candidate, THE Frontend SHALL show the Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar, the full name and, beneath it in muted text, the Username and the Email, SHALL keep matching on full name, Username prefix, and exact Email, and SHALL send no search request until the trimmed text has 2 characters, showing the hint "Type at least 2 characters." meanwhile (Decisions S, U, and W).

### Requirement 4: Cards

**User Story:** As a team member, I want cards with description, subtasks, priority, dates, and attachments, so that I can capture and track a piece of work in one place.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a Card_Creator with Board_Staff_Access to a Board creates a Card in a Column of that Board, THE Backend SHALL store the Card with a title, a description, a Priority of `high`, `medium`, or `low`, a Float `order`, and nullable `start_date`, `end_date`, `category_id`, and `assignee_id` (Decision A).
2. IF a Card is submitted with a Priority other than `high`, `medium`, or `low`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
3. IF a Card is submitted with an `end_date` earlier than its `start_date`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
4. IF an authenticated user who is not a Card_Creator with Board_Staff_Access attempts to create a Card, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision A).
5. WHEN a Card_Creator with Board_Staff_Access deletes a Card, THE Backend SHALL Soft_Delete the Card by setting `deleted_at`.
6. WHEN a Card_Creator with Board_Staff_Access archives a Card, THE Backend SHALL set the Card's `archived_at` and allow the archive to be reversed.
7. IF an authenticated Member attempts to create, delete, or archive a Card, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present.
8. WHEN a Member with Board_Access edits a Card assigned to that Member, THE Backend SHALL apply the edit (Decisions 2 and A).
9. IF a Member edits a Card whose `assignee_id` is not that Member, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 (Decision 2).
10. WHEN a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin with Board_Staff_Access to the Card's Board edits any Card on that Board, THE Backend SHALL apply the edit (Decision A).
11. WHEN a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin with Board_Staff_Access assigns a Card to a user, THE Backend SHALL set the Card's `assignee_id` and create an `assigned` Notification for that user in the same transaction. (V12: the assignee must be the Board_Owner or a Board_Member, Requirement 22, criterion 11.)
12. WHEN a user with edit rights on a Card (Board_Staff_Access for a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin; Board_Access as the Assigned_Member for a Member) adds a Subtask to that Card, THE Backend SHALL store the Subtask with a title, `is_done` defaulting to false, and a Float `order`. (V12: unchanged, and stated as the matrix of Requirement 27, criterion 1.)
13. WHEN a user with edit rights on a Card toggles a Subtask's `is_done` or reorders it, THE Backend SHALL persist the change through `PATCH /api/subtasks/{id}`, setting a Subtask dropped between two siblings to the average of their `order` values, the first position to `min_order - 1`, the last position to `max_order + 1`, an empty checklist's first Subtask to 1.0, and renumbering the whole checklist as 1.0, 2.0, ... in the same transaction when the gap between adjacent `order` values falls below 1e-6. (V12: unchanged, and stated as the matrix of Requirement 27, criterion 1.)
14. WHEN a user with edit rights on a Card (Board_Staff_Access for a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin; Board_Access as the Assigned_Member for a Member) deletes a Subtask, THE Backend SHALL remove the Subtask through `DELETE /api/subtasks/{id}`. (V12: unchanged, and stated as the matrix of Requirement 27, criterion 1.)
15. WHEN a user with edit rights on a Card (Board_Staff_Access for a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin; Board_Access as the Assigned_Member for a Member) adds an Attachment, THE Backend SHALL store either a `url` or a `file_path` with `mime_type`.
16. IF an Attachment has neither a `url` nor a `file_path`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
17. IF an uploaded Attachment file type is not PDF, Word, or RAR, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
18. THE Backend SHALL serve each uploaded Attachment file through an authenticated endpoint or a Signed_File_URL that enforces the same Board_Access and role rules as the owning Card, and SHALL NOT expose a permanently public, unauthenticated URL (Decision D).
19. WHEN a user clicks an Attachment, THE Frontend SHALL open it with `target="_blank"` and `rel="noopener noreferrer"` and provide no in-app preview, and for a file Attachment whose Signed_File_URL has expired, THE Frontend SHALL request a fresh URL from `GET /api/attachments/{id}/url` before opening it (Decision D).
20. WHEN a user opens a Card, THE Frontend SHALL show one large two-pane modal, sized to the viewport minus a margin, used for both viewing and editing: the left pane holds three cards, Details (title, attribute chips, description), Attachments, and Subtasks, and the right pane holds two cards, Comments (the composer and the thread) and, below it, Activity (the Card's Activity_Log); each pane scrolls vertically on its own, whatever its content height, between a fixed header and a fixed footer (Decision I).
21. WHEN a user clicks Save changes in the modal footer, THE Frontend SHALL commit every pending change of the left pane, the Card fields in one `PATCH /api/cards/{id}` call followed by the staged Attachment additions and removals, without closing the modal and without affecting the Comment composer (Decision I). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 25, criteria 1 to 6: there is no Save changes button; every change saves by itself.)**
22. THE Frontend SHALL render the Subtask checklist as a flat list of checkboxes with inline-editable titles and a drag handle for reordering.
23. THE Backend SHALL never hard-delete a row from the Card table.
24. THE Backend SHALL set each Signed_File_URL to expire 15 minutes after issue (Decision D).
25. IF a request to the file route carries a bad, modified, or expired file signature, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, because the file route takes no Token (Decision D).
26. WHEN a signed-in viewer with Board_Access to the Card requests `GET /api/attachments/{id}/url`, THE Backend SHALL return a fresh Signed_File_URL; a Guest obtains one only through the shared route of Requirement 18 (Decisions D and K; reworded in V10).
27. THE Backend SHALL return a link-type Attachment's `url` as stored (Decision D).
28. WHEN a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin with Board_Staff_Access sets a Card's `assignee_id` to null, THE Backend SHALL clear the assignment, write one `card.unassigned` Activity_Log row in the same transaction, and create no Notification.
29. THE Backend SHALL carry no `deleted_at` on Subtask, Attachment, or Comment rows and SHALL hide them by joining the parent Card's `deleted_at`.
30. THE Frontend SHALL show a Card's category, Priority, dates, and assignee in the Card modal as chips (a coloured label pill for the category, a flag chip for the Priority, one chip for the date range with a red Overdue state, and an avatar with an add button for the assignee), each opening a popover that edits a draft, and SHALL send nothing until the user clicks Save changes (Decision I). **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 25: each chip popover saves its own field when its value is confirmed or closed; there is no draft.)**
31. THE Frontend SHALL edit the start and end dates in one popover with a single range calendar and two optional fields, Start date and End date, and SHALL highlight every day between the two dates as one block when both are set (Decision I).
32. THE Frontend SHALL NOT let a start date later than the end date be chosen: the calendar disables days before the start while the end date is picked, a typed pair with the start after the end shows an inline error and disables Save changes, moving the start past an existing end date clears the end date and shows a hint, and the same day for both is allowed (Decision I; the Backend rule is criterion 3). **(Amended in V12: there is no Save changes button to disable; an invalid pair sends nothing and shows the inline error, Requirement 25, criterion 5.)**
33. THE Frontend SHALL pick the assignee in a small popover with a search box, the Board's members (Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar, full name, and Email, loaded through `GET /api/boards/{id}/members?include=email`), a single choice, and a Remove action, with the search box matching full name, Username, and Email case-insensitively, and SHALL hide the control from a Member, who may not assign (Decisions I and S; the Backend rule is criterion 11). (V12: the picker lists the Board_Owner first, tagged "Owner", and then the Board_Members, Requirement 22, criteria 13 and 19, and saves when a choice is made, Requirement 25, criterion 4.)
34. THE Frontend SHALL offer no column select in the Card modal; a Card changes Column only by drag-and-drop (Requirement 5) (Decision I).
35. THE Frontend SHALL place every action of the Card modal in its footer, aligned to the right: Archive (secondary), Delete (danger), then Save changes (primary, last), SHALL ask for confirmation in a small confirmation popover before Delete takes effect, and SHALL offer no Close button in the footer: the modal closes through the header close icon and Escape (Decision I). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 25, criterion 1: the footer holds Archive and Delete only.)**
36. THE Frontend SHALL show the Attachments card in two columns: the existing Attachments on the left and, on the right, one add control with a selector for the type, Link or File, that shows only the input of the chosen type; below 960 pixels the two columns stack; each Attachment is a bordered tile with a type icon (a document icon for PDF and Word, an archive icon for RAR, a link icon for a link), the file name in a small font, and a small remove icon (Decision I).
37. THE Frontend SHALL show the Subtask checklist in its own card, apart from the Attachments card (Decision I).
38. THE Frontend SHALL let each pane of the Card modal scroll vertically whenever its content is taller than the space between the modal header and footer (Decision I).
39. THE Frontend SHALL show in the Card modal header the Card's title and the Board's name separated by a vertical bar ("Card title | Board name"), SHALL update the title part as the user edits the title field before saving, SHALL show "Untitled card" when the title is empty, and SHALL show the Column's name as a badge beside it (Decision I). (V12: the title part follows the field as it is typed; saving happens on blur or Enter, Requirement 25, criterion 2.)
40. THE Frontend SHALL give the Card modal the control, type, and spacing scale of Requirement 14 criterion 20, so that inputs, selects, buttons, and chips in one row have the same height (Decision I).
41. THE Frontend SHALL show each attribute of the Card modal (Column, Category, Priority, dates, assignee) as one plain row of an icon and text in the same font size and height, with no caption above it and no coloured pill, the Priority keeping only a coloured flag icon, and the Column shown read-only (Decision I).
42. THE Frontend SHALL show in the Card modal header only the title text ("Card title | Board name") and the close icon, vertically centred on one line (Decision I).
43. THE Frontend SHALL stage Attachment additions (links and picked files) and removals in the modal draft and apply them only on Save changes, uploading picked files at that moment; WHEN the user closes the modal with unsaved changes, THE Frontend SHALL ask whether to discard them (Decision I). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 25, criteria 6, 13, and 14: Attachments save at once and there is no "unsaved changes" prompt.)**
44. THE Frontend SHALL ask for confirmation in a small confirmation popover, with Cancel and a danger Delete, before deleting a Subtask, removing an Attachment, or deleting a Card (Decision I). **(Amended in V12 by Decision AC: removing an Attachment and deleting a Comment use the modal form of `BaseConfirm`; the other actions keep the popover, Requirement 26.)**
45. THE Frontend SHALL give each Attachment tile and the add-attachment button the same height, border, and radius (Decision I).
46. THE Frontend SHALL show the Card title in the Details card as plain text aligned with the attribute rows below it, with an Edit button, like the description, that turns it into an input (Decision I).
47. THE Frontend SHALL lay out the Start date and End date rows of the date popover on one grid, so the checkboxes, the labels (in a fixed-width column), and the date inputs line up (Decision I).
48. THE Frontend SHALL let the user add a file Attachment through a drop zone that accepts a dragged file or opens the file picker on click, captioned in sentence case ("Drop a file here or browse · PDF, Word or RAR"), and SHALL stage the file as in criterion 43 (Decision I).
49. THE Frontend SHALL place the Card modal close icon inside the header row, so it never overlaps a section of either pane (Decision I).
50. THE Frontend SHALL draw the Category and the Priority of a board Card as two different badge types: the Category as a soft-filled pill in the Category colour with a small dot and its name in lowercase, and the Priority as an outlined badge (border in the Priority colour, no fill) with a flag icon and the Priority in lowercase; the Card modal keeps the same split (Decision I).

### Requirement 5: Drag-and-Drop Card Movement

**User Story:** As a team member, I want to drag cards between columns, so that a card's new position persists after a page refresh.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a user drops a Card between two siblings with `order` values a and b, THE Backend SHALL set the moved Card's Float `order` to the average of a and b without rewriting sibling rows, except for the renumbering defined in criterion 11.
2. WHEN a user drops a Card, THE Frontend SHALL call `PATCH /api/cards/{id}/move` with the target Column and computed Float `order` once, on drop, and reconcile local state with the response.
3. WHEN a drag is in progress, THE Frontend SHALL insert a placeholder at the drop target and animate sibling Cards into place with a FLIP transform.
4. WHEN a Member with Board_Access moves a Card assigned to that Member, THE Backend SHALL apply the move (Decisions 2 and A).
5. IF a Member moves a Card whose `assignee_id` is not that Member, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 (Decision 2).
6. WHEN a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin with Board_Staff_Access to the Card's Board moves any Card on that Board, THE Backend SHALL apply the move (Decision A).
7. IF a request to move a Card carries no Token, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401.
8. WHEN a Card move succeeds, THE Backend SHALL write exactly one Activity_Log row with action `card.moved` and the source and target Column in the same transaction.
9. WHEN a page is refreshed after a successful move, THE Frontend SHALL render the Card in the moved position.
10. WHEN a user drops a Card at the first position of a Column, THE Backend SHALL set the Card's `order` to `min_order - 1`, WHEN at the last position, to `max_order + 1`, and WHEN into an empty Column, to 1.0.
11. IF the gap between two adjacent `order` values in a Column falls below 1e-6, THEN THE Backend SHALL renumber that whole Column as 1.0, 2.0, ... in the same transaction as the move.
12. IF the target Column's `board_id` does not match the Card's current `board_id`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
13. THE Frontend SHALL implement the drag with the HTML5 drag-and-drop API: Cards are draggable and each Column body accepts the drop, and the Column bodies scroll on their own while a Card is dragged (Decision I).

### Requirement 6: Categories

**User Story:** As a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin, I want to manage each board's own categories, so that viewers can filter cards by the kinds of work that board runs.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a Manager, an Admin, or a Supervisor with Board_Staff_Access creates a Category on a Board, THE Backend SHALL store the Category with the Board's id, a name, and a nullable color (Decision A).
2. WHEN a Manager, an Admin, or a Supervisor with Board_Staff_Access renames or recolors a Category, THE Backend SHALL apply the change through `PATCH /api/categories/{id}`.
3. WHEN a Manager, an Admin, or a Supervisor with Board_Staff_Access deletes a Category, THE Backend SHALL remove it through `DELETE /api/categories/{id}`.
4. IF an authenticated Member, or a Supervisor without Board_Staff_Access, attempts to create, edit, or delete a Category, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present.
5. WHEN a user with Board_Access to a Board requests `GET /api/boards/{board_id}/categories`, THE Backend SHALL return that Board's Categories without a role check (Decision A).
6. WHEN a Guest requests `GET /api/shared/{token}/categories` with a valid Share_Token, THE Backend SHALL return that Board's Categories; a Guest request to `GET /api/boards/{board_id}/categories` SHALL return HTTP 401 (Decision K; reworded in V10).
7. THE Backend SHALL return only the Categories owned by the requested Board.
8. WHEN a Board is soft-deleted, THE Backend SHALL keep that Board's Categories stored and make them unreachable.
9. THE Frontend SHALL render the Board's filter-row chips from the `GET /api/boards/{board_id}/categories` response and use no hard-coded chip list.
10. WHEN the viewer switches Boards, THE Frontend SHALL re-fetch and re-render the filter-row chips.
11. WHERE the viewer is a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin, THE Frontend SHALL show a Categories entry in the Sidebar that opens an add, rename, recolor, and delete screen scoped to the current Board. (V16: the Categories item of the Top_Nav and Icon_Rail, Requirement 39, SHELL-05 and SHELL-06.)
12. IF a Category create or rename uses a name that already exists on the same Board when compared case-insensitively, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `CONFLICT`.
13. THE Frontend SHALL draw each Category filter chip with a border, using the brand colour for the border of the active chip (Decision I).
14. THE Frontend SHALL display every Category name in lowercase (board Cards, filter chips, the Card modal, the Categories screen) through styling only, keeping the stored name as entered (Decision I).

### Requirement 7: Comments

**User Story:** As a team member, I want threaded comments with manager priority flags, so that important discussion stays on top and managers can turn their own comments into cards.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a Member, Supervisor, or Manager posts to `POST /api/cards/{id}/comments` on a Card whose Board the user has Board_Access to, THE Backend SHALL create a top-level Comment (Decision A).
2. IF a Comment body is missing or empty, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`, a top-level `message`, and an `error.details` entry for the field `content` holding a list of message strings, the first being `content is required` (Decision G).
3. IF an authenticated Admin attempts to create a Comment, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, and IF no Token is present, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 (Decision B).
4. WHEN a Member, Supervisor, or Manager with Board_Access to the Card's Board posts to `POST /api/comments/{id}/replies` targeting a top-level Comment, THE Backend SHALL create the Reply and increment the parent's Reply_Count in the same transaction (Decision A).
5. IF the target of a reply request has a non-null `parent_id`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400.
6. THE Backend SHALL provide no like or vote endpoint in the comments domain.
7. WHEN a Manager calls `PATCH /api/comments/{id}/priority` on any Comment, THE Backend SHALL toggle that Comment's `is_priority`.
8. IF an authenticated user who is not a Manager calls `PATCH /api/comments/{id}/priority`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present.
9. WHEN `GET /api/cards/{id}/comments` is called, THE Backend SHALL return Priority_Comments first, ordered newest priority first, followed by the remaining top-level Comments in the requested order.
10. WHEN the `sort` parameter is `latest` or omitted, THE Backend SHALL order the remaining top-level Comments newest first.
11. WHEN the `sort` parameter is `popular`, THE Backend SHALL order the remaining top-level Comments by Reply_Count descending, with ties broken by `created_at` descending.
12. THE Backend SHALL return each top-level Comment's Replies nested inline, ordered oldest first, regardless of the `sort` parameter.
13. THE Backend SHALL include `reply_count`, `is_priority`, and `can_create_card` on every returned Comment.
14. WHEN the viewer is a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin and the Comment's author role is manager, THE Backend SHALL set `can_create_card` to true regardless of `is_priority` (Decision 3).
15. IF the viewer is a Member or Guest, or the Comment's author role is not manager, THEN THE Backend SHALL set `can_create_card` to false (Decision 3).
16. IF a Guest calls a comment endpoint, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 whatever the Board's visibility and SHALL return no `comments` field; no shared route returns Comments (Decision K; reworded in V10, breaking: V2 to V9 returned 403 for a Guest on a public Board). (V12: Boards have no visibility.)
17. THE Frontend SHALL render Priority_Comments first with a priority indicator, then the remaining top-level Comments under a visible Latest and Popular toggle that shows the Reply_Count as a badge.
18. THE Frontend SHALL show a Reply action only on top-level Comments.
19. WHERE `can_create_card` is true on a Comment, THE Frontend SHALL show the "create new card" action on that Comment.
20. WHERE the Card response omits `comments`, THE Frontend SHALL omit the Comment thread.
21. THE Backend SHALL include the author's full name as `author_name` and the author's Avatar_URL (or null) as `author_avatar_url` on every returned Comment and Reply, loading names and avatar paths for all authors of a Card's Comments in one query (Decision U), and THE Frontend SHALL show that name and never a placeholder for a known author (Decision I).
22. THE Frontend SHALL render the Comment thread as a thread, not as separate boxes: each Comment shows the author's avatar (the Avatar_Image from `author_avatar_url`, or the Initials_Avatar) on the left, the name and time on one line, the text below, and Reply and priority as small text actions; Replies are indented under their parent with a thin vertical line from the parent's avatar (Decision I).

### Requirement 8: Activity Log

**User Story:** As a team member, I want an append-only history of what happened on a card and a board, so that I can trust the record of changes.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a state-changing action succeeds, THE Backend SHALL insert exactly one Activity_Log row with `actor_id`, `action`, `target_type`, `target_id`, `metadata`, and `created_at` in the same transaction as the change.
2. THE Backend SHALL never update or delete an Activity_Log row after insertion.
3. WHEN a signed-in user with Board_Access to a Card's Board requests the Card's activity, THE Backend SHALL return the rows where `target_type` is `card` and `target_id` is that Card's id (Decision A).
4. WHEN a signed-in user with Board_Access to a Board requests the Board's activity feed, THE Backend SHALL return every row for the Cards and Columns belonging to that Board (Decision A).
5. IF a Guest requests Card activity or a Board activity feed, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 and no `activity` or `activity_log` field, and no shared route returns Activity_Log data, including when the field is requested explicitly (Decision K; reworded in V10).
6. WHEN a user opens Activity from the Sidebar nav, THE Frontend SHALL open the Side_Panel on the Activity view. (V16: from the Top_Nav or Icon_Rail, Requirement 39, SHELL-08.)
7. WHERE the Card response omits `activity`, THE Frontend SHALL omit the Card activity section.
8. THE Backend SHALL include the actor's full name as `actor_name` on every returned Activity_Log row, loading names for all actors of a response in one query, and THE Frontend SHALL show that name and never a placeholder for a known actor (Decision I).
9. THE Backend SHALL store, in the `metadata` of each new Activity_Log row, the names needed to read it without a lookup: `card_title` on every Card event; for `card.updated`, `changes` as an object of `{ "from": ..., "to": ... }` per changed field among `title`, `priority`, `start_date`, `end_date`, and `category` (the Category's name), with a changed `description` recorded by field name only; `from_column_name` and `to_column_name` on `card.moved` beside the ids; `assignee_name` on `card.assigned`; `title` on Subtask events; and `name` on Attachment events (Decision I). (V12: one `card.updated` row per saved request, and new Subtask and Attachment actions, Requirement 25, criteria 11 and 12.)
10. THE Frontend SHALL write each Activity_Log row as the actor, the action, the object, and, for a move or a change, where it went, for example "Manager moved "Card title" from [Todo] to [In Progress]" or "Manager changed the priority of "Card title" from Medium to High", with the actor in bold, the object in quotes, Column names as badges, and the time under the sentence, and SHALL fall back to generic wording for rows written before Decision I that lack these names (Decision I).
11. WHEN a signed-in user with Board_Access requests `GET /api/boards/{id}/activity` or `GET /api/cards/{id}/activity` with `limit` and optional `before`, THE Backend SHALL return at most `limit` rows (default 20, at most 100) older than the row `before` in the same order and response shape as today; IF `before` names a row outside that feed, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` (Decision J).
12. THE Frontend SHALL load the first page of an activity list sized to fill the visible area and show a Load more action that requests the next page with `before` set to the last shown row, hiding the action once a page returns fewer rows than `limit` (Decision J).

### Requirement 9: Notifications and Inbox

**User Story:** As a member, I want to be notified when a card is assigned to me or reaches its deadline, so that I do not miss work.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a Card is assigned to a user, THE Backend SHALL insert a Notification of type `assigned` for that user in the same transaction as the assignment. (V12: Notification types `board_join_requested`, `board_added`, and `board_join_rejected` are added, Requirement 28.)
2. WHEN the scheduled daily job runs, THE Backend SHALL create one `deadline` Notification for the Assigned_Member of each Card whose `end_date` equals today in the APP_TIMEZONE timezone and whose `deleted_at` is null, using no per-user timezone.  (V17: superseded by DUE-01 to DUE-05, Requirement 58; Open Question 56.)
3. WHEN the scheduled daily job runs more than once on the same date, THE Backend SHALL create no duplicate `deadline` Notification for the same Card and assignee.  (V17: superseded by DUE-03, Requirement 58.)
4. THE Backend SHALL create Notifications on the server and the Frontend SHALL not compute or poll them client-side.
5. WHEN a signed-in user requests the Inbox, THE Backend SHALL return only that user's Notifications and an unread count that reflects read-marking through criteria 8 and 9.
6. WHEN a user clicks Inbox in the Sidebar or clicks the header bell, THE Frontend SHALL open the Side_Panel on the Inbox view through the same toggle. (V12: each Inbox row is a link, Requirement 28, criteria 5 and 6.) (V16: from the Top_Nav, the Icon_Rail, or the bell, Requirement 39, SHELL-07.)  (V17: superseded by NAV-06, Requirement 47: the bell only.)
7. WHERE the API response omits Inbox data, THE Frontend SHALL omit the Inbox panel.
8. WHEN a signed-in user calls `PATCH /api/notifications/{id}/read` on one of the user's own Notifications, THE Backend SHALL mark that Notification read.
9. WHEN a signed-in user calls `PATCH /api/notifications/read-all`, THE Backend SHALL mark all of that user's own Notifications read.
10. IF a user calls `PATCH /api/notifications/{id}/read` on another user's Notification, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 404.
11. WHEN a signed-in user requests the Inbox with `limit` and optional `before`, THE Backend SHALL return at most `limit` of that user's Notifications (default 20, at most 100) older than the Notification `before`, keeping `unread_count` for all of the user's Notifications (Decision J).
