12. THE Frontend SHALL load the first Inbox page sized to fill the visible area and show a Load more action for the next page, hiding it once a page returns fewer rows than `limit` (Decision J).

### Requirement 10: Analytics

**User Story:** As a Supervisor or Manager, I want pre-aggregated board analytics, so that I can judge progress without opening every board.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a Supervisor requests `GET /api/boards/{id}/analytics` for a Board whose `owner_id` is that Supervisor, THE Backend SHALL return an Analytics_Summary (Decision 1).
2. IF a Supervisor requests analytics for a Board whose `owner_id` is not that Supervisor, including a Board where that Supervisor is a BoardMember, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403.
3. WHEN a Manager requests analytics for any Board, THE Backend SHALL return an Analytics_Summary.
4. IF an authenticated Member or Admin requests analytics, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, and IF no Token is present, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 (Decision B, intentional).
5. THE Backend SHALL return Analytics_Summary payloads of counts, deltas, and overdue totals, such as `{ "status": true, "message": "OK", "data": { "done": 12, "in_progress": 4, "overdue": 2 } }`, counting an Overdue_Card as a Card whose `end_date` is before today, whose Column is not `Done`, and which is not soft-deleted, and never a raw dump of every Card. (V16: `done_delta` counts Cards whose `completed_at` is within 7 days, Requirement 43, THR-04.)
6. WHEN a Manager requests cross-board analytics, THE Backend SHALL return one Analytics_Summary per Board in a single pre-aggregated response.
7. THE Backend SHALL grant a Supervisor analytics access only through Board ownership and never through BoardMember (Decision A).
8. WHERE the viewer is a Supervisor or a Manager, THE Frontend SHALL show an analytics strip on Home with the totals of Cards in progress, done, and overdue, from `GET /api/boards/{id}/analytics` for each Board a Supervisor owns and from the cross-board analytics for a Manager; Members and Admins SHALL NOT see the strip (Decisions 1 and B).

### Requirement 11: Board Visibility and Archiving

**User Story:** As a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin, I want to control board visibility and archive boards and cards, so that guests see only what the owner shares through a link and finished work stays recoverable.

Note (V12, Decision Y): criteria 1 to 3, 6, 11, 12, and 13 are superseded by Requirement 22 because Boards have no visibility. Criteria 4, 5, 7 to 10 stand (Board archive and soft-delete are unchanged).

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. THE Backend SHALL store each Board's `visibility` as exactly `public` or `private`. **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**
2. WHEN a Manager, an Admin, or a Supervisor who is the Board_Owner or a BoardMember sets a Board's visibility, THE Backend SHALL apply the change and record it in the Activity_Log (Decision A). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**
3. IF an authenticated Member, or a Supervisor who is neither owner nor BoardMember, attempts to set Board visibility, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present. **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**
4. IF a visitor without a Token requests the Board list, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 with an error Envelope (Decision K; reverses V2 to V9, which returned the public Boards to a Guest; breaking).
5. IF a Guest requests any Board by id, public or private, through a route other than a shared route, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 with an error Envelope (Decision K; widened in V10 from private Boards to every Board). (V12: every Board is private.)
6. WHEN a signed-in Member requests a private Board where the Member is neither the Board's owner, a BoardMember, a Manager, nor an Admin, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 (Decision A). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**
7. THE Backend SHALL return a Board's Columns and Cards to a Guest only through the shared routes of Requirement 18, and those responses SHALL omit `comments`, `activity`, and `activity_log` (Decision K; reworded in V10).
8. WHEN a Manager or Admin archives a Board, THE Backend SHALL set the Board's `archived_at` and allow the archive to be reversed (Decision 4).
9. IF an authenticated Member or Supervisor attempts to archive a Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision 4).
10. WHEN a Supervisor, Manager, or Admin with Board_Staff_Access archives a Card, THE Backend SHALL set the Card's `archived_at`.
11. IF a signed-in Supervisor who is neither the Board_Owner nor a BoardMember requests a private Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 (Decision A). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**
12. WHEN a signed-in user requests the Board list, THE Backend SHALL return the public non-deleted Boards plus the non-deleted Boards to which the user has Board_Access (Decision A). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**
13. THE Backend SHALL let every signed-in user, a Member included, read a public Board and its Columns, Cards, and Categories; only a Guest loses public browsing (Decisions A and K, restated in V10). **(Superseded in V12 by Requirement 22: Boards have no visibility; kept for history.)**

### Requirement 12: Search and Filter

**User Story:** As a user viewing a board, I want to search and filter its cards, so that I can find work quickly.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHEN a user with Board_Access calls `GET /api/boards/{id}/cards` with `search=<text>`, THE Backend SHALL return only Cards on that Board whose title or description contains the search value as a case-insensitive substring.
2. WHEN a user with Board_Access calls `GET /api/boards/{id}/cards` with `filter[priority]`, `filter[assignee]`, or `filter[category]`, THE Backend SHALL return only Cards matching every supplied filter.
3. THE Backend SHALL exclude Soft_Deleted Cards from search and filter results.
4. IF a filter field is not supported, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
5. WHEN a user selects a Category chip, THE Frontend SHALL request the Board's Cards with the matching category filter.
6. WHEN a Guest calls `GET /api/shared/{token}/cards` with `search` or `filter[...]`, THE Backend SHALL apply them as criteria 1 and 2 do and return Card data only, with no Comment or Activity_Log fields (Decision K; reworded in V10; Open Question 13 is resolved by Decision W).
7. IF a signed-in user without Board_Access calls the Cards endpoint of a private Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, and IF a Guest calls `GET /api/boards/{id}/cards` of any Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 (Decisions A and K). (V12: "a private Board" reads "a Board", because every Board is private, Requirement 22.)

### Requirement 13: API Conventions and Data Integrity

**User Story:** As a frontend developer, I want one predictable API shape and consistent data rules, so that the client has a single place to unwrap responses and errors.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. THE Backend SHALL serve all endpoints under `/api/...` without a version segment.
2. WHEN a request succeeds, THE Backend SHALL return `{ "status": true, "message": <non-empty string>, "data": <payload> }` with the payload as an object, a list, or null, using a short descriptive `message` for each mutating endpoint (for example "Card moved") and "OK" for reads (Decision G).
3. IF a request fails, THEN THE Backend SHALL return `{ "status": false, "message": <non-empty string>, "error": { "code": string, "details"?: object } }` with the matching HTTP status of 400, 401, 403, 404, 405, 409, 429, or 500, stating the message text only at the top level and including no `message` field in `error`, and with `error.details` present only for `VALIDATION_ERROR`, for the `CONFLICT` of a registration (per field, criterion 4 of Requirement 1), and for `RATE_LIMITED` (`retry_after`) (Decisions G and O; amended in V10).
4. THE Backend SHALL return the Envelope shape, including `status` and `message`, on every response except the Swagger UI HTML page, the raw OpenAPI document, and the successful file bytes of the signed file route; error responses of the signed file route SHALL still use the Envelope (Decision G).
5. THE Backend SHALL use a UUID primary key on every entity and include `created_at` and `updated_at` on every entity.
6. THE Backend SHALL include `deleted_at` and `archived_at` on Board and Card.
7. THE Backend SHALL access the database only through each blueprint's own `repository.py`.
8. THE Backend SHALL read CORS origins from an environment variable allow-list and never use the wildcard `*`.
9. THE System SHALL provide a `.env.example` for the Backend and the Frontend and commit no real `.env` file.
10. IF a request carries no Token on an endpoint that requires one, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401, and IF a valid Token lacks the required role or Board_Access, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, except `POST /api/auth/register`, `POST /api/auth/login`, `GET /api/auth/availability`, the shared routes of Requirement 18, and the signed file routes of Attachments and avatars (Decisions D and R), which take a signature in place of a Token (Decision K; reworded in V10, the earlier exception for Guests reading public Boards is removed).
11. THE Backend SHALL set the Envelope `status` to true when the HTTP status is 2xx and to false otherwise, on every Envelope response, including 401, 403, 404, 405, 409, 429, and 500 responses and the errors of the signed file route, with the exceptions of the Swagger UI HTML page, the raw OpenAPI document, and the successful file bytes of the signed file route, which carry no Envelope, and THE Backend SHALL carry the numeric HTTP status on the HTTP line only (Decision G).
12. THE Backend SHALL use flask-smorest, registering every blueprint on one `Api` instance, and SHALL declare request bodies, query arguments, and responses of every endpoint with marshmallow schemas (Decision E).
13. IF request body or query validation fails, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` object mapping each failing field name to a list of message strings from marshmallow, and SHALL NOT return HTTP 422 (Decisions E and G).
14. THE Backend SHALL keep each blueprint layered `routes.py → service.py → repository.py` with the marshmallow schemas in `schema.py` (Decision E).
15. THE Backend SHALL declare each success response with a schema that wraps the blueprint's payload schema in the Envelope (Decision E).
16. THE Backend SHALL declare the SQLAlchemy relationships `Board.columns`, `Board.members`, `Card.subtasks`, `Card.attachments`, and `Comment.replies`, each paired with its inverse through `back_populates`, declared with `lazy="raise"`, and without a delete cascade (Decision F).
17. THE Backend SHALL define no `to_dict` or other serialization method on any model (Decision F).
18. THE Backend SHALL ensure that services and schemas trigger no lazy load, by having each repository load the related rows it returns with `selectinload` or `joinedload` (Decision F).
19. THE Backend SHALL leave every list endpoint unpaged except the Inbox and the two activity feeds, which take `limit` and `before` as defined in Requirements 8.11 and 9.11; an unknown or non-numeric `limit` SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` (Decision J).

### Requirement 14: Non-Functional Quality

**User Story:** As a product owner, I want measurable performance, security, and mobile usability targets, so that quality can be verified.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHILE the Backend is under normal load, THE Backend SHALL respond within 300 ms at the 95th percentile.
2. WHEN a Board with 200 Cards loads over a throttled Fast 3G connection, THE Frontend SHALL reach first meaningful paint within 2 seconds.
3. THE Backend SHALL include no `comments`, `activity`, or `activity_log` field in any shared route response, and SHALL return HTTP 401 to a Guest who calls a comment or activity endpoint directly (Decision K; reworded in V10).
4. WHILE the viewport width is between 360 and 414 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL show every page without horizontal scroll or cut-off or overlapping content.
5. THE Frontend SHALL write base (unprefixed) styles for a 360 to 414 pixel screen and add tablet and desktop layouts only through `min-width` variants, never a `max-width` variant that strips a desktop layout down for mobile.
6. WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL show the Sidebar as an off-canvas drawer behind a scrim with its toggle in a persistent header. (V16: superseded by Requirement 39, SHELL-04: the Nav_Drawer.)
7. WHILE the viewport is 960 pixels or wider, THE Frontend SHALL keep the Sidebar visible, expanded or collapsed to the icon rail (criterion 16). (V16: superseded by Requirement 39, SHELL-01 to SHELL-03: the Icon_Rail and the Top_Nav.)
8. THE Frontend SHALL place each primary action (add card, open Inbox, switch Board) within a persistent header or the bottom third of the screen on mobile.
9. THE Frontend SHALL take all colors, spacing, radii, and shadows from the Design_Tokens, exposed to Tailwind through its theme, and SHALL NOT use Tailwind's default palette, arbitrary values such as `bg-[#fff]` or `p-[13px]`, or hard-coded hex or pixel values in components, and SHALL NOT write numeric literals for sizes, layers (z-index), durations, or opacities: every such value is a named token utility (Decision H).
10. THE Frontend SHALL build shared buttons, badges, modals (with a header, a scrolling body, and a footer slot), popovers, calendars, tooltips, searchable selects, and menus only as `BaseButton`, `BaseBadge`, `BaseModal`, `BasePopover`, `BaseCalendar`, `BaseTooltip`, `BaseCombobox`, and, from V10, `BaseMenu` in `components/shared/`, implemented as thin wrappers over the shadcn/ui `Button`, `Badge`, `Dialog`, `Popover`, `Calendar`, `Tooltip`, `Command`, and `DropdownMenu` primitives, and no other file SHALL import the shadcn/ui primitives directly (Decisions H and I).
11. THE Frontend SHALL render components as Server Components unless a component needs state, an event handler, or a browser API.
12. WHEN a list endpoint returns rows, THE Backend SHALL execute a number of database queries that does not depend on the number of returned rows, so that the board cards list with Subtasks and Attachments loads in at most a fixed number of queries instead of one query per Card (Decision F).
13. THE Frontend SHALL style components with Tailwind CSS and shadcn/ui primitives copied into `components/ui/`, themed from the Design_Tokens so the look follows the product owner's visual reference (light surfaces, navy brand, pastel tags, icon rail, flat columns), and MAY refine spacing, motion, and visual hierarchy without changing that palette, layout, or the interaction patterns (Decision H). (V16: superseded by Requirement 38, the Design_System.)
14. THE Frontend SHALL set the Tailwind `lg` breakpoint to 960 pixels so the Sidebar drawer behaviour of criteria 6 and 7 is unchanged (Decision H).
15. THE Frontend SHALL show a visible focus ring from the `--ring` token on every interactive element, and SHALL NOT remove an outline without a replacement focus style (Decision H).
16. THE Frontend SHALL give the Sidebar a toggle, which is the BrandLogo at its top (V11, Requirement 21, criterion 8), that switches between an expanded state (icons with text labels, with the Boards list and team members below them) and a collapsed state (the icon rail only, the logo mark alone with the tooltip RevoTask, and every other icon with a tooltip shown on hover and on focus), the expanded state showing Logo_Full (the mark with the RevoTask wordmark), SHALL remember the choice in the browser, and SHALL keep the off-canvas drawer below 960 pixels unchanged (Decision I). (V16: superseded by Requirement 39, SHELL-22: the Sidebar is removed.)
17. THE Frontend SHALL set the font to Inter, self-hosted from the application bundle with no request to a third-party font service, through the `--font-stack` token (Decision I). (V16: amended by Requirement 38, DS-10 and DS-11: Plus Jakarta Sans headings and Inter body, both self-hosted.)
18. THE Frontend SHALL keep the page itself from scrolling on a Board: the header stays fixed, the Columns scroll sideways inside the Main area, each Column scrolls vertically inside itself, and each Column's add-card action stays pinned at its bottom (Decision I).
19. THE Frontend SHALL name the application RevoTask in the Sidebar (through Logo_Full), the page titles, and the sign-in pages (through BrandLogo, Requirement 21), and SHALL keep corner radii small (no more than 10 pixels on Cards, Columns, buttons, and the modal) through the radius tokens (Decision I). (V16: the radius limit is superseded by Requirement 38, DS-12 to DS-14; the product name is RevoBoard.)
20. THE Frontend SHALL use one control, type, and spacing scale everywhere: inputs, selects, buttons, and Card modal attribute rows 40 pixels high, compact buttons and filter chips 32 pixels; page and modal titles 18 pixels semibold, section headings 14 pixels semibold, body and control text 14 pixels regular, secondary text 13 pixels muted, labels 12 pixels medium muted; an 8 pixel grid with 16 pixels of padding in every card and section, 12 pixels between rows, 24 pixels between sections, and aligned left edges, all through tokens (Decision I). (V16: type sizes come from Requirement 38, DS-08, and control sizes from DS-17 and DS-18; the 8 pixel grid stands.)
21. WHEN the pointer leaves a Sidebar item, or the item loses keyboard focus, THE Frontend SHALL hide its tooltip, and a click on the item SHALL NOT leave the tooltip showing (Decision I). (V16: applies to the Icon_Rail items, Requirement 39, SHELL-02.)
22. THE Frontend SHALL size board Columns fluidly between 272 and 320 pixels, give a board Card 16 pixels of padding, a 15 pixel title, 13 pixel secondary text, and 12 pixel tags, show up to two lines of the Card's description, and let these text sizes grow slightly from 1280 pixels of width, all through tokens (Decision I).
23. THE Frontend SHALL replace every native select with `BaseCombobox`, a button that opens a popover holding a keyboard-navigable list and, for lists of six or more options, a search field (Decision I).
24. THE Frontend SHALL show tooltips only through `BaseTooltip`, on the Sidebar items when collapsed and on every avatar, including the assignee avatar on a board Card, which shows the member's name (Decision I). (V16: tooltips on the Icon_Rail items, Requirement 39, SHELL-02.)
25. THE Frontend SHALL use the danger button for every delete or remove action, the secondary button for archive and cancel, and the primary button for submit, save, and add (Decision I).
26. THE Frontend SHALL give the Sidebar one type hierarchy: the brand shown as the BrandLogo image (no text size, V11), navigation items 14 pixels medium, Board rows 14 pixels regular, section labels 11 pixels uppercase muted, member names 13 pixels medium with the Username 12 pixels muted (V10; was the login), with every item the same height and left padding (Decision I). (V16: superseded by Requirement 38, DS-08 and DS-18.)
27. THE Frontend SHALL treat every page of the `(app)` route group as a Protected_Route: WHEN a request for such a page carries no Session_Cookie, THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/login` before the page renders through `frontend/proxy.ts`, and SHALL NOT guard `/login`, `/register`, or `/shared/{token}` (Decision P).
28. THE Frontend SHALL treat a malformed, expired, or Backend-rejected Session_Cookie on a Protected_Route as signed out: it ends on `/login`, removes the cookie, and keeps the Safe_Return_Path, as Requirement 1, criteria 57 to 61 define (Decision T).

### Requirement 15: Automated Testing

Note (V17): the V17 test plan, its criteria `[V17-T01]` to `[V17-T14]`, and the owner's visual review checklist are in `specs/v17/41-VERIFICATION.md`; they extend this requirement and Requirement 44.
**User Story:** As a developer, I want automated tests for the API and the main user journey, so that regressions are caught.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for comment creation covering success, validation failure, unauthenticated, and unauthorized cases.
2. THE System SHALL include a Playwright end-to-end test of the Kanban journey that runs against the deployed URL.
3. THE System SHALL contain no `waitForTimeout` call in any Playwright test file.
4. THE System SHALL unit-test each `service.py` against a fake repository without a real database.
5. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests covering the HTTP 401 (no Token) and HTTP 403 (insufficient role or access) cases for every mutating endpoint.
6. THE System SHALL include a `pytest` test that an unrelated Member receives HTTP 403 on a private Board (Decision A). (V12: every Board is private; see criterion 29.)
7. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for Attachment access covering a permitted viewer, an unrelated Member, a Guest (HTTP 401 on the authenticated route), and an expired signature (Decision D; reworded in V10).
8. THE System SHALL include a `pytest` test that counts the database queries of the board cards list with 5 Cards and with 50 Cards, each with Subtasks and Attachments, and asserts that the two counts are equal (Decision F).
9. THE System SHALL include a `pytest` test that asserts accessing an unloaded relationship raises an error (Decision F).
10. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL be written in Playwright under `frontend/tests/e2e/auth/` as `signin.spec.ts` and `signout.spec.ts`, and SHALL reach every locator and action of the login page and the Sign_Out_Control only through the Auth_Page_Object (V9).
11. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL read the base URL from `E2E_BASE_URL`, the Test_Account's Username or Email from `E2E_USER_IDENTIFIER`, and its password from `E2E_USER_PASSWORD` (the Username or Email of the Test_Account in V10), and SHALL contain no credential literal (V9).
12. IF `E2E_BASE_URL`, `E2E_USER_IDENTIFIER`, or `E2E_USER_PASSWORD` is unset or empty, THEN THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL fail before the first test with an error message that names the missing variable (V9).
13. THE Frontend SHALL list `E2E_BASE_URL`, `E2E_USER_IDENTIFIER`, and `E2E_USER_PASSWORD` with empty or placeholder values in `frontend/.env.example`, and no real `.env` file SHALL be committed (V9).
14. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL use web-first assertions such as `expect(locator).toBeVisible()` and `expect(page).toHaveURL()`, and SHALL contain no `waitForTimeout` call (extends criterion 3) (V9).
15. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL start every test in a fresh browser context with no Session_Cookie, SHALL create no account or other data, and SHALL leave the Test_Account unchanged (V9). The only rows the suite causes are Rate_Limiter attempt rows for a Throwaway_Identifier, which the Rate_Limiter deletes when they age out of the window (V10).
16. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for the Rate_Limiter that use a fake clock and small thresholds passed through the app factory, covering the block at the threshold, the reset on success, the per-IP limit, the window expiry, the `Retry-After` value, two workers sharing the same table, that `POST /api/auth/register` is never answered with 429 however many requests one Client_IP makes, and the `change_password` scope (block at the threshold per user, reset on success, independent per user, no password comparison while blocked) (Requirement 20; Decisions Q and W; V10).
17. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests that a Share_Token stops working immediately after disable or regenerate, that no shared response contains `comments`, `activity`, or `activity_log`, and that a Signed_File_URL issued through a Share_Token stops working after disable or regenerate (Requirement 18; V10).
18. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for unique case-insensitive `username` and `email` and for the V10 migration reset of criterion 63 of Requirement 1 (the abort when `APP_ENV` is not `development` or `testing`, the empty user tables and the NOT NULL columns after `upgrade`, and a working `downgrade`) (Decisions M and V; V10, reworded in round 3).
19. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL read nothing about the rate-limit thresholds from the Backend: the rate-limit test SHALL keep submitting wrong passwords for a Throwaway_Identifier until the form shows the wait time, up to 10 attempts, and SHALL fail with a message naming `RATE_LIMIT_LOGIN_PAIR_MAX` if no block appears (V10).
20. THE System SHALL include `pytest` API tests for the avatar endpoints. Happy cases: a PNG, a JPEG, and a WebP are each accepted and the returned Avatar_URL serves the same bytes with the matching Content-Type; a file named `x.txt` that holds PNG bytes is accepted, and the stored name is a generated name with the detected extension; replacing deletes the previous file and `DELETE` deletes the current one and succeeds when there is none; a user without an avatar has `avatar_url` null. Negative cases: no Token returns 401; a text file named `x.png`, an SVG, a GIF, and an empty file return 400 with `details.file`; a file of 2,097,153 bytes returns 400 and one of 2,097,152 bytes is accepted; a missing `file` part returns 400; a file name such as `../../x.png` leaves no file outside `UPLOAD_DIR/avatars/`; a bad, modified, or expired signature returns 403; a signature for one user does not open another user's file; a user without an avatar returns 404 from the file route; `PATCH /api/auth/me` with `avatar_url` returns 400; no shared route response contains `avatar_url` (Decision R; V10 round 2).
21. THE System SHALL include Frontend component tests for the Profile avatar section covering the preview after choosing a file, the Upload and Remove actions (Remove behind `BaseConfirm`), the wrong-type message, the too-large message, a server `details.file` message, and the Initials_Avatar fallback with one refresh of the URL on an image load error (Decision R; V10 round 2).
22. THE E2E_Auth_Suite SHALL contain no avatar test, because uploading changes account data and criterion 15 forbids that; avatar behaviour is verified by criteria 20 and 21 and by a manual smoke check before release (upload, preview, replace, remove) (V10 round 2).
23. THE System SHALL include `vitest` tests for `frontend/proxy.ts` (no cookie, malformed cookie, `exp` in the past, a valid unexpired cookie, cookie removal on the redirect, and the public paths untouched) and for the Safe_Return_Path rule (`/boards`, `/boards?x=1`, `//evil.com`, `/\evil.com`, `https://evil.com`, `/other`, and a 513-character path), and `pytest` tests that a malformed or expired Bearer Token on a shared route is answered as a Guest with 200 (Decision T; V10 round 2).
24. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests that the candidate response carries `email` for the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin and returns 403 for other signed-in users, that `GET /api/boards/{id}/members` has no `email` key by default and returns 403 for `include=email` from a Member, that no Comment, Activity_Log, Notification, shared, or default member response body contains another user's Email, and that a candidate search of 0 or 1 characters after trimming returns 400 with `details.search` while 2 characters are accepted (Decisions S and W; V10 rounds 2 and 3).
25. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests that `author_avatar_url`, `avatar_url`, and `assignee_avatar_url` of Requirement 19, criterion 34, are signed URLs that open the right bytes and are null for a user without an Avatar_Image; that the same fields are absent from every shared route, Activity_Log row, and Notification body; that no Email appears in the Comment, member (default), shared, activity, or Notification bodies; and that the query count of the Comment list and of the Card list is the same for 5 and for 50 distinct authors or assignees (Decision U; V10 round 3).
26. THE System SHALL include Frontend component tests that `BaseAvatar` shows the Avatar_Image when the URL is set and the Initials_Avatar of Requirement 19, criterion 39 when it is null or fails twice, and that `CommentThread`, the Sidebar Team members, `MemberSearch`, `MemberPicker`, the board Card, and the Card modal assignee chip render it (Decision U; V10 round 3).
27. THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests that `flask seed-users` and `flask seed-data` give every seeded user a Username, an Email, and a full name, that the Test_Account `member` can log in with its Username and with its Email, and that a second run creates no duplicate (Requirement 1, criterion 64; Decision V; V10 round 3).
28. [BRAND-T] THE System SHALL include the Playwright and `vitest` checks of Requirement 21, criterion 17: the favicon request, the logo alt text and natural size, no third-party request, the collapsed and expanded Sidebar variants, no hard-coded colour in `BrandLogo`, and no file left in `frontend/images/` (V11).
29. [V12-T1] THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for the access model of Requirement 22: that no `visibility` column exists and a `visibility` field is rejected or ignored as the schema defines; the Board list for an owner, a BoardMember, an unrelated Member, a Manager, and an Admin; HTTP 403 for an unrelated Member and Supervisor on every Board-scoped route; no BoardMember for a Manager or an Admin (including the role-change cleanup of ACC-06); assignee only from owner or BoardMembers (including the cleanup of ACC-10 and ACC-12); and the migration reset and abort of ACC-17 (Decision Y).
30. [V12-T2] THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for Requirements 23 and 24: Public_Link management only by Manager and Admin (owner, Supervisor BoardMember, and Member get 403); one test per write endpoint of PUB-10 and PUB-11 (comment, edit field, attachment, subtask, archive, delete) returning 401 and 403 and changing nothing; HTTP 405 on every mutating method under `/api/shared`; the invite lifecycle (generate, regenerate, disable, old token 404); the join-request flow (request 201, Manager and Admin request 400, member and duplicate 409, simultaneous requests leave one pending row, reject then request again, approve creates one BoardMember, owner PATCH 403, decided request 409); and that no join-request create or decision response and no Notification body contains an Email or a token, and the pending list (INV-13) is the only join-request response that carries an Email (Decisions Z, AA, and S as amended in V12 round 2).
31. [V12-T3] THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for Requirement 26 and 27: comment delete by the author (reply decrements `reply_count`, top-level deletes its replies, `n` is correct), 403 for a Manager and an Admin, 404 unknown, the `comment.deleted` row without text; the Subtask matrix of SUB-01 for each role and relationship; `progress` as 0 to 10, null without Subtasks, and 400 for a posted `progress`; and that Property 53 (reply count consistency) holds under random interleaved adds and deletes (Decisions AC and AD).
32. [V12-T4] THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for Requirement 28: the three partial unique indexes (two board notifications with a null `card_id` on the same day are deduplicated, two different join requests both notify, a card notification is deduplicated as before), the CHECK constraint, the notification fan-out of INV-08, INV-14, INV-15, and ACC-12, and a constant query count for `GET /api/notifications` at 5 and at 50 rows (Decision AE).
33. [V12-T5] THE System SHALL include Frontend component tests for Requirement 25: no "Save changes" button; blur and Enter on the title send one `PATCH` with only `title`; no request for an unchanged value or an empty title; each chip popover sends one `PATCH` with one field; the Saving, Saved, and error-with-Retry states; closing waits for an in-flight save; the failed-field close confirmation; a response does not overwrite a field being edited; and each Subtask and Attachment call goes out at once (Decision AB).
34. [V12-T6] THE System SHALL include Frontend component tests for Requirements 24, 26, 28, and 29: the Invite modal for owner, Manager, and Admin (Approve and Reject only for Manager and Admin), the `/invite/{token}` states, the attachment and comment confirm modals (Cancel sends nothing, the reply text appears), the Inbox rows as links with the hrefs of INB-05, the unavailable-card notices, and the Profile grid classes at the 960 pixel breakpoint (Decisions AA, AC, AE, and AF).
35. [V12-T7] THE System SHALL include `vitest` tests that `frontend/proxy.ts` redirects a cookie-less `/invite/{token}` request to `/login?next=/invite/{token}` and that the Safe_Return_Path rule accepts `/invite/<43 URL-safe characters>` and still rejects `//evil.com`, `/\evil.com`, `https://evil.com`, `/other`, and `/invite` followed by `//` (Decision P as amended in V12).
36. [V12-T8] THE System SHALL include `pytest` tests for round 2 within V12: `GET /api/boards/{id}/members` returns the Board_Owner first, once, with `is_owner` true (also when the owner is a BoardMember) and no `email` by default; `include=email` adds the Email for the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin and returns 403 for a Member; the candidate search never lists the owner; `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/members/{owner_id}` returns 404 for an owner who is not a BoardMember; `GET /api/boards/{id}/join-requests` carries `email` for the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin, returns 403 for a Member and for a Supervisor who is only a BoardMember, and 401 without a Token; and no Email appears in the create or decision responses, Notification bodies, Activity_Log `metadata`, Comment bodies, or any `/api/shared` payload (Decisions M and S; ACC-13, ACC-20, INV-13, INV-28).
37. [V12-T9] THE System SHALL include Frontend component tests for round 2 within V12: the Sidebar Team members list shows the owner first with the "Owner" tag and no remove button on that row while the other rows keep theirs for the owner, Manager, and Admin; `MemberSearch` shows no owner row; the assignee popover lists the owner first; each pending row of the Invite modal shows Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar, full name, Username, and Email; and no Inbox row or Sidebar member row renders an Email (ACC-19, INV-19, INV-27).
38. [V12-M1] THE System SHALL keep a manual verification checklist for the invite and join-request flow (design.md, "Manual verification checklist (V12 invite flow)", run by task 35.31) that uses two accounts and writes data on a local or scratch database only.
39. [V12-D1] THE System SHALL list "automated invite-flow end-to-end test" as a deferred item and SHALL schedule no data-writing Playwright test or Playwright project for the invite, join, or approve flow in V12; the E2E_Auth_Suite stays read-only (Requirement 1, criteria 65 to 72; AUTH-E2E-V12-08).

### Requirement 16: Stretch Features

**User Story:** As a product owner, I want the prototype's extra card features tracked as stretch work, so that they do not delay the core paths.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. WHERE the stretch features are enabled, THE Backend SHALL store a nullable 0 to 10 integer `progress` and an integer `views_count` defaulting to 0 on Card. **(Amended in V12 by Decision AD: `progress` is no longer stored; only `views_count` stays a stretch column.)**
2. WHERE the stretch features are enabled, THE Frontend SHALL show the per-card progress meter, views counter, and the decorative Overview, Notes, and Questions tabs as in the prototype. **(Amended in V12: the progress meter is derived from Subtasks and is not stretch, Requirement 27; the views counter and the decorative tabs stay stretch.)**
3. THE System SHALL build the stretch features only after Requirements 1 to 15 work end-to-end.

### Requirement 17: API Documentation

**User Story:** As a frontend developer and reviewer, I want a generated OpenAPI document, an interactive Swagger page, and a written API reference, so that I can learn every endpoint without reading the backend code, while the public production server exposes no extra surface.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. THE Backend SHALL generate its OpenAPI 3 document with flask-smorest, describing every registered `/api` route with its method, path, path and query parameters, request schema, Envelope response schema, Token requirement, and error responses 400, 401, 403, 404, and 409, plus 429 on the rate-limited operations of Requirement 20 (Decisions E and O).
2. THE System SHALL keep a hand-written `backend/API.md` that lists the same endpoints grouped by blueprint, with the Envelope shape, the error codes, the role-to-endpoint matrix, and one example request and response for each group.
3. WHEN a developer runs `flask export-openapi`, THE Backend SHALL write the generated document to `backend/openapi.json`.
4. WHEN `APP_ENV` is `development` or `testing`, THE Backend SHALL serve Swagger UI at `/api/docs` and the generated document at `/api/openapi.json`.
5. WHEN `APP_ENV` is anything other than `development` or `testing` (including `production` and unset), THE Backend SHALL NOT register the Swagger UI route or the `/api/openapi.json` route, so both return the 404 Envelope.
6. THE Backend SHALL read `APP_ENV` from the environment, default it to `production`, list it in `backend/.env.example`, and not refuse to start when it is missing.
7. THE Backend SHALL treat the documentation routes as read-only and public, SHALL NOT return secrets, and SHALL exclude them from the 401/403 mutating-route sweep.
8. THE System SHALL include an automated test that fails when a registered `/api` route has no operation in the generated OpenAPI document.
9. THE System SHALL include an automated test that confirms the documentation routes return 404 when `APP_ENV` is `production`.
10. THE System SHALL NOT depend on a hand-written `openapi.yaml`, `flask-swagger-ui`, or `pyyaml` for documentation (Decision E).
11. THE Backend SHALL document in the OpenAPI document and in `backend/API.md` the avatar routes under `/api/users` (`POST` and `DELETE /api/users/me/avatar`, `GET /api/users/{user_id}/avatar/file`), the `avatar_url`, `author_avatar_url`, and `assignee_avatar_url` fields of the Comment, member, candidate, and Card schemas, the `search` minimum length of the candidates endpoint, and the 429 response of `POST /api/auth/change-password` (Decisions U and W; V10 round 3).

### Requirement 18: Board Share Links

**User Story:** As a Board_Owner, Manager, or Admin, I want to share a read-only view of a Board through a link that I can revoke, so that people without an account can see the work without seeing the discussion.

Note (V12, Decisions Y and Z): the Share_Link is now the Public_Link (Requirement 23); only a Manager or an Admin manages it, and it is read-only for every visitor. Amended criteria carry a label below.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [SHARE-01] THE Backend SHALL store a nullable, unique `share_token` on each Board, where null means sharing is off (Decision K).
2. [SHARE-02] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/share` while sharing is off, THE Backend SHALL generate a Share_Token of at least 256 bits from a cryptographically secure source, store it, write one `board.share_enabled` Activity_Log row in the same transaction, and return `{ "enabled": true, "token": string, "path": "/shared/<token>" }` (Decision K). **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 1: Manager and Admin only.)**
3. [SHARE-03] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/share` while sharing is on, THE Backend SHALL return the existing Share_Token unchanged and write no Activity_Log row. **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 1: Manager and Admin only.)**
4. [SHARE-04] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/share/regenerate`, THE Backend SHALL replace the Share_Token with a new one, write one `board.share_regenerated` Activity_Log row, and make the previous Share_Link stop working at once (Decision K). **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 1: Manager and Admin only.)**
5. [SHARE-05] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/share`, THE Backend SHALL set `share_token` to null, write one `board.share_disabled` Activity_Log row when sharing was on, and make the previous Share_Link stop working at once; WHEN sharing is already off, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 200 and write no row (Decision K). **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 1: Manager and Admin only.)**
6. [SHARE-06] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `GET /api/boards/{id}/share`, THE Backend SHALL return `{ "enabled": boolean, "token": string or null, "path": string or null }`. **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 1: Manager and Admin only.)**
7. [SHARE-07] IF any other signed-in user, including a Supervisor who is only a BoardMember, calls an endpoint of criteria 2 to 6, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present. **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 2: the Board_Owner gets 403.)**
8. [SHARE-08] THE Backend SHALL keep the Share_Token out of every Activity_Log `metadata`, every log line, and every Board response other than the responses of criteria 2 to 6.
9. [SHARE-09] WHEN a visitor calls `GET /api/shared/{token}` with the Share_Token of a non-deleted Board, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 200 without requiring a Token, with the Board's `id`, `name`, `description`, its Columns, and `has_board_access`, and without `owner_id`, members, `share_token`, `comments`, `activity`, or `activity_log` (Decision K).
10. [SHARE-10] IF a Share_Token is unknown, was disabled or replaced, or belongs to a soft-deleted Board, THEN every shared route SHALL return the same HTTP 404 `NOT_FOUND` response.
11. [SHARE-11] WHEN a visitor calls `GET /api/shared/{token}/cards`, THE Backend SHALL return the Cards of that Board that `GET /api/boards/{id}/cards` returns, each with its Subtasks, Attachments, and the assignee's `full_name` as `assignee_name` (plus the opaque `assignee_id` that the Card list already carries, but never the assignee's Username, Email, or Avatar_URL), and SHALL accept `search` and `filter[priority|assignee|category]` as Requirement 12 defines; the shared filter row of the Frontend offers search and Category chips only (Decision W, resolving Open Question 13).
12. [SHARE-12] WHEN a visitor calls `GET /api/shared/{token}/categories`, THE Backend SHALL return the Categories of that Board only.
13. [SHARE-13] WHEN a visitor calls `GET /api/shared/{token}/cards/{card_id}`, THE Backend SHALL return the Card with its Subtasks and Attachments, and SHALL return HTTP 404 when the Card does not belong to the Board of that Share_Token or is soft-deleted.
14. [SHARE-14] WHEN a visitor calls `GET /api/shared/{token}/attachments/{id}/url` for an Attachment of a Card of that Board, THE Backend SHALL return a Signed_File_URL scoped to that Share_Token, and SHALL return HTTP 404 for any other Attachment.
15. [SHARE-15] THE Backend SHALL expose no Comment endpoint, no Activity endpoint, and no mutating method under `/api/shared/`, so that a Guest never receives Comments or Activity_Log data through a Share_Link, whether the Board is public or private (Decision K). (V12: every Board is private; the rule holds for all.)
16. [SHARE-16] WHEN a Signed_File_URL scoped to a Share_Token is presented to the file route, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 unless the signature is valid, unexpired, and the Board's current Share_Token still matches the scope, so that disabling or regenerating a Share_Link invalidates outstanding file URLs at once.
17. [SHARE-17] WHERE a Board is `private`, THE Backend SHALL allow the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin to enable a Share_Link, and SHALL keep returning HTTP 403 to a signed-in user without Board_Access on every non-shared route of that Board (Requirement 11, criterion 6). **(Obsolete in V12: no Board is public; a Manager or an Admin may enable a Public_Link on any Board.)**
18. [SHARE-18] THE Backend SHALL apply no expiry date to a Share_Token in V10.
19. [SHARE-19] THE Backend SHALL send `Cache-Control: private, no-store` on every shared route response, and THE Frontend SHALL send `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` and `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` on every `/shared/{token}` page.
20. [SHARE-20] WHEN a signed-in user opens `/shared/{token}` and `has_board_access` is true, THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/boards/{id}`; otherwise THE Frontend SHALL show the Shared_View (Decision K).
21. [SHARE-21] THE Frontend SHALL render the Shared_View without drag-and-drop, add-card, edit, assign, Comments, Activity, Inbox, Sidebar, or App_Header User_Menu, with a "Shared view, read only" banner and a Sign in link, SHALL open a Card as a read-only modal with no footer actions, and SHALL open Attachments through `target="_blank"` and `rel="noopener noreferrer"`, requesting a fresh URL from `GET /api/shared/{token}/attachments/{id}/url` when a Signed_File_URL has expired. (V12: no write control for any visitor, Requirement 23, criterion 7.)
22. [SHARE-22] WHERE the viewer is the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin, THE Frontend SHALL show a Share button in the Board's App_Header that opens a popover with the current state, an enable toggle, the full link with a Copy button, a Regenerate action, and a Disable action; Regenerate and Disable SHALL ask for confirmation through `BaseConfirm` and SHALL say that the old link stops working. **(Amended in V12 by Requirement 23, criterion 9: the button is "Public link" and is shown to a Manager and an Admin only.)**
23. [SHARE-23] IF the shared route answers HTTP 404, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show a page that says the link is not valid and offers a Sign in link, without saying whether the link was disabled or never existed.
24. [SHARE-24] IF a request to a shared route carries a malformed, expired, or invalid Bearer Token, THEN THE Backend SHALL ignore the Token and answer as for a request with no Token (never 401), with `has_board_access` false, so that a stale Session_Cookie cannot break a Share_Link (Decision T).
25. [SHARE-25] THE Backend SHALL carry in no shared route response any `avatar_url`, `author_avatar_url`, `assignee_avatar_url`, Username, or Email of a user, so that the Shared_View shows an assignee by `assignee_name` and an Initials_Avatar only (Decision U, Decision W).

### Requirement 19: App Header, User Menu, and Profile

**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want one header on every page with my account menu, so that I can reach my profile and sign out from the same place.

Note (V12, Decision AF): the Profile_Page layout is amended by Requirement 29; the header Share button and visibility icon of V10 become the "Public link" and "Invite" buttons (Requirements 23 and 24).

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [HDR-01] THE Frontend SHALL render the App_Header on every Protected_Route page, fixed at the top, so that the same header serves Home, a Board, the Categories and member screens, the Profile_Page, and the admin screens (Decision L). (V16: the header content is Requirement 39, SHELL-09.)
2. [HDR-02] THE Frontend SHALL show in the App_Header a User_Menu_Trigger (a user icon, or the avatar when `avatar_url` is set) with the accessible name "User menu", and WHEN the user activates it, THE Frontend SHALL open a menu whose items are, in order, Profile and then Log out (Decision L).
3. [HDR-03] WHEN the user activates Profile, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to `/profile` and close the menu.
4. [HDR-04] WHEN the user activates Log out, THE Frontend SHALL call `POST /api/auth/logout`, discard the Token and the Session_Cookie whether the call succeeds or fails, and navigate to `/login` (criteria 15 and 16 of Requirement 1).
5. [HDR-05] THE Frontend SHALL show no Log out item and no profile entry in the Sidebar (Decision L).
6. [HDR-06] THE Frontend SHALL build the User_Menu with `BaseMenu`, so that it opens and closes with the keyboard, closes on Escape and returns focus to the trigger, and shows the focus ring of Requirement 14, criterion 15.
7. [HDR-07] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL keep the User_Menu_Trigger in the persistent header (Requirement 14, criterion 8).
8. [PROF-01] WHEN a signed-in user opens `/profile`, THE Frontend SHALL show the full name as an editable field, the avatar section of criterion 28, the Username and the Email as read-only text, and a change-password form with Current password and New password fields, loading them from `GET /api/auth/me`.  (V17: superseded by PRF-01 to PRF-04, Requirement 54.)
9. [PROF-02] WHEN a signed-in user calls `PATCH /api/auth/me` with `full_name`, THE Backend SHALL apply the change and return the `user` shape of Requirement 1, criterion 1.
10. [PROF-03] THE Backend SHALL accept `full_name` only when it is non-blank after trimming and at most 255 characters; the avatar changes only through criteria 18 to 27 (Decision R, which withdraws the `https` URL rule of V10); otherwise THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for the field.
11. [PROF-04] IF a `PATCH /api/auth/me` body contains `username`, `email`, `role`, `avatar_url`, or any other field the endpoint does not define, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and change nothing (Decision V: `email` is no longer editable here).  (V17: superseded by PRF-09, Requirement 54.)
12. [PROF-05] WHEN a signed-in user calls `POST /api/auth/change-password` with `current_password` and `new_password` of at least 8 characters and the user is not blocked by criteria 19 to 23 of Requirement 20, THE Backend SHALL verify the current password, store the new hash, return HTTP 200 with the message "Password changed", and leave the issued Token valid until its expiry (Requirement 1, criterion 14).
13. [PROF-06] [Withdrawn in V10 round 3, Decision V] No account has a null Email, so there is no Email_Prompt and `PATCH /api/auth/me` does not accept an Email. The ID stays so that references do not move.
14. [PROF-07] [Withdrawn in V10 round 3, Decision V] The Email is no longer saved from the Profile_Page, so the `CONFLICT` on an Email save no longer exists there. The ID stays so that references do not move.
15. [PROF-08] WHEN a profile change or a password change succeeds, THE Backend SHALL write one Activity_Log row (`user.profile_updated` or `user.password_changed`, target type `user`) in the same transaction, with no password, hash, or Token in `metadata`.
16. [PROF-09] IF `current_password` does not match the stored password, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `current_password`, count one failed attempt for the user in the Rate_Limiter (Requirement 20, criterion 20), and never return HTTP 401, because a 401 makes the Frontend discard the session (Requirement 13, criterion 10); IF the user is blocked, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 `RATE_LIMITED` (Requirement 20, criterion 19) and the Frontend SHALL keep the session.
17. [PROF-10] THE Frontend SHALL validate `new_password` for at least 8 characters and `current_password` for a non-empty value before sending, and SHALL map `error.details` onto the matching fields.

**Avatar upload (V10 round 2, Decision R)**

18. [AVT-01] WHEN a signed-in user calls `POST /api/users/me/avatar` as `multipart/form-data` with one file part named `file`, THE Backend SHALL store the image as that user's Avatar_Image and return HTTP 200 with message "Avatar updated" and the `user` shape of Requirement 1, criterion 1.
19. [AVT-02] THE Backend SHALL accept an upload only when its leading bytes identify PNG (`89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A`), JPEG (`FF D8 FF`), or WebP (`RIFF`, four size bytes, then `WEBP`), and SHALL ignore the file extension and the client's Content-Type for that decision; otherwise THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `file`.
20. [AVT-03] IF an upload is empty or larger than 2,097,152 bytes, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `file`, SHALL read at most 2,097,153 bytes of the part, and SHALL store nothing.
21. [AVT-04] THE Backend SHALL store the Avatar_Image as `UPLOAD_DIR/avatars/<32 hexadecimal characters>.<png|jpg|webp>`, where the name is random and the extension comes from the detected type, and SHALL NOT use, store, or log the original file name, and SHALL record only that generated name in `User.avatar_path`.
22. [AVT-05] WHEN an upload replaces an existing Avatar_Image, THE Backend SHALL delete the previous file after the database change commits; IF that deletion fails, THEN THE Backend SHALL log the failure without the user's data and still return success; IF the database change fails, THEN THE Backend SHALL delete the file it just wrote.
23. [AVT-06] WHEN a signed-in user calls `DELETE /api/users/me/avatar`, THE Backend SHALL set `avatar_path` to null, delete the file after the commit, and return HTTP 200 with message "Avatar removed" and the `user` shape with `avatar_url` null; WHEN the user has no Avatar_Image, THE Backend SHALL return the same response and change nothing.
24. [AVT-07] THE Backend SHALL return `avatar_url` in every `user` payload (register, login, `GET` and `PATCH /api/auth/me`, and the Admin user endpoints) as a Signed_File_URL `/api/users/{user_id}/avatar/file?exp=<unix>&sig=<hex>` valid for 15 minutes, produced by the signing function of Decision D with the scope `avatar` so that an avatar signature never opens an Attachment file, or as null when the user has no Avatar_Image; THE Backend SHALL create the URL only while building a response to a request that has authenticated.
25. [AVT-08] WHEN a request reaches `GET /api/users/{user_id}/avatar/file` with a valid, unexpired signature for that user, THE Backend SHALL return the stored bytes with the Content-Type of the detected type, `Content-Disposition: inline`, `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, and `Cache-Control: private, max-age=300`; IF the signature is missing, modified, or expired, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 with the error Envelope; IF the user has no Avatar_Image, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 404. The route takes no Token, because an `<img>` request cannot send one (the trade-off of Decision D).
26. [AVT-09] THE Backend SHALL include an Avatar_URL only in the `user` shapes of criterion 24 and in the payloads of criterion 34 (Comment and Reply authors, Board members, candidates, Card assignees), and in no other payload: Notifications, Activity_Log rows, and every shared route carry none, and a Guest on a Share_Link sees initials only (Decision U, resolving Open Question 15).
27. [AVT-10] IF a `PATCH /api/auth/me` body or an Admin `POST /api/users` or `PATCH /api/users/{id}` body contains `avatar_url`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and change nothing (Decision R; breaking for the Admin endpoints).
28. [AVT-11] THE Frontend SHALL show on the Profile_Page an avatar section with the current Avatar_Image or the Initials_Avatar, a "Choose image" button that opens a file picker limited to PNG, JPEG, and WebP, a preview of the chosen file before it is sent, an Upload button (primary) and a Cancel button (secondary) while a file is chosen, and, only while an Avatar_Image exists, a Remove button (danger) that asks for confirmation through `BaseConfirm`.
29. [AVT-12] IF the chosen file is not PNG, JPEG, or WebP by its declared type, or is larger than 2 MB, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show "Use a PNG, JPEG, or WebP image." or "The image must be 2 MB or smaller." beside the picker and send no request; WHEN the Backend returns an `error.details.file` message, THE Frontend SHALL show it there, and on a network failure THE Frontend SHALL show a retry message and keep the chosen file.
30. [AVT-13] WHILE `avatar_url` is null, THE Frontend SHALL show the Initials_Avatar; IF an avatar image fails to load, THEN THE Frontend SHALL request `GET /api/auth/me` once for a fresh URL, retry once, and then show the Initials_Avatar.
31. [AVT-14] WHEN an upload or a removal succeeds, THE Frontend SHALL update the avatar in the Profile_Page, the User_Menu_Trigger, and the stored user without a page reload, and SHALL show a success message.
32. [AVT-15] WHEN an upload or a removal succeeds, THE Backend SHALL write one Activity_Log row `user.profile_updated` with target type `user` and `metadata` `{ "fields": ["avatar"] }` in the same transaction, with no file name, path, or URL in `metadata` (criterion 15).
33. [AVT-16] IF `POST` or `DELETE /api/users/me/avatar` carries no Token, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401; WHEN two uploads for the same user run at the same time, THE Backend SHALL serialize them on the user row so that exactly one Avatar_Image file remains for that user.

**Avatars in viewer-facing payloads (V10 round 3, Decision U)**

34. [AVT-17] THE Backend SHALL add an Avatar_URL, or null when the user has no Avatar_Image, as `author_avatar_url` on every Comment and Reply, as `avatar_url` on every row of `GET /api/boards/{id}/members` (with or without `include=email`) and of `GET /api/boards/{id}/candidates`, and as `assignee_avatar_url`, together with `assignee_name`, on every Card returned by `GET /api/boards/{id}/cards`, `GET /api/cards/{id}`, and the Card create, update, move, and assign responses (null for both when the Card has no assignee).
35. [AVT-18] THE Backend SHALL build each of those URLs with the signing of criterion 24 (scope `avatar`, 15 minutes), only while building a response to a request that has authenticated, and SHALL add no Email to any of those payloads beyond the places Decision S allows.
36. [AVT-19] THE Backend SHALL read the author and assignee names and avatar paths in the existing batched lookups (one query for all authors or assignees of a response), declare no new model relationship, trigger no lazy load, and sign each distinct user once per response, so that the query count does not depend on the number of distinct authors or assignees (Requirement 14, criterion 12; Decisions F and U).
37. [AVT-20] THE Backend SHALL leave every shared route without an Avatar_URL and THE Frontend SHALL show the Initials_Avatar for the assignee in the Shared_View, because a share-scoped avatar URL needs its own signature scope, a new shared field, and a file-route branch for a status-only view (Decisions K and U).
38. [AVT-21] THE Frontend SHALL draw every user avatar through `BaseAvatar`, showing the Avatar_Image when the URL is set and the Initials_Avatar otherwise, for the author of each Comment and Reply, each Team members row of the Sidebar, each member search result, each row of the assignee popover, the assignee on a board Card, the assignee chip of the Card modal, and the member avatars of a Home tile (Decision U). (V12: no visibility icon on Home tiles, Requirement 22, criterion 14.)
39. [AVT-22] THE Frontend SHALL compute the Initials_Avatar from `full_name` as the first letter of each of the first two words, upper-cased, one letter for a one-word name, and `?` for a blank name, and SHALL show it when the URL is null or the image fails after the one refresh of criterion 30 (Decision U).
40. [AVT-23] THE Frontend SHALL show no Email in any avatar tooltip, `alt` text, or label; `BaseAvatar` takes the full name as its tooltip and `alt` text (Decisions S and U).

### Requirement 20: Rate Limiting

**User Story:** As a system owner, I want repeated failed sign-ins, repeated wrong current passwords, and bulk availability checks to be blocked for a while, so that passwords cannot be guessed and the account list is harder to probe.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [RL-01] IF the Rate_Limiter has counted 5 failed login attempts for the same pair of lower-cased Identifier and Client_IP within the Rate_Limit_Window, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 with the code `RATE_LIMITED` for every further login attempt of that pair until fewer than 5 failures remain in the window (Decision O).
2. [RL-02] IF the Rate_Limiter has counted 20 failed login attempts from the same Client_IP within the Rate_Limit_Window across all Identifiers, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 with the code `RATE_LIMITED` for every further login attempt from that Client_IP until fewer than 20 failures remain.
3. [RL-03] WHEN a login succeeds, THE Backend SHALL delete the failed-attempt rows of that pair and SHALL leave the per-IP count unchanged.
4. [RL-04] THE Backend SHALL count one failed attempt for each login that ends in the generic HTTP 401, whether the Identifier is unknown or the password is wrong, and SHALL count no attempt for a login that ends in HTTP 400, or in HTTP 429.
5. [RL-05] WHEN the Rate_Limiter blocks a request, THE Backend SHALL return `{ "status": false, "message": string, "error": { "code": "RATE_LIMITED", "details": { "retry_after": integer } } }` with HTTP status 429 and a `Retry-After` header holding the same integer number of seconds (Decision O).
6. [RL-06] THE Backend SHALL compute `retry_after` as the number of whole seconds, rounded up and at least 1, until the oldest counted attempt that keeps the pair or the Client_IP at its limit leaves the Rate_Limit_Window.
7. [RL-07] [Withdrawn in V10 round 2, Decision Q] Registration is not rate limited; criterion 18 states the rule. The ID stays so that references do not move.
8. [RL-08] IF a Client_IP makes more than `RATE_LIMIT_AVAILABILITY_MAX` availability requests within the Rate_Limit_Window, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 with the code `RATE_LIMITED` for each further availability request; every availability request counts (kept by the specifier and reversible, Open Question 14).
9. [RL-09] THE Backend SHALL keep the attempt counts in the Postgres table `rate_limit_attempts`, SHALL serialize the check and the insert for one key with a transaction-scoped advisory lock, and SHALL commit a failed-attempt row before it returns the 401, so that counts hold across worker processes and survive the rollback that follows an error. Rate_Limiter rows are operational records, not state changes of the Board domain, so they write no Activity_Log row.
10. [RL-10] THE Backend SHALL read `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS` (default 900), `RATE_LIMIT_LOGIN_PAIR_MAX` (default 5), `RATE_LIMIT_LOGIN_IP_MAX` (default 20), `RATE_LIMIT_AVAILABILITY_MAX` (default 60), and `RATE_LIMIT_CHANGE_PASSWORD_MAX` (default 5) from the environment, SHALL list each in `backend/.env.example` with a placeholder, and SHALL accept the same settings as arguments of the app factory, so that tests use small limits without setting `APP_ENV`.
11. [RL-11] THE Backend SHALL take the Client_IP from the connection's remote address, and SHALL read `X-Forwarded-For` only through the number of trusted proxies set in `TRUSTED_PROXY_COUNT` (default 0).
12. [RL-12] THE Backend SHALL store in `rate_limit_attempts` only a keyed hash of the Identifier-and-IP pair, of the IP, or of the user id (scope `change_password`), and never the plaintext Identifier or an Email.
13. [RL-13] WHEN the Rate_Limiter records an attempt, THE Backend SHALL delete the rows of that scope that are older than the Rate_Limit_Window.
14. [RL-14] WHILE a pair is blocked, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 without comparing the password, so that a correct password does not unblock the pair.
15. [RL-15] WHEN a login response is HTTP 429, THE Frontend SHALL show the wait time as a countdown in minutes and seconds beside the form message, disable the submit button until the countdown ends, and then enable it and clear the message; THE Frontend SHALL read the wait from `error.details.retry_after`.
16. [RL-16] WHEN the availability check is blocked, THE Frontend SHALL stop on-blur checks until the wait ends and SHALL show no error for the field, because the 409 on submit stays authoritative (Requirement 1, criterion 50).
17. [RL-17] THE Backend SHALL list `Retry-After` in the CORS exposed headers.
18. [RL-18] THE Backend SHALL NOT rate limit `POST /api/auth/register` and SHALL keep no `register` scope in `rate_limit_attempts`, so that a registration request is answered only with 201, 400, or 409 (Decision Q; registration rate limiting deferred).
19. [RL-19] IF a signed-in user has had `RATE_LIMIT_CHANGE_PASSWORD_MAX` failed change-password attempts within the Rate_Limit_Window, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 with the code `RATE_LIMITED` for every further `POST /api/auth/change-password` call of that user until fewer failures remain; the counter is keyed by the user id and not by the Client_IP (Decision W, resolving Open Question 9).
20. [RL-20] THE Backend SHALL count one failed attempt for each change-password call that ends in HTTP 400 with `details.current_password`, and SHALL count no attempt for a call that ends in any other 400, in 401, or in 429; WHEN a change-password call succeeds, THE Backend SHALL delete that user's failed-attempt rows of the scope.
21. [RL-21] WHILE a user is blocked by criterion 19, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 429 without comparing the current password, with the body, the `Retry-After` header, and the `retry_after` value of criteria 5 and 6.
22. [RL-22] WHEN a change-password response is HTTP 429, THE Frontend SHALL show the wait time as a countdown beside the form message, disable the Change password button until the countdown ends, and keep the session, because a 429 is not a 401.
23. [RL-23] THE Backend SHALL keep a `change_password` value in the scope column of `rate_limit_attempts` and SHALL purge the rows of that scope older than the Rate_Limit_Window when it records an attempt, as criterion 13 states for the other scopes.

### Requirement 21: Branding (Logo and Favicon)

**User Story:** As the product owner, I want the RevoTask logo and favicon on every page where the application is named, so that the product looks the same in the browser tab, the Sidebar, the sign-in pages, and the header.

#### Acceptance Criteria

New in V11 (Decision X). No API, data model, or permission changes.

1. [BRAND-01] THE Frontend SHALL serve the Favicon_File from `frontend/app/favicon.ico` through the Next.js file convention, so that `GET /favicon.ico` returns HTTP 200 with a content type of `image/x-icon` or `image/vnd.microsoft.icon`.
2. [BRAND-02] THE Frontend SHALL leave the `<link rel="icon">` to Next.js, so that the rendered `<head>` of every page contains exactly one `link` element with `rel="icon"` and `href="/favicon.ico"`, and `app/layout.tsx` contains no manual icon `<link>` and no `metadata.icons` entry.
3. [BRAND-03] THE Frontend SHALL keep Logo_Mark at `frontend/public/brand/logo-revo.svg` and Logo_Full at `frontend/public/brand/logo-text-revo.svg`, and SHALL refer to them only by the absolute paths `/brand/logo-revo.svg` and `/brand/logo-text-revo.svg`, written only in `BrandLogo`.
4. [BRAND-04] THE Frontend SHALL render every logo through `BrandLogo` in `components/shared/` with the variants `mark` (Logo_Mark) and `full` (Logo_Full), and feature components, pages, and layouts SHALL import `BrandLogo` and SHALL contain no raw `<img>` element and no other `next/image` use for the logo.
5. [BRAND-05] THE BrandLogo SHALL render with `next/image` and the `unoptimized` prop, because the Next.js image optimizer does not process SVG and rejects it unless `dangerouslyAllowSVG` is enabled; THE Frontend SHALL leave `dangerouslyAllowSVG` unset and SHALL add no `images` entry to `next.config.ts`.
6. [BRAND-06] THE BrandLogo SHALL pass `width` and `height` equal to the `viewBox` of the file (480 by 480 for `mark`, 540 by 165 for `full`) so that the browser reserves the aspect ratio before the file loads, SHALL set the displayed size with token-backed size classes (Requirement 14, criterion 9), and SHALL display `mark` at no more than 480 CSS pixels wide and `full` at no more than 540 CSS pixels wide, so that the Embedded_Raster is never enlarged.
7. [BRAND-07] THE BrandLogo SHALL give the image the alt text `RevoTask`, and WHERE the logo sits inside a control that has its own accessible name, or next to visible text that reads RevoTask, THE BrandLogo SHALL render `alt=""` through a `decorative` property.
8. [BRAND-08] WHILE the viewport is 960 pixels or wider, THE Frontend SHALL show in the Sidebar toggle `BrandLogo` with the variant `full` when the Sidebar is expanded, and the variant `mark` with the tooltip `RevoTask` when it is collapsed; THE toggle SHALL stay a button named "Collapse sidebar" or "Expand sidebar" with `aria-expanded`, THE Sidebar SHALL drop the separate text "RevoTask" because Logo_Full contains the wordmark, and below 960 pixels THE off-canvas drawer SHALL show `full` at the top without a toggle button. (V16: the full logo is in the App_Header and the Icon_Rail has no logo, Requirement 39, SHELL-09 and SHELL-10.)
9. [BRAND-09] THE Frontend SHALL show `BrandLogo` on the login and register pages in the brand panel (variant `full`, replacing the placeholder tile and the small text) and in the form card header below 960 pixels (variant `full`), and WHILE a logo is shown on the brand panel gradient, THE Frontend SHALL place it on a `bg-surface-raised` tile with a radius token, because the Embedded_Raster has an opaque background (Open Question 21).
10. [BRAND-10] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels and the page is not a Board, THE App_Header SHALL show the link to `/boards` as `BrandLogo` (variant `mark`, `decorative`) followed by the visible text RevoTask, so that the link name stays "RevoTask".
11. [BRAND-11] (Superseded in V13 by Requirement 32: titles are `RevoTask | <name>`, no suffix.) THE Frontend SHALL keep the title `RevoTask` in the root `metadata` and the suffix "· RevoTask" in the page titles, and SHALL add no `app/icon`, `app/apple-icon`, or `app/manifest` in V11, because the owner supplied only `favicon.ico` (Open Question 20).
12. [BRAND-12] THE BrandLogo and the elements around it SHALL use only token-backed utilities (Requirement 14, criterion 9), with no hex colour, no Tailwind default palette colour, and no arbitrary value.
13. [BRAND-13] THE Frontend SHALL load the favicon and every logo from its own origin, so that a page makes no request to a third-party host for them.
14. [BRAND-14] WHEN the Brand_Assets are moved, THE implementation SHALL use a plain move (the three files are untracked, so `git mv` does not apply), SHALL leave no copy of any Brand_Assets file in `frontend/images/` or elsewhere, and SHALL remove the empty `frontend/images/` directory.
15. [BRAND-15] THE BrandLogo SHALL be a Server Component without state or event handlers (Decision H and the Server Component rule), SHALL pass `loading="eager"` because every logo sits above the fold, and SHALL NOT use the deprecated `priority` property.
16. [BRAND-16] THE System SHALL treat the Brand_Assets files as replaceable in place: a later true-vector SVG with the same file name and `viewBox` replaces the Embedded_Raster file with no change to Frontend code.
17. [BRAND-17] THE System SHALL verify the branding with these checks: (a) Playwright: `GET /favicon.ico` returns 200 with an icon content type, and the page `<head>` has the one icon link; (b) Playwright: on `/login` at 1280 pixels and at 360 pixels the visible logo image has `alt` `RevoTask` (or `alt=""` beside the text RevoTask) and a `naturalWidth` above 0 after load; (c) Playwright: no request in the page's network log goes to a host other than the application's own origin and the API origin; (d) `vitest`: `BrandLogo` renders `mark` and `full` with the right `src`, `width`, `height`, alt text, and `decorative` behaviour; (e) `vitest`: the Sidebar shows `full` when expanded and `mark` with the tooltip RevoTask when collapsed; (f) `vitest`: no component or page except `BrandLogo` contains the strings `/brand/` or `<img`, and `BrandLogo` contains no hex colour; (g) `vitest`: `frontend/images/` does not exist, or exists with no files; (h) no `waitForTimeout` in the new Playwright tests (Requirement 15, criterion 3).

### Requirement 22: Private-Only Board Access Model

**User Story:** As a Manager, I want every Board to be private and open only to its owner, its members, and staff, so that unrelated signed-in users never see work they were not invited to.

Note (V12, Decision Y): this requirement reverses Decision A (public Boards readable by every signed-in user), the Board `visibility` column, and Requirement 11 criteria 1 to 3, 6, 11, 12, and 13. Those criteria stay in this document with a "Superseded in V12" label so the history is readable. The local development database is reset by the V12 migration; nothing is backfilled.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [ACC-01] THE Backend SHALL treat every Board as private: THE Board table SHALL have no `visibility` column, no request body or query string SHALL accept a `visibility` field, no response SHALL contain one, and no Activity_Log row SHALL be written with the action `board.visibility_changed` (Decision Y).
2. [ACC-02] THE Backend SHALL grant Board_Access to a signed-in user on a non-soft-deleted Board exactly when the user is the Board_Owner, a BoardMember, a Manager, or an Admin (Decision Y).
3. [ACC-03] IF a signed-in user without Board_Access calls any Board-scoped route other than the routes under `/api/shared` and `/api/invite`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 with an error Envelope, and IF no Token is present, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401.
4. [ACC-04] WHEN a signed-in Member or Supervisor requests `GET /api/boards`, THE Backend SHALL return the non-soft-deleted Boards where the user is the Board_Owner or a BoardMember; WHEN a Manager or an Admin requests it, THE Backend SHALL return every non-soft-deleted Board (Decision Y).
5. [ACC-05] THE Backend SHALL never store a BoardMember row for a user whose role is `manager` or `admin`; IF `POST /api/boards/{id}/members` names such a user, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and `error.details.user_id` (Decision Y).
6. [ACC-06] WHEN an Admin changes a user's role to `manager` or `admin`, THE Backend SHALL delete that user's BoardMember rows and pending BoardJoinRequests in the same transaction and write one `board.member_removed` Activity_Log row per removed BoardMember (Decision Y).
7. [ACC-07] WHEN a Manager or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/members` for a Member or Supervisor account that is not yet a BoardMember, THE Backend SHALL create the BoardMember, write one `board.member_added` Activity_Log row, and create one `board_added` Notification for that user in the same transaction (Decision Y; amends Requirement 3, criterion 11).
8. [ACC-08] IF a signed-in user whose role is `member` or `supervisor`, the Board_Owner included, calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/members` or `GET /api/boards/{id}/candidates`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision Y; amends Requirement 3, criteria 12 and 25).
9. [ACC-09] WHEN a Manager or an Admin requests `GET /api/boards/{id}/candidates?search=<text>` with at least 2 characters after trimming, THE Backend SHALL return only users whose role is `member` or `supervisor`, who are neither the Board_Owner nor a BoardMember, under the V10 matching, ordering, 20-row limit, and fields (Requirement 3, criterion 25).
10. [ACC-10] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/members/{user_id}`, THE Backend SHALL delete the BoardMember, clear `assignee_id` on every Card of that Board assigned to that user, write one `card.unassigned` Activity_Log row per cleared Card, and create no Notification, all in one transaction (Requirement 3, criterion 14, and Decision Y).
11. [ACC-11] WHEN a Card is assigned through `PATCH /api/cards/{id}` with a non-null `assignee_id`, THE Backend SHALL accept the value only when the user is the Board_Owner or a BoardMember of that Card's Board, and SHALL otherwise return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and `error.details.assignee_id` (Decision Y; amends Requirement 4, criterion 11).
12. [ACC-12] WHEN a Manager or an Admin reassigns a Board's `owner_id` to a Supervisor, THE Backend SHALL create one `board_added` Notification for the new owner in the same transaction, and SHALL clear `assignee_id` on Cards of that Board assigned to the previous owner unless the previous owner is a BoardMember, writing one `card.unassigned` row per cleared Card (Decision Y; amends Requirement 3, criterion 16).
13. [ACC-13] THE Backend SHALL return from `GET /api/boards/{id}/members` the Board_Owner (first, with `is_owner` true) and every BoardMember, each with `id`, `full_name`, `username`, `avatar_url`, and `is_owner`, listing a user once even when the Board_Owner is also a BoardMember, so that the assignee picker lists exactly the users who may be assigned (Decision Y; Open Question 23).
14. [ACC-14] THE Frontend SHALL render no visibility icon, no lock icon, no visibility toggle, and no visibility filter in the Sidebar Boards list, the Board header, the Home tiles, the new-board modal, or any Board edit form (Decision Y; amends Requirement 3, criteria 20, 22, 23, and 29).
15. [ACC-15] WHEN a signed-in Member or Supervisor has no Board, THE Frontend SHALL show on Home the message "You are not on any board yet. Ask a manager or a board owner for an invite link." and no list of other Boards (Decision Y).
16. [ACC-16] WHERE the viewer is a Manager or an Admin, THE Frontend SHALL show the add button in the Sidebar Team members heading, which opens the member search of Requirement 3, criteria 24 and 32, and SHALL show no add button to the Board_Owner or any other viewer (Decision Y; amends Requirement 3, criterion 24).
17. [ACC-17] WHEN the V12 migration runs and `APP_ENV` is `development` or `testing`, THE Backend SHALL drop `boards.visibility`, add `boards.invite_token`, create `board_join_requests`, alter `notifications` as Requirement 28 criterion 1 states, delete the rows of every user-referencing table as the V10 migration did, and print one line saying that local data was reset and that `flask seed-data` recreates it; IF `APP_ENV` has any other value, THEN THE migration SHALL abort before changing anything (Decision Y). (V14, amended by Decision AN: outside development and testing the migration may run on an empty `users` table and never truncates; Requirement 37.)
18. [ACC-18] WHEN `flask seed-data` runs, THE Backend SHALL create a Manager, an Admin, a Supervisor who owns a seeded Board, a second Supervisor, the Members `member` and `member2`, two seeded Boards (one with `member` and `member2` as BoardMembers, one without any Member), one seeded Card assigned to `member` with Subtasks, and no BoardMember row for the Manager or the Admin; running it twice SHALL create no duplicate (Decision Y; extends Requirement 1, criterion 64).
19. [ACC-19] THE Frontend SHALL list the Board_Owner first in the Sidebar Team members section, tagged "Owner", with the Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar, the full name, and the Username and no Email, and SHALL show no remove button on that row to any viewer; WHERE the Board has no owner, THE Frontend SHALL show no owner row (Open Question 23; amends Requirement 3, criterion 24).
20.
