[ACC-20] IF `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/members/{user_id}` names the Board_Owner and the Board_Owner is not a BoardMember, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 404 with the code `NOT_FOUND` and change nothing (Open Question 22; the owner row of ACC-19 has no remove action, so the Frontend never sends this request for it).

### Requirement 23: Public Link

**User Story:** As a Manager or Admin, I want to publish a read-only view of a Board through a link that I can revoke, so that anyone holding the link can see the work without gaining access to the discussion or to any edit action.

Note (V12, Decision Z): the V10 Share_Link is renamed Public_Link. The stored column stays `Board.share_token`, the Share_Token keeps its name in code, and the routes stay `/api/boards/{id}/share...`, `/api/shared/{token}...`, and the page `/shared/{token}`, so that no URL, column, or Frontend route changes; only the roles and the wording change. Requirement 18 criteria 2 to 7, 17, 21, and 22 are amended below; the other criteria of Requirement 18 stand.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [PUB-01] WHEN a Manager or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/share`, `POST /api/boards/{id}/share/regenerate`, `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/share`, or `GET /api/boards/{id}/share`, THE Backend SHALL apply the behaviour of Requirement 18, criteria 2 to 6, to the Board (Decision Z; amends Requirement 18, criteria 2 to 6).
2. [PUB-02] IF a signed-in user who is not a Manager or an Admin, the Board_Owner and a Supervisor BoardMember included, calls an endpoint of criterion 1, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present (Decision Z; amends Requirement 18, criterion 7).
3. [PUB-03] WHEN any visitor, a Guest or a signed-in user with or without Board_Access, opens a Public_Link whose Share_Token is current, THE Backend SHALL answer the shared routes of Requirement 18, criteria 9 to 14, with HTTP 200 and read-only data, and SHALL return `has_board_access` true only when the signed-in caller has Board_Access (Decision Z).
4. [PUB-04] WHEN a signed-in user with Board_Access opens `/shared/{token}`, THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/boards/{id}`; WHEN any other visitor opens it, THE Frontend SHALL show the Shared_View, a signed-in user without Board_Access included (Decision Z; Requirement 18, criterion 20).
5. [PUB-05] THE Backend SHALL NOT return Comments, the Activity_Log, `owner_id`, the member list, `invite_token`, `share_token`, any Email, Username, or Avatar_URL from any route under `/api/shared`, whoever calls it (Requirement 18, criteria 9, 15, and 25).
6. [PUB-06] WHEN a Manager or an Admin regenerates or disables the Public_Link, THE Backend SHALL make the previous link and every Signed_File_URL issued through it stop working at once, and THE Backend SHALL apply no expiry date to a Share_Token (Requirement 18, criteria 4, 5, 16, and 18).
7. [PUB-07] THE Frontend SHALL render in the Shared_View and for any visitor without Board_Access no write control: no add-card, no drag handle, no edit field, no chip popover trigger, no Subtask checkbox that can be toggled, no attachment add or remove control, no comment composer, no Archive, no Delete, no Invite button, no Public link button, and no assignee control (Decision Z; Requirement 18, criterion 21).
8. [PUB-08] WHERE the viewer of the Shared_View is signed in without Board_Access, THE Frontend SHALL show a "Go to Home" link in the banner in place of the Sign in link (Decision Z).
9. [PUB-09] WHERE the viewer is a Manager or an Admin on a Board, THE Frontend SHALL show a "Public link" button in the Board's App_Header that opens the popover of Requirement 18, criterion 22, and SHALL show no such button to the Board_Owner or any other viewer (Decision Z; amends Requirement 18, criterion 22).  (V17: superseded by SHR-01 and SHR-02: the Public link is a section of the Share_Modal, Requirement 55.)
10. [PUB-10] IF an unauthenticated visitor calls `POST /api/cards/{id}/comments`, `PATCH /api/cards/{id}`, `POST /api/cards/{id}/attachments`, `PATCH /api/subtasks/{id}`, `POST /api/cards/{id}/archive`, or `DELETE /api/cards/{id}`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401 and change nothing.
11. [PUB-11] IF a signed-in user without Board_Access calls any endpoint of criterion 10 for a Card of a Board whose Public_Link they hold, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 and change nothing, because a Share_Token grants no write access on any route outside `/api/shared`.
12. [PUB-12] THE Backend SHALL answer any POST, PATCH, PUT, or DELETE under `/api/shared` with HTTP 405 and change nothing (Requirement 18, criterion 15).

### Requirement 24: Invite Link and Join Requests

**User Story:** As a Board_Owner, I want to invite people to my Board with a link and see who asked to join, so that the right people can request access and a Manager or Admin can approve them.

Note (V12, Decision AA): the Invite_Link is a second token, separate from the Public_Link. It never grants access by itself: it only lets a signed-in Member or Supervisor ask. Only a Manager or an Admin decides.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [INV-01] THE Backend SHALL store a nullable, unique `invite_token` on each Board (at least 256 bits from a cryptographically secure source, `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`), where null means invites are off, and SHALL keep it out of every Activity_Log `metadata`, every log line, and every response other than the invite management endpoints of criteria 2 to 4 (Decision AA).
2. [INV-02] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/invite` while invites are off, THE Backend SHALL generate and store an `invite_token`, write one `board.invite_enabled` Activity_Log row, and return HTTP 200 with `{ "enabled": true, "token": string, "path": "/invite/<token>" }`; WHILE invites are on, THE Backend SHALL return the existing token and write no row.
3. [INV-03] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `POST /api/boards/{id}/invite/regenerate`, THE Backend SHALL replace the `invite_token`, write one `board.invite_regenerated` row, and make the previous link stop working at once; WHEN they call `DELETE /api/boards/{id}/invite`, THE Backend SHALL set `invite_token` to null, write one `board.invite_disabled` row when invites were on, and return HTTP 200 in either case.
4. [INV-04] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `GET /api/boards/{id}/invite`, THE Backend SHALL return `{ "enabled": boolean, "token": string or null, "path": string or null }`.
5. [INV-05] IF any other signed-in user calls an endpoint of criteria 2 to 4, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 when no Token is present.
6. [INV-06] WHEN a signed-in user calls `GET /api/invite/{token}` with a current `invite_token` of a non-soft-deleted Board, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 200 with `{ "board": { "id", "name", "description" }, "state": string, "request": { "id", "status" } or null }`, where `state` is `staff` for a Manager or an Admin, `member` for the Board_Owner or a BoardMember, `pending` when the user has a pending BoardJoinRequest, and `can_request` otherwise.
7. [INV-07] IF `GET /api/invite/{token}` or `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests` carries no Token, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 401, and IF the token is unknown, was disabled or replaced, or belongs to a soft-deleted Board, THEN THE Backend SHALL return the same HTTP 404 `NOT_FOUND` response in every case.
8. [INV-08] WHEN a signed-in Member or Supervisor who is neither the Board_Owner nor a BoardMember and has no pending request calls `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests`, THE Backend SHALL create a BoardJoinRequest with status `pending`, write one `board.join_requested` Activity_Log row, create one `board_join_requested` Notification for the Board_Owner and for every Manager and Admin in the same transaction, and return HTTP 201 with `{ "id", "board_id", "status", "created_at" }`.
9. [INV-09] IF a Manager or an Admin calls `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and `error.details.role`, and create no row.
10. [INV-10] IF the caller is the Board_Owner or a BoardMember, or already has a pending request for that Board, THEN `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests` SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `CONFLICT` and `error.details.board`, and create no row.
11. [INV-11] THE Backend SHALL enforce at most one `pending` BoardJoinRequest per `(board_id, user_id)` by a partial unique index, and SHALL map a violation raised by two simultaneous requests to the HTTP 409 of criterion 10.
12. [INV-12] WHEN a rejected requester calls `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests` again, THE Backend SHALL create a new `pending` request with no cooldown and leave the earlier rejected row unchanged.
13. [INV-13] WHEN the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin calls `GET /api/boards/{id}/join-requests`, THE Backend SHALL return the `pending` requests of that Board, oldest first, each with `id`, `status`, `created_at`, and `user` as `{ id, full_name, username, email, avatar_url }` (the Email is returned only to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin, which are the only roles allowed to call this endpoint; Decisions M and S as amended in V12 round 2); the optional `?status=pending|approved|rejected|all` query SHALL select another set; any other signed-in user SHALL receive HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 with no Token.
14. [INV-14] WHEN a Manager or an Admin calls `PATCH /api/join-requests/{id}` with `{ "status": "approved" }` on a `pending` request, THE Backend SHALL set the status, set `decided_by` to the caller, create the BoardMember for the requester (no duplicate when one exists), write one `board.member_added` Activity_Log row, create one `board_added` Notification for the requester, and return HTTP 200 with the request, all in one transaction.
15. [INV-15] WHEN a Manager or an Admin calls `PATCH /api/join-requests/{id}` with `{ "status": "rejected" }` on a `pending` request, THE Backend SHALL set the status, set `decided_by`, write one `board.join_rejected` Activity_Log row, create one `board_join_rejected` Notification for the requester, and return HTTP 200, without creating a BoardMember.
16. [INV-16] IF a user who is not a Manager or an Admin, the Board_Owner included, calls `PATCH /api/join-requests/{id}`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 with no Token; IF the id is unknown THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 404; IF the request is not `pending` THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `CONFLICT`; IF `status` is neither `approved` nor `rejected` THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
17. [INV-17] THE Backend SHALL leave pending requests pending when the Invite_Link is regenerated or disabled, because a request is a record of an action already taken.
18. [INV-18] THE Backend SHALL send `Cache-Control: private, no-store` on every `/api/invite` response, and THE Frontend SHALL send `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` and `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` on every `/invite/{token}` page.
19. [INV-19] WHERE the viewer is the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin, THE Frontend SHALL show an "Invite" button in the Board's App_Header that opens a modal with: the current state, a Generate action when invites are off, the full link with a click-to-copy control, Regenerate and Disable actions (each behind a `BaseConfirm` popover that says the old link stops working), and the list of pending join requests with the requester's Avatar_Image or Initials_Avatar, full name, Username, Email, and the request time.  (V17: superseded by SHR-01 and SHR-02: the Invite_Link is a section of the Share_Modal, Requirement 55.)
20. [INV-20] WHERE the viewer is a Manager or an Admin, THE Frontend SHALL show Approve (primary) and Reject (secondary) on each pending request in the Invite modal; WHERE the viewer is the Board_Owner and not a Manager or an Admin, THE Frontend SHALL show the same list without those actions and with the note "A manager or an admin decides on requests."
21. [INV-21] WHERE the viewer can see pending requests and the Board has at least one, THE Frontend SHALL show the count as a badge on the Invite button.  (V17: superseded by SHR-01: the count is on the Share item.)
22. [INV-22] WHEN a visitor without a Session_Cookie opens `/invite/{token}`, THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/login?next=/invite/<token>`; WHEN the visitor signs in or registers there, THE Frontend SHALL return to `/invite/<token>` (Requirement 1, criteria 59 and 66).
23. [INV-23] WHILE the viewer is signed in and `state` is `can_request`, THE Frontend SHALL show the Board name, the description, and a "Request to join" button that calls `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests` and then shows "Request sent. You will get a notification when a manager or an admin decides."
24. [INV-24] WHILE `state` is `pending`, THE Frontend SHALL show "Your request is waiting for a decision." with no button; WHILE `state` is `member`, THE Frontend SHALL show "You are already a member of this board." with an Open board link; WHILE `state` is `staff`, THE Frontend SHALL show "You already have access to every board." with an Open board link.
25. [INV-25] IF the invite route answers HTTP 404, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show one page that says the invite link is not valid, without saying whether it was disabled, replaced, or never existed.
26. [INV-26] THE Backend SHALL record each BoardJoinRequest as `id`, `board_id`, `user_id`, `status` (`pending`, `approved`, or `rejected`), nullable `decided_by`, `created_at`, and `updated_at`.
27. [INV-27] THE Frontend SHALL show a requester's Email only in the pending list of the Invite modal, to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin, and SHALL show it in no Inbox row, Notification text, Activity_Log row, tooltip, `alt` text, or label (Decision S as amended in V12 round 2).
28. [INV-28] THE Backend SHALL return no Email in the response of `POST /api/invite/{token}/requests`, in the response of `PATCH /api/join-requests/{id}`, in any Notification payload, or in any Activity_Log `metadata` of the join-request actions, and SHALL read `User.email` for the pending list through the join of the request's `user_id` without copying it into `board_join_requests` (Decision S as amended in V12 round 2).

### Requirement 25: Card Modal Auto-Save

**User Story:** As a user who edits a Card, I want every change to save by itself and to see that it saved, so that I never lose an edit and never have to remember a Save button.

Note (V12, Decision AB): this requirement supersedes the draft model of Decision I and Decision J's sentence "attachment changes in the card modal wait for Save changes". Requirement 4 criteria 21, 30, 32, 35, and 43 and the "one save, one Activity_Log row" rule are superseded and carry a label. The Backend API of `PATCH /api/cards/{id}` does not change; the Frontend sends smaller requests.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [SAVE-01] THE Frontend SHALL show no "Save changes" button and keep no draft of the Card in the Card modal; the footer holds only Archive (secondary) and Delete (danger, behind a `BaseConfirm` popover), right-aligned (Decision AB).
2. [SAVE-02] WHEN the title field loses focus or the user presses Enter in it, and the trimmed value differs from the last saved value, THE Frontend SHALL send one `PATCH /api/cards/{id}` with `{ "title": value }`; WHEN the description field loses focus or the user presses Ctrl+Enter or Cmd+Enter in it, and the value differs, THE Frontend SHALL send one `PATCH` with `{ "description": value }`.
3. [SAVE-03] IF the title is empty after trimming, THEN THE Frontend SHALL send no request, show the inline error "Title is required." beside the field, and keep the field focused for editing.
4. [SAVE-04] WHEN the user confirms a value in the category popover, the priority popover, or the assignee popover (choosing an option or Remove), THE Frontend SHALL send one `PATCH` with only that field (`category_id`, `priority`, or `assignee_id`) and close the popover.
5. [SAVE-05] WHEN the user closes the dates popover and the start date, the end date, or both differ from the last saved values, THE Frontend SHALL send one `PATCH` with `start_date` and `end_date`; WHILE the popover is open and the pair is invalid (Requirement 4, criterion 32), THE Frontend SHALL send nothing and show the inline error.
6. [SAVE-06] WHEN the user adds a Subtask, toggles it, renames it, reorders it, or deletes it, THE Frontend SHALL send that one call (`POST /api/cards/{id}/subtasks`, `PATCH /api/subtasks/{id}`, or `DELETE /api/subtasks/{id}`) at once, and WHEN the user adds an Attachment link or picks an Attachment file, THE Frontend SHALL send `POST /api/cards/{id}/attachments` at once (Decision AB; supersedes Requirement 4, criterion 43).
7. [SAVE-07] WHILE a field's request is in flight, THE Frontend SHALL show "Saving…" beside that field; WHEN it succeeds, THE Frontend SHALL show "Saved" for 2 seconds and then nothing; IF it fails, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show the server message (`error.details` first, otherwise `message`) as an inline error with a Retry button beside that field, keep the field editable with the user's text, and keep the last saved value as the comparison base.
8. [SAVE-08] THE Frontend SHALL announce each field's state through an `aria-live="polite"` region beside the field, so that "Saving", "Saved", and the error reach assistive technology.
9. [SAVE-09] WHEN the response of a field's `PATCH` arrives, THE Frontend SHALL merge into the displayed Card only the fields that request sent, so that a later response never overwrites another field the user is still editing (last write wins per field).
10. [SAVE-10] THE Backend SHALL apply concurrent edits as last write wins: it SHALL carry no version or ETag check on Cards in V12, and two users editing the same field produce two `card.updated` rows, the later value stands.
11. [SAVE-11] WHEN a `PATCH /api/cards/{id}` changes no value, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 200 and write no Activity_Log row; WHEN it changes at least one value, THE Backend SHALL write exactly one `card.updated` row whose `metadata.changes` lists only the fields that changed, so that one save writes one row and, because the Frontend sends one field per request (the two dates together), the row reads as one change (Decision AB; replaces the "one save, one row" rule of Decision I).
12. [SAVE-12] WHEN a Subtask or Attachment is added, changed, or removed, THE Backend SHALL write one Activity_Log row in the same transaction with the action `card.subtask_added`, `card.subtask_updated` (toggle, rename, or reorder), `card.subtask_removed`, `card.attachment_added`, or `card.attachment_removed`, and with `metadata` holding `card_title` and the Subtask title or the Attachment name, and THE Frontend SHALL describe each as a sentence in `describeActivity` (Requirement 8, criteria 9 and 10).
13. [SAVE-13] WHEN the user closes the Card modal (header icon, Escape, or a click on the backdrop) while a field has a pending blur save or a request in flight, THE Frontend SHALL send the pending save, wait for every in-flight request to finish, and then close; WHILE it waits, THE Frontend SHALL show "Saving…" in the header.
14. [SAVE-14] IF the user closes the Card modal while a field is in the failed state of criterion 7, THEN THE Frontend SHALL ask in a `BaseConfirm` dialog "Some changes were not saved. Close anyway?" and close only on confirmation (replaces the "unsaved changes" prompt of Requirement 4, criterion 43).
15. [SAVE-15] THE Frontend SHALL keep the Comment composer independent of every field save, and a posted Comment SHALL appear in the thread without touching the state of any other field (Requirement 7).
16. [SAVE-16] WHERE the viewer has no edit right on the Card (Requirement 4, criteria 8 and 9, and every viewer of the Shared_View), THE Frontend SHALL render the fields read-only with no save state.

### Requirement 26: Comment Deletion and Attachment Removal Confirmation

Note (V17): the popover and modal forms of `BaseConfirm` are replaced by `ConfirmDialog` (FIX-07, Requirement 45, Decision AY); the cases that need a confirmation are unchanged.
**User Story:** As a user, I want to delete my own comments and to be asked before an attachment is removed, so that I can retract what I wrote and never lose a file by a stray click.

Note (V12, Decision AC): `BaseConfirm` has two forms. The popover form is a small panel anchored to the trigger and is used for Delete Card, Delete Subtask, Remove Board member, Regenerate or Disable a Public_Link or Invite_Link, and Remove profile picture; Approve and Reject on a join request are not confirmed. The modal form is a centred dialog with a backdrop and focus trap and is used where the action removes content other people may rely on or removes more than one row: removing an Attachment and deleting a Comment. Requirement 4 criterion 44 is amended for Attachments only.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [DEL-01] WHEN a user activates Remove on an Attachment, THE Frontend SHALL open the modal form of `BaseConfirm` with the title "Remove attachment?", the Attachment's name, a danger "Remove" button, and a "Cancel" button, and SHALL call `DELETE /api/attachments/{id}` only after the user confirms (Decision AC; amends Requirement 4, criterion 44).
2. [DEL-02] WHEN the user confirms, THE Frontend SHALL remove the Attachment tile when the call returns HTTP 200; IF the call fails, THEN THE Frontend SHALL keep the tile and show the inline error with Retry beside the Attachments card.
3. [DEL-03] WHEN an Attachment with an uploaded file is removed, THE Backend SHALL delete the Attachment row and write one `card.attachment_removed` Activity_Log row in one transaction and SHALL delete the stored file after the commit.
4. [DEL-04] WHEN the author of a Comment or Reply calls `DELETE /api/comments/{id}` and still has Board_Access to the Card's Board, THE Backend SHALL delete that row and return HTTP 200 with `{ "deleted": n }`, where `n` is the number of rows removed (Decision AC).
5. [DEL-05] WHEN a Reply is deleted, THE Backend SHALL decrement the parent's `reply_count` by one in the same transaction, so that `reply_count` stays equal to the number of the parent's Replies.
6. [DEL-06] WHEN a top-level Comment that has Replies is deleted, THE Backend SHALL delete its Replies in the same transaction and return `n` equal to one plus the number of Replies.
7. [DEL-07] IF a signed-in user who is not the author calls `DELETE /api/comments/{id}`, a Manager and an Admin included, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403; IF no Token is present THEN HTTP 401; IF the id is unknown THEN HTTP 404; and in each case THE Backend SHALL change nothing (Decision AC; Manager and Admin deletion of others' Comments is out of scope).
8. [DEL-08] WHEN a Comment or Reply is deleted, THE Backend SHALL write one `comment.deleted` Activity_Log row in the same transaction with `target_type` `card`, `target_id` the Card's id, and `metadata` holding `card_title`, `comment_id`, `is_reply`, and `deleted_replies`, and no part of the Comment's text, so that the append-only log stays intact and the removed text is not kept.
9. [DEL-09] THE Backend SHALL treat `comment.deleted` as the single documented exception to Requirement 4, criterion 29 for Comments: a Comment has no `deleted_at`, is hidden with its Card by the Card's `deleted_at`, and is physically removed only by the author's action of criterion 4; Subtask rows and Attachment rows follow Requirement 4, criterion 29 unchanged, and Activity_Log rows are never deleted (Requirement 8, criterion 2).
10. [DEL-10] WHERE the viewer is the author of a Comment or Reply on a Card they can open, THE Frontend SHALL show a Delete action on it; WHEN the user activates it, THE Frontend SHALL open the modal form of `BaseConfirm` with the title "Delete comment?" and, for a top-level Comment with Replies, the text "This also deletes its N replies." (N from `reply_count`), and SHALL remove the Comment or Reply and update the parent's reply count on HTTP 200.
11. [DEL-11] THE Frontend SHALL show no Delete action on another user's Comment and no Delete action in the Shared_View or to any viewer without Board_Access.

### Requirement 27: Subtask Permissions and Derived Progress

**User Story:** As a Member, I want to tick off the Subtasks of a Card assigned to me, and as any viewer I want the Card's progress to follow its Subtasks, so that the meter is never typed by hand and never out of date.

Note (V12, Decision AD): Requirement 4 criteria 12 to 14 already give the Assigned_Member the edit rights of a Subtask, so this decision does not reverse Decision 2; it states the exact matrix and makes the toggle an explicit, tested requirement. Reconciliation: the Card's `progress` was a stored, stretch column (Requirement 16, criterion 1) that no code writes yet; V12 makes it a computed, read-only field.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [SUB-01] THE Backend SHALL apply this Subtask matrix, each action also requiring Board_Access: toggle `is_done`, add, rename, reorder, and delete are allowed to a Manager, an Admin, and a Supervisor with Board_Staff_Access on any Card of the Board, and to a Member only on a Card whose `assignee_id` is that Member; every other combination returns HTTP 403, or HTTP 401 with no Token (Decision AD; Requirement 4, criteria 12 to 14).
2. [SUB-02] WHEN an Assigned_Member calls `PATCH /api/subtasks/{id}` with `{ "is_done": true }` or `{ "is_done": false }`, THE Backend SHALL persist the value, write one `card.subtask_updated` row, and return the Subtask.
3. [SUB-03] IF a Member calls any Subtask endpoint for a Card whose `assignee_id` is not that Member or is null, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 403 and change nothing (Decision 2).
4. [SUB-04] THE Backend SHALL compute `progress` on every Card payload as `(20 * done + total) // (2 * total)` (rounded half up, an integer from 0 to 10) when `total` is greater than 0, and as null when the Card has no Subtasks, where `done` and `total` count the Card's Subtasks, and SHALL also return `subtask_done_count` and `subtask_total_count`, all from the already loaded Subtasks with no extra query.
5. [SUB-05] IF a request body contains `progress`, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and `error.details.progress`, because `progress` is derived (Decision AD; supersedes Requirement 16, criterion 1 for `progress`; `views_count` stays stored and stretch).
6. [SUB-06] WHERE a Card has at least one Subtask, THE Frontend SHALL show a progress meter with the text "done of total" on the board Card and in the Card modal's Subtasks card; WHERE a Card has none, THE Frontend SHALL show no meter on the board Card and the text "No subtasks yet" in the modal.
7. [SUB-07] WHEN a Subtask is toggled, added, or deleted, THE Frontend SHALL update the meter from the response without a reload.
8. [SUB-08] WHERE the viewer is a Member and the Card is assigned to that Member, THE Frontend SHALL show enabled Subtask checkboxes; WHERE the Card is assigned to someone else, THE Frontend SHALL show them disabled; in the Shared_View and for any viewer without Board_Access THE Frontend SHALL show them disabled and send no request (Requirement 23, criterion 7).
9. [SUB-09] THE Backend SHALL include `progress`, `subtask_done_count`, and `subtask_total_count` in the shared Card payload, which already carries Subtasks (Requirement 18, criterion 11).

### Requirement 28: Inbox Navigation and Notification Types

**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want to open what a notification is about with one click, so that I land on the right Card or Board instead of hunting for it.

Note (V12, Decision AE): this requirement amends Requirement 9 (types and links) and Glossary Notification. A Notification of type `deadline` or `assigned` keeps its V2 rules.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [INB-01] THE Backend SHALL store each Notification with `type` (`assigned`, `deadline`, `board_join_requested`, `board_added`, or `board_join_rejected`), nullable `card_id`, nullable `board_id` (always set by the Backend on new rows), nullable `actor_id`, nullable `join_request_id`, `notify_date`, `is_read` (default false), and `created_at`, with a CHECK that a card type has a `card_id` and a board type has a `board_id` and no `card_id` (Decision AE).
2. [INB-02] THE Backend SHALL deduplicate Notifications with three partial unique indexes: `(user_id, card_id, type, notify_date)` where `card_id IS NOT NULL`; `(user_id, type, join_request_id)` where `join_request_id IS NOT NULL`; and `(user_id, type, board_id, notify_date)` where `card_id IS NULL AND join_request_id IS NULL`, so that rows with a null `card_id` stay deduplicated, every join request notifies once per user, and a null never makes two rows distinct (Decision AE).
3. [INB-03] WHEN a Manager or an Admin makes a Supervisor the Board_Owner of a Board (Requirement 22, criterion 12), THE Backend SHALL create one `board_added` Notification for that Supervisor with `board_id` and the actor in the same transaction.
4. [INB-04] THE Backend SHALL return on each Notification in `GET /api/notifications`: `id`, `type`, `board_id`, `board_name`, `card_id`, `card_title`, `actor_name`, `is_read`, and `created_at`, loading names in a fixed number of queries whatever the number of rows, and SHALL return no Email.
5. [INB-05] THE Frontend SHALL render each Inbox row as one link whose `href` is `/boards/{board_id}?card={card_id}` for `assigned` and `deadline`, `/boards/{board_id}?invite=1` for `board_join_requested`, `/boards/{board_id}` for `board_added`, and `/boards` for `board_join_rejected` (the requester has no access to the Board), so that the row is reachable and activatable by keyboard (Enter) and by middle click (Decision AE).
6. [INB-06] WHEN the user activates an Inbox row, THE Frontend SHALL send `PATCH /api/notifications/{id}/read`, lower the unread count at once, navigate without waiting for the response, and close the Side_Panel.
7. [INB-07] WHEN the Board page opens with `?card=<id>`, THE Frontend SHALL open that Card's modal after the Board loads, and WHEN the modal closes THE Frontend SHALL remove the parameter.
8. [INB-08] IF the Card of `?card=` is soft-deleted, unknown, or on another Board, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show the notice "This card is no longer available." above the Board and open no modal; IF the Card is archived, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show "This card is archived." and open no modal.
9. [INB-09] IF the Board answers HTTP 403 or HTTP 404 for a Notification target, THEN THE Frontend SHALL show a page with the message "You no longer have access to this board, or it was removed." and a link to Home, never a blank page or the raw error.
10. [INB-10] WHEN the Board page opens with `?invite=1` and the viewer is the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin, THE Frontend SHALL open the Invite modal on the pending-request list (Requirement 24, criterion 19); for any other viewer THE Frontend SHALL ignore the parameter.
11. [INB-11] THE Frontend SHALL write each new Notification type as a sentence naming the actor and the Board: "Ada Lovelace asked to join "Board name"", "You were added to "Board name"", and "Your request to join "Board name" was declined".

### Requirement 29: Profile Page Layout

Note (V17): superseded by Requirement 54 (the Profile, Decision BG).
**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want the Profile page to use the width of my screen, so that my account details and my password change are both in view on a desktop.

Note (V12, Decision AF): this requirement amends Requirement 19 only in layout; the fields, validation, and endpoints of Requirement 19 do not change.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [PRO-01] WHILE the viewport is 960 pixels wide or more, THE Frontend SHALL lay out the Profile_Page content in two columns of equal width inside a full-width container (the page padding of the App_Header shell, no centred narrow column): the avatar card and the account details card on the left, stacked, and the change-password card on the right.
2. [PRO-02] WHILE the viewport is narrower than 960 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL stack the cards in one column in this order: avatar, account details, change password (mobile-first base styles, with the two-column grid added by a `min-width: 960px` query).
3. [PRO-03] THE Frontend SHALL build the Profile_Page from the existing tokens only: controls 40 pixels high (compact buttons 32 pixels), 16 pixel padding in every card, 24 pixels between cards, titles and labels at the sizes of Requirement 14, criterion 20, and no hard-coded colour or arbitrary value.
4. [PRO-04] THE Frontend SHALL keep every field, action, validation message, and endpoint of Requirement 19 on the Profile_Page, and the avatar preview SHALL keep its size at every width.

### Requirement 30: Role Tag in the Assignee Picker

**User Story:** As a Manager or Supervisor, I want each person in the assignee list to show their role, so that I can tell a Member from a Supervisor before I assign a Card.

Note (V13, Decision AG): amends Requirement 4 criterion 33 (the assignee popover) and Requirement 22 criterion 13 (the members list). The role is not private: other users already see it in the Sidebar. The Email rule of Decision S does not change.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [ROLE-01] THE Backend SHALL return `role` (`member` or `supervisor`) on every row of `GET /api/boards/{id}/members`, with the other fields of Requirement 22 criterion 13 unchanged; Managers and Admins never appear in that list (Requirement 22, criterion 5), so no row carries `manager` or `admin`.
2. [ROLE-02] THE Frontend SHALL show a role tag in each row of the assignee popover, after the full name: the text "Supervisor" or "Member", drawn by `BaseBadge` (`neutral` tone for Member, `blue` for Supervisor, `tag` shape), at the badge text size, never colour alone, and the tag SHALL be part of the row's accessible name.
3. [ROLE-03] THE Frontend SHALL show the same tag on the Board_Owner row of the Sidebar Team members list beside the "Owner" tag (Requirement 22, criterion 19), and on each other row, so that the Sidebar and the picker read alike; the Email stays out of both unless Requirement 4 criterion 33 shows it to staff.
4. [ROLE-04] THE Frontend SHALL let the search of the assignee popover match the role word as well as the name, Username, and Email (for example "super"), and SHALL keep the current selection, the Unassigned row, and the keyboard behaviour of `BaseCombobox`.
5. [ROLE-05] THE Frontend SHALL show no role tag in the Shared_View and SHALL add no `role` to any `/api/shared` payload (Requirement 23, criterion 5).

### Requirement 31: Control Sizes in the Invite and Public Link Controls

**User Story:** As a user of the Board header and the Invite modal, I want the buttons to be the same size as every other control, so that the screen looks like one system.

Note (V13, Decision AH): the V12 build used compact (32 pixel) buttons beside 40 pixel inputs and a 40 pixel header bell. Requirement 14 criterion 20 says controls share one scale: 40 pixels, compact 32 pixels only inside a dense row.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [SIZE-01] THE Frontend SHALL draw the Invite button and the Public link button in the Board's App_Header at the default control size (40 pixels high, `BaseButton` `md`), with the 18 pixel icon, 12 pixel horizontal padding gap of the system, and the same height as the Inbox bell and the User_Menu trigger, and SHALL show the count badge of Requirement 24 criterion 21 inside that height. (V16: control sizes come from Requirement 38, DS-17 and DS-18.)
2. [SIZE-02] THE Frontend SHALL draw "Generate invite link", "Regenerate", "Disable", and the Public link popover's Regenerate and Disable at 40 pixels (`md`), and the link field and its Copy button at the same 40 pixel height in one row, so that the field and the button align on one baseline and one top edge.
3. [SIZE-03] THE Frontend SHALL keep Approve and Reject at the compact size (32 pixels) only inside a join-request row, because that row is a dense list; each of them SHALL still have a 44 pixel touch target below 960 pixels (Requirement 14, criterion 21).
4. [SIZE-04] THE Frontend SHALL give the Invite modal the standard modal anatomy of Decision J: header with title and close icon, body at 24 pixel padding with 24 pixels between sections, and no extra footer; each section SHALL be a titled block (`h3` at the heading size), not an unlabelled paragraph, and the empty state SHALL read like the other empty states ("No requests waiting.").
5. [SIZE-05] THE Frontend SHALL use only existing size and spacing tokens for these controls, SHALL add no arbitrary-value utility, and SHALL pass the styling test of Decision H.
6. [SIZE-06] WHILE the viewport is narrower than 960 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL keep the Invite and Public link buttons reachable in the header without horizontal scroll: icon with a visible text label while there is room, and icon only (with the accessible name kept) when the Board name and the buttons do not fit.

### Requirement 32: Document Titles

**User Story:** As a user with several tabs open, I want each tab to say where I am, so that I can find RevoTask and the page I need at a glance.

Note (V13, Decision AI): amends Requirement 21 criterion BRAND-11 (the suffix "· RevoTask") and every page `metadata` title of earlier versions. The root title stays `RevoTask`.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [TITLE-01] THE Frontend SHALL set the document title of every page to `RevoTask | <name>` through the Next.js `metadata` API, where `<name>` is the page or panel the user has open, so that the brand comes first and the name follows; the root layout SHALL define `title.default` `RevoTask` and `title.template` `RevoTask | %s`.
2. [TITLE-02] THE Frontend SHALL use these names: Home `Home`; a Board its name (`RevoTask | Alpha Board`); Categories `Categories · <Board name>`; Profile `Profile`; Users `Users`; an invitation `Invitation`; a Shared_View `Shared · <Board name>`; sign-in `Log in`; registration `Register`; the session-expired and not-valid pages `Unavailable`.
3. [TITLE-03] THE Frontend SHALL build the Board and Categories titles with `generateMetadata` from the Board the page already loads (no second request), and SHALL fall back to `RevoTask | Board` when the Board cannot be read; THE title SHALL NOT contain a Token, an invite token, a Share_Token, an Email, or a Comment.
4. [TITLE-04] WHEN the Inbox or the Activity panel is open, THE Frontend SHALL set the title to `RevoTask | Inbox` or `RevoTask | Activity` (with the unread count as `RevoTask | Inbox (3)` when it is above zero), and SHALL restore the title of the page when the panel closes; the title SHALL follow the panel the user opened last.
5. [TITLE-05] WHERE a Card modal is open, THE Frontend SHALL show `RevoTask | <Card title> · <Board name>` and restore the Board title when it closes (Open Question 29).
6. [TITLE-06] THE Frontend SHALL escape no title by hand (React and Next.js do) and SHALL cut a title longer than 60 characters with "…" so that the tab stays readable.
7. [TITLE-07] THE Frontend SHALL keep `robots` `noindex` on the invitation and Shared_View pages, and the other `metadata` fields (description, icons by file convention) SHALL not change.
8. [TITLE-08] THE System SHALL include `vitest` tests for the title builder (a pure function: name in, title out, 60 character cut, panel and unread count) and one test per route that renders its title; the e2e suite SHALL assert `document.title` of `/login` and `/register` only (read-only, Requirement 1 criteria 65 to 72 unchanged).

### Requirement 33: Production Topology

Note (V17): DEP-05 and DEP-13 are amended by Requirement 46 (one origin, `/app` and `/app/api`).
**User Story:** As the product owner, I want RevoTask to run on my server behind Nginx and return after a reboot, so that people can use it from the internet.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [DEP-01] THE System SHALL run the Backend, the Frontend, and a deadline scheduler as Docker Compose services built from the monorepo, each as a non-root user.
2. [DEP-02] THE System SHALL use the PostgreSQL installed on the server at `localhost:5432`, database `revoboard_db`, owned by the non-superuser role `revou`, and SHALL provide idempotent setup SQL that takes the password at run time.
3. [DEP-03] THE System SHALL run every service with `network_mode: host`, binding the Backend to `127.0.0.1:5001` and the Frontend to `127.0.0.1:3000`.
4. [DEP-04] THE Backend SHALL be served by Gunicorn with 3 workers.
5. [DEP-05] THE System SHALL provide a new Nginx site file that serves one origin, sends `/api/` to the Backend and every other path to the Frontend, preserves the public host and port, overwrites `X-Forwarded-For` with the client address, and limits the request body (Open Question 35). THE Backend SHALL run with `TRUSTED_PROXY_COUNT=1`. (V16: two hosts, Requirement 42, HOST-10 and HOST-15.)  (V16: amended by Requirement 42; V17: superseded by PATH-03 and PATH-11, Requirement 46.)
6. [DEP-06] THE Frontend SHALL call `/api` on the current origin from the browser and SHALL read an absolute loopback Backend URL from `INTERNAL_API_URL` on the server, so that one build works on the IP address and on the domain.
7. [DEP-07] THE Frontend SHALL send every redirect, `/session-expired` included, to the public origin or as a relative path, and never to `localhost` or a container address.
8. [DEP-08] WHEN the server boots, THE System SHALL start PostgreSQL, Nginx, and Docker through their enabled system services, and every Compose service through `restart: unless-stopped`.
9. [DEP-09] THE System SHALL run `flask notify-deadlines` at least hourly from the scheduler service; a repeated run SHALL create no duplicate Notification.
10. [DEP-10] THE System SHALL keep uploads outside the checkout and the images, so that they survive a deploy and a reboot.
11. [DEP-11] THE System SHALL keep every secret and password out of the repository, the images, and the CI logs; application secrets SHALL live only in a server file with mode 600.
12. [DEP-12] THE System SHALL rotate container logs with the `json-file` driver, `max-size` 10 MB and `max-file` 3.
13. [DEP-13] UNTIL the domain and TLS are configured, THE System SHALL accept plain HTTP on the server IP and port 8082. The later TLS change SHALL add `Secure` to the Session_Cookie and update `ALLOWED_ORIGINS`. The planned domain is `revoboard.belajardeploy.web.id`. (V16: the planned domain is the Site_Host and `app.` under it is the App_Host, Requirement 42, HOST-15.)  (V17: the planned domain is one origin; access by IP and port 8082 stays, PATH-11.)

### Requirement 34: Continuous Integration and Deployment

**User Story:** As the product owner, I want a push to master to be tested in a separate environment and then deployed, so that a broken build does not reach production.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [CICD-01] WHEN a pull request targets `master` or a commit is pushed to `master`, THE System SHALL run `pytest`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vitest run`, and `next build` in GitHub Actions.
2. [CICD-02] WHEN those checks pass, THE System SHALL build both images and start the production Compose stack in the CI runner against a throwaway PostgreSQL with throwaway secrets, starting from an empty database.
3. [CICD-03] THE smoke test SHALL verify the migrations, a second start, `/api/health`, login with a seeded test user, a server-rendered Board page, and the redirect target of `/session-expired`, all through Nginx.
4. [CICD-04] WHEN both jobs pass on a push to `master`, and only then, THE System SHALL deploy that exact commit over SSH with a pinned host key and build the images on the server, tagged with the commit SHA.
5. [CICD-05] THE System SHALL run one deployment at a time and SHALL NOT cancel a running deployment.
6. [CICD-06] BEFORE it replaces the running services, THE System SHALL back up the database (Open Question 34).
7. [CICD-07] IF the health check fails after a deploy, THEN THE System SHALL restart the previous images without running migrations, SHALL leave the schema as it is, and SHALL report the job as failed.
8. [CICD-08] THE System SHALL require each later migration to stay compatible with the previous release, because a rollback does not downgrade the schema.

### Requirement 35: Health Endpoint

**User Story:** As the operator, I want one endpoint that says whether the Backend works, so that deploys and health checks can decide automatically.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [HLT-01] WHEN `GET /api/health` is called without a Token and the database answers `SELECT 1`, THE Backend SHALL return 200 in the standard Envelope.
2. [HLT-02] IF the database does not answer, THEN THE Backend SHALL return 503 in the standard error Envelope within 5 seconds, without exception text, host, user, or connection string.
3. [HLT-03] THE Backend SHALL apply no rate limit to this endpoint, SHALL follow the routes, service, and repository layers, and SHALL list it in `openapi.json` and `API.md`.

### Requirement 36: Demo Seed

**User Story:** As the product owner, I want demo data in the deployed app, so that I can show it working.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [SEED-01] THE System SHALL document a manual, one-time `flask seed-data` command in a one-off container that alone receives `APP_ENV=development`, after the first migration.
2. [SEED-02] THE System SHALL take the seed password at run time and SHALL NOT write it to the repository, the server environment file, or the shell history.
3. [SEED-03] THE running Backend SHALL keep `APP_ENV=production`, the API documentation SHALL stay hidden, and no start or deploy step SHALL run a seed.

### Requirement 37: Migrations on Start

**User Story:** As the operator, I want the Backend to migrate on start without risk to data, so that a deploy needs no manual step.

#### Acceptance Criteria

1. [MIG-01] WHEN the Backend container starts normally, THE System SHALL run `flask db upgrade` once before Gunicorn starts and SHALL NOT start Gunicorn if it fails; a rollback start SHALL skip this step.
2. [MIG-02] WHEN the V10 or V12 migration runs outside development and testing and `users` is empty, THE migration SHALL apply its schema changes without `TRUNCATE`.
3. [MIG-03] IF either migration runs outside development and testing and `users` has rows, THEN THE migration SHALL abort with no change. Behaviour in development and testing SHALL NOT change.

### Requirement 38: Design System

**User Story:** As the product owner, I want the app, the sign-in pages, and the landing page to look like `design/kanban-prototype-v2.html` and to share one design system, so that every component looks balanced next to every other component and the product matches the prototype.

**Visual source of truth.** The prototype is the source of truth for how RevoBoard looks. Its 13 pages were rendered in a browser (Chrome, 1440 and 390 pixels wide) and their computed styles measured; the renders are in `design/reference/v2/` and the numbers below come from those measurements, not from the Tailwind class names. The prototype is not consistent with itself: pages 1, 5, 10, 11, 12, and 13 share one "Template Layout" and are the canonical look; pages 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, and 9 were drawn later with a slate colour set, different radii (12, 28, and 36 pixels), a different rail width (72 to 96 pixels), and hex colours of their own. Where pages disagree, the canonical look wins and the other pages are redrawn with the canonical recipes (DS-26). Where this requirement and a reference image differ in a detail, the measured value in this requirement wins and the image shows the intent.

#### Acceptance Criteria

**Single source**

1. [DS-01] THE Frontend SHALL take every colour, type size, line height, letter spacing, font weight, spacing, radius, shadow, size, layer, duration, opacity, gradient, and blur from the Design_System: custom properties in `frontend/styles/tokens.css` exposed to Tailwind through the theme alias in `frontend/styles/globals.css`. THE Frontend SHALL NOT add a second token file, a second theme, or a token prefix for the landing page, the sign-in pages, or any other area (Decision AQ).
2. [DS-02] THE Design_System SHALL define these brand and accent tokens, with the prototype value they come from:

| Token | Value | Prototype name | Use |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--brand` | `#002954` | `primary` | dark pill buttons, active filter chip, submit button on the auth card, avatar fill |
| `--brand-strong` | `#001b3c` | `on-primary-fixed` | hover of `--brand` |
| `--brand-soft` | `#1c3f6e` | `primary-container` | second fill of the brand gradient, secondary avatar fill |
| `--brand-tint` | `#d6e3ff` | `primary-fixed` | info highlight fill |
| `--on-brand` | `#ffffff` | `on-primary` | text and icons on `--brand` |
| `--on-brand-muted` | `#8cabe1` | `on-primary-container` | secondary text on `--brand` and `--brand-soft` |
| `--accent` | `#006b5f` | `secondary` | primary action pill, progress fill, active rail circle, links, unread dot (Decision AP) |
| `--accent-strong` | `#005047` | `on-secondary-fixed-variant` | hover of `--accent` |
| `--accent-tint` | `#76f4e0` | `secondary-container` | logo tile, count badges, "Completed" and "Med" pills, CTA button on a dark band |
| `--on-accent` | `#ffffff` | `on-secondary` | text and icons on `--accent` |
| `--on-accent-tint` | `#006f63` | `on-secondary-container` | text on `--accent-tint` |

3. [DS-03] THE Design_System SHALL define these surface and text tokens:

| Token | Value | Prototype name | Use |
|---|---|---|---|
| `--surface-100` | `#f3f2ff` | `surface-container-low` | page canvas, and every tinted fill: Columns, the filter bar, the Board_Switcher trigger, input fields, the Top_Nav track, a segmented switch, a quiet pill button |
| `--surface-200` | `#ffffff` | `surface-container-lowest` | the app frame, the Icon_Rail, Cards, modals, panels, popovers, chips on a tinted fill |
| `--surface-sunken` | `#ebedff` | `surface-container` | hover and active fill, the active Top_Nav item, category tags, the progress track, a quiet badge |
| `--surface-strong` | `#dce1ff` | `surface-container-highest` | count badges, disabled fill, skeleton |
| `--ink` | `#0f193c` | `on-surface` | body and heading text |
| `--ink-muted` | `#43474f` | `on-surface-variant` | secondary text, icons, labels |
| `--ink-faint` | `#747780` | `outline` | decorative icons and disabled text only; never body text |

   THE Design_System SHALL retire `--surface-hover` and the `#faf8ff` page colour of the first V16 draft.
4. [DS-04] THE Design_System SHALL define `--border` as `#ebedff` (a one pixel outline on cards inside the landing page and the invite rows), keep `--ring` as the focus ring colour (Requirement 14 criterion 15), and draw form fields, the filter bar, and Columns without a border, as the prototype does: a field is a `--surface-100` fill, and its boundary is the fill plus the focus ring.
5. [DS-05] THE Design_System SHALL define `--danger` as `#ba1a1a`, `--danger-soft` as `#ffdad6`, `--on-danger` as `#ffffff`, and `--on-danger-soft` as `#93000a`.
6. [DS-06] THE Design_System SHALL keep the `--label-*` background and text pairs and the `--tone-*` solid hues of V7 (used for Column dots and category chips) unchanged, and SHALL keep `--online`, `--scrim`, and `--scrim-strong`, rebuilding the scrim colours from `--ink`. THE Design_System SHALL NOT adopt the prototype's tertiary cyan or any colour that is not in the tables of this requirement or in the V7 pairs. THE Design_System SHALL define two gradients: `--gradient-brand` (`--brand` to `--brand-soft`, 135 degrees, with a soft `--accent-tint` glow) for the auth brand panel, and `--gradient-cta` (`--brand` to `--accent`, 90 degrees) for the landing call-to-action band.
7. [DS-07] THE Frontend SHALL test every text and surface pair of the Design_System in `frontend/tests/` by computing its contrast ratio from `tokens.css`, requiring at least 4.5 to 1 for text under 24 pixels (and for bold text under 19 pixels) and at least 3 to 1 for the focus ring on every surface and for large text. The pairs are `--ink`, `--ink-muted`, `--accent`, and `--danger` on `--surface-100`, `--surface-200`, and `--surface-sunken`; `--ink-muted` on `--surface-strong`; `--on-brand` on `--brand` and `--brand-soft`; `--on-brand-muted` on `--brand` and `--brand-soft`; `--on-accent` on `--accent`; `--on-accent-tint` on `--accent-tint`; `--on-danger` on `--danger`; `--on-danger-soft` on `--danger-soft`; and every `--label-*` pair. (Measured ratios of the prototype values: `--on-accent-tint` on `--accent-tint` 4.57, `--ink-muted` on `--surface-100` 8.41, `--accent` on `--surface-100` 5.80, `--on-danger-soft` on `--danger-soft` 7.24.)

**Type**

8. [DS-08] THE Design_System SHALL define this one type scale, measured from the prototype. Each entry is a Tailwind `text-*` utility that bundles size, line height, weight, and letter spacing through the theme's companion variables, so a component never sets those four separately:

| Utility | Size / line | Weight | Tracking | Family | Use (measured on) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `text-display` | 34 / 42 from 960 pixels, 28 / 36 below | 700 | -0.02em | heading | landing `h1`, auth brand panel title |
| `text-xl` | 24 / 30 | 700 | -0.015em | heading | page and section titles, the Board name in the Board_Switcher, auth form title |
| `text-title` | 20 / 26 | 600 | -0.01em | heading | modal and panel titles, the Dashboard greeting |
| `text-page-title` | 18 / 24 | 600 | 0 | heading | Column title, landing feature title |
| `text-base` | 15 / 22 | 400 | 0 | body | landing and auth paragraph text |
| `text-card-title` | 15 / 21 (16 / 22 from 1280 pixels) | 600 | 0 | body | board Card title |
| `text-sm` | 14 / 20 | 400 (500 on buttons and nav) | 0 | body | default text, Card description, buttons, nav |
| `text-meta` | 13 / 18 | 500 | 0 | body | Card footer, secondary text |
| `text-xs` | 12 / 16 | 600 | 0 | body | filter chips |
| `text-2xs` | 11 / 15 | 600 | 0.05em, upper case for tags | body | category tags, priority pills, count badges, section eyebrows |

   THE Design_System SHALL retire `--text-heading` (16) and `--text-lg` (17), which the prototype does not use, and the restyle SHALL move each use to the nearest entry above.
9. [DS-09] THE Frontend SHALL use only the weights 400, 500, 600, and 700 through Tailwind's `font-normal`, `font-medium`, `font-semibold`, and `font-bold`, and SHALL NOT use a weight, size, tracking, or line-height utility in a component where a `text-*` entry of criterion 8 already sets it.
10. [DS-10] THE Frontend SHALL set the heading family (`text-display`, `text-xl`, `text-title`, `text-page-title`) in Plus Jakarta Sans, through a `--font-heading` token (Decision AR).
11. [DS-11] THE Frontend SHALL set every other text in Inter through `--font-stack`. BOTH families SHALL be self-hosted from the application bundle with `@fontsource-variable/plus-jakarta-sans` and `@fontsource-variable/inter`, loaded with `font-display: swap`, and the Frontend SHALL make no request to a third-party font or icon service (Requirement 14 criterion 17, amended). THE landing page SHALL load the same two families and no other.

**Shape, depth, and size**

12. [DS-12] THE Design_System SHALL define the radius scale, measured from the prototype, by role (Decision AS): `--radius-sm` 8 pixels (a security note, a small button), `--radius-field` 12 pixels (auth and form fields, the auth tab switch, the auth submit button, inner rows), `--radius-card` 16 pixels (Cards, Card-like rows, the Board_Switcher trigger, textareas, the "Add Card" button, the landing preview's inner cards), `--radius-frame` 24 pixels (the app frame, Columns, the filter bar, modals, the Side_Panel, the auth card, the landing feature cards and preview), and `--radius-full` (pill buttons, chips, badges, tags, nav items, avatars, the Icon_Rail). It SHALL retire `--radius-md`, `--radius-lg`, and the 6 pixel `--radius-card` of V7.
13. [DS-13] THE Frontend SHALL apply the radius roles as the table of criterion 18 states, and SHALL write no radius as a literal or an arbitrary value; a component picks a role only. The prototype's 28 and 36 pixel radii (page 3) and 32 pixel radius (page 4) collapse to `--radius-frame`, and its 6 pixel radius (page 4 nav) to `--radius-sm`.
14. [DS-14] THE Frontend SHALL draw the Icon_Rail, buttons, chips, tags, badges, avatars, and the Top_Nav track as full pills or circles, as the prototype does.
15. [DS-15] THE Design_System SHALL keep the spacing scale 4, 8, 12, 16, 20, 24, 32, and 48 pixels (`--space-1` to `--space-12`) on an 8 pixel grid, and SHALL add `--page-x` (16 pixels below 960 pixels, 24 pixels from 960 pixels) as the inner padding of the app frame, and `--gutter` (12 pixels, 16 pixels from 960 pixels) as the gap between Columns and between Cards. The prototype names map as: `space-xs` 4, `space-sm` 8, `space-md` 12, `space-lg` 16, `space-xl` 24, `space-2xl` 32, `space-3xl` 48. Section spacing on the landing page uses `--space-12` and `--space-8` only.
16. [DS-16] THE Design_System SHALL define these shadows and no other, measured from the prototype: `--shadow-card` (`0 1px 2px` of `--ink` at 4 to 5 percent: Cards, pill buttons, chips, the app frame, the Icon_Rail), `--shadow-hover` (Card hover), `--shadow-popover` (`0 10px 25px` at 10 percent: popovers, menus, the Board_Switcher panel), `--shadow-dragging`, `--shadow-modal` (`0 25px 50px` at 10 percent: modals), `--shadow-panel` (`0 25px 50px` at 25 percent: the Side_Panel and the auth card), and `--shadow-float` (`0 20px 50px` at 12 percent: the landing product preview). Shadow colour is `--ink` at a token opacity.
17. [DS-17] THE Design_System SHALL add `--size-header` 64 pixels (the App_Header height from 960 pixels), `--size-icon-lg` 24 pixels, `--size-tile` 48 pixels (an icon tile), `--size-panel` 460 pixels (the Side_Panel width), `--container-site` 80rem (the landing content width), and `--container-copy` 42rem (a paragraph column); keep `--rail-w` at 64 pixels; and define the control sizes the prototype uses, replacing the 40 and 32 pixel pair of Requirement 14 criterion 20 and V13: `--size-control` 40 pixels (fields, the Top_Nav track, rail items, "Add Card"), `--size-button` 36 pixels (a primary pill button), `--size-control-sm` 32 pixels (a Top_Nav item, an avatar), `--size-pill` 28 pixels (a compact pill button such as "Public link"), and `--size-chip` 24 pixels (a filter chip). Below 960 pixels every icon-only control and every pill below 44 pixels SHALL keep a hit area of `--size-touch` (44 pixels) in both directions through an expanded invisible target, without changing how it looks.
18. [DS-18] THE Frontend SHALL build every shared component to this table (all values measured on the canonical pages), so that sizes and corners relate the same way on every page. A component picks the row it belongs to and adds no value of its own:

| Component | Height | Radius | Padding | Type | Fill | Shadow |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| App frame | auto | frame | 0; content `--page-x` | n/a | `--surface-200` | card |
| Icon_Rail | 64 wide, full height of the canvas minus its margin | full | y 24 | n/a | `--surface-200` | card |
| Rail item | 40 | full | none | n/a | none; active `--accent` with `--on-accent` icon; inactive icon `--ink-muted` | none |
| Rail home circle | 44 | full | none | n/a | `--accent`, icon `--on-accent` | popover |
| App_Header | `--size-header` | none | x `--page-x` | n/a | `--surface-200` | none |
| Top_Nav | track 40 | full | track 4 | n/a | track `--surface-100` | none |
| Top_Nav item | 32 | full | x 16 | `text-sm` medium | none; active `--surface-sunken` with semibold `--ink` | none |
| Button primary | `--size-button` | full | x 16, y 8 | `text-sm` medium | `--accent` on `--on-accent` | card |
| Button dark | `--size-pill` | full | x 16 | `text-sm` medium | `--brand` on `--on-brand` | card |
| Button quiet | `--size-pill` | full | x 12 | `text-sm` medium | `--surface-100` on `--ink` | card |
| Button danger | as the button it sits with | full | as above | as above | `--danger-soft` on `--on-danger-soft`; solid `--danger` on `--on-danger` for a confirm | none |
| Auth submit | 40, full width | field | x 16 | `text-sm` medium | `--brand` on `--on-brand` | modal |
| Icon button | 40 or 32 square | full | none | n/a | none, `--surface-sunken` on hover | none |
| Input, `BaseCombobox` trigger, date field | 40 | field (card in a modal) | x 16 (x 40 left when it has a leading icon) | `text-sm` | `--surface-100`, no border | none |
| Textarea | at least 80 | card | 12 by 16 | `text-sm` | `--surface-100` | none |
| Filter chip | `--size-chip` | full | x 12 | `text-xs` | `--surface-200`; active `--brand` on `--on-brand` | card |
| Category tag | auto | full | x 8, y 2 | `text-2xs` upper case | `--surface-sunken` on `--ink` | none |
| Priority pill | auto | full | x 8, y 2 | `text-2xs` | High `--danger-soft` on `--on-danger-soft`; Med `--accent-tint` on `--on-accent-tint`; Low `--surface-sunken` on `--ink-muted`; each with a 13 pixel flag icon | none |
| Count badge | auto | full | x 8, y 2 | `text-2xs` | `--surface-strong` on `--ink-muted`; teal variant `--accent-tint` on `--on-accent-tint` | none |
| Role tag, status pill | auto | full | x 8, y 2 | `text-2xs` | a `--label-*` pair, `--accent-tint`, `--surface-sunken`, or `--danger-soft` | none |
| Board Card | auto | card | 16 | `text-card-title`, `text-sm`, `text-meta` | `--surface-200` | card |
| Card progress bar | 4 | full | n/a | n/a | track `--surface-sunken`, fill `--accent` (`--danger` for an overdue Card) | none |
| Column | auto, width `--column-w` | frame | 12 | `text-page-title` title | `--surface-100` | none |
| "Add Card" | `--size-control` | card | none | `text-sm` medium `--ink-muted` | `--surface-200` | card |
| Filter bar | auto | frame | 12 | n/a | `--surface-100` | none |
| Board_Switcher trigger | auto | card | 6 by 12 | `text-xl` | `--surface-100` | card |
| KPI tile, Home tile | auto | frame | 24 | `text-xl` value, `text-meta` label | `--surface-200`, `--border` | card |
| Popover, menu, Board_Switcher panel | auto | frame | 12 | `text-sm` | `--surface-200` | popover |
| Tooltip | auto | sm | y 4, x 8 | `text-xs` | `--ink` fill, `--surface-200` text | none |
| `BaseModal` | auto | frame | 24 | title `text-title` | `--surface-200` | modal |
| Modal scrim | full screen | n/a | n/a | n/a | `--scrim` with `--blur-overlay` | none |
| Side_Panel | full height minus a 24 pixel inset, `--size-panel` wide | frame | header 24, body 16 | title `text-title` | `--surface-200` | panel |
| Panel row | auto | card | 14 | `text-sm` | unread `--surface-100`, read none | none |
| Segmented switch | 40 | field (card in a panel) | 4 | `text-sm` medium | track `--surface-100`; active item `--surface-200` | card |
| Auth card | auto | frame | 0 | n/a | `--surface-200` | panel |
| Auth brand panel | full height of the card | frame on its left corners | 48 | title `text-display` | `--gradient-brand`, text `--on-brand`, secondary `--on-brand-muted` | none |
| Avatar | 24, 28, 32, or 40 | full | n/a | `text-2xs` bold initials | `--brand` or `--accent` or `--brand-soft` with `--on-brand` initials | none |
| Progress meter (Board_Toolbar) | 6 | full | n/a | n/a | track `--surface-sunken`, fill `--accent` | none |
| Landing section | auto | none | y 48, x `--page-x` | `text-xl` title, `text-base` copy | canvas or `--surface-100` band | none |
| Landing feature card | auto | frame | 24 | `text-page-title`, `text-sm` | `--surface-100` | none |
| Landing product preview | auto | frame | 24 | the app's own components | `--surface-200`, `--border` | float |
| Landing CTA band | auto | card | 32 | `text-xl` | `--gradient-cta`, text `--on-brand` | popover |
| FAQ item | auto | card | 16 | `text-sm` medium question | `--surface-200`, `--border` | none |

   Numbers in the table are pixels and stand for the matching token (`--size-*`, `--space-*`, `--radius-*`); a component uses the token, never the number.

**Components and rules**

19. [DS-19] THE Frontend SHALL draw every icon with `lucide-react` at `--size-icon-sm` (14), 13 pixels for the priority flag, `--size-icon` (18), or `--size-icon-lg` (24), and SHALL NOT load the Material Symbols font the prototype uses. THE Frontend SHALL map each prototype icon to the lucide icon of the same meaning in the task that restyles that screen.
20. [DS-20] THE Frontend SHALL give every interactive component these states from the Design_System and no other: hover (`--surface-sunken` on quiet controls, `--brand-strong` on dark, `--accent-strong` on primary), pressed (the hover colour, no scale), focus-visible (the ring of Requirement 14 criterion 15), disabled (`--opacity-disabled`, no pointer events), and a transition of `--duration-fast` on colour, background, and shadow only.
21. [DS-21] THE Frontend SHALL translate the prototype only through the tables above: a prototype class such as `text-[22px]`, `bg-[#006B5F]`, `w-2 h-2`, `rounded-3xl`, `shadow-[0_20px_50px_rgba(15,25,60,0.12)]`, or `py-3.5` is never copied; the nearest token utility is used instead, and a value with no token is a Design_System change made through this requirement.
22. [DS-22] THE Frontend SHALL keep the Tailwind theme cleared of defaults (`--color-*`, `--spacing-*`, `--radius-*`, `--shadow-*`, `--container-*`, `--breakpoint-*`, `--text-*`, `--font-*`, `--ease-*`, `--blur-*`) and expose only the tokens of this requirement, and SHALL keep two breakpoints: `lg` at 960 pixels and `xl` at 1280 pixels.
23. [DS-23] THE Design_System SHALL ship one light theme. THE Frontend SHALL NOT enable `dark:` variants.
24. [DS-24] THE landing page, the sign-in pages, and every Protected_Route page SHALL pass the same style checks of Requirement 44 (no hex, no arbitrary value, no numeric layer, duration, or opacity, no raw `<button>`, no `max-*` variant), and the landing components SHALL be built from `Base*` components and the recipes of criterion 18.

**Reference and fidelity**

25. [DS-25] THE repository SHALL keep the renders of the prototype in `design/reference/v2/` (desktop at 1440 pixels and mobile at 390 pixels for each of the 13 pages, with the landing page cut into three desktop slices) together with a `README.md` that names the source page of each image and lists what the image shows that V16 does not build (exclusions 1 to 17 of `specs/v16/00-OVERVIEW.md`). THE Frontend task that restyles a screen SHALL compare the result with the images of that screen before it is ticked (Requirement 44, V16-T15).
26. [DS-26] THE Frontend SHALL redraw pages 3, 4, 6, 7, 8, and 9 of the prototype with the canonical recipes of criterion 18 and the layout of Requirement 40: the slate and emerald colours of those pages map to `--ink-muted`, `--surface-100`, `--surface-sunken`, and `--accent`; their 12, 28, 32, and 36 pixel radii map to the radius roles; and their 72 to 96 pixel rails and their per-page header heights map to the one Icon_Rail and the one App_Header.
27. [DS-27] THE Frontend SHALL test the recipe table in a real browser (Requirement 44, V16-T16): for each row that has a measurable height, radius, padding, font size, weight, and fill, the computed style of the built component equals the token value, so that drift from the prototype is caught by a test and not by eye.
**Column width, public board, rich text (round 6 within V18)**
28. [DS-28] THE Column width `--column-w` SHALL be a share of the board row, which is a size container: below 960 pixels one Column and a peek of the next; from 960 pixels three Columns and half of the fourth (`(100cqw - 2 x --page-x - 3 x --gutter) / 3.5`). Fewer Columns grow to fill the row; the row keeps `--page-x` on both ends while it scrolls (amends DS-21; `--column-w-min` is removed, no token is added). THE public board (Shared_View) SHALL use the same frame, header line, eyebrow and `h1`, filter toolbar, Column row, Column header, and Card tiles as the Board, with a "Read only" badge and a Sign in or Go to Home button in its header; no token is added for it.
29. [DS-29] THE Board description and the Card description SHALL be rich text edited with TinyMCE, self-hosted from `/tinymce` (copied from `node_modules` by `scripts/copy-tinymce.mjs` before `dev` and `build`; no cloud key; the GPL licence key). THE editor SHALL offer only: paragraph, heading, subheading, bold, italic, underline, strikethrough, bulleted and numbered lists, link, quote, and clear formatting; its content area SHALL be styled from the design tokens at runtime and its frame SHALL use the one radius, the border token, and the inset focus ring. THE Backend SHALL sanitise every description with one allowlist (`app/common/rich_text.py`, `nh3`: `p br strong em u s ul ol li a h2 h3 blockquote code pre`, `href` and `title` on links, `http`, `https`, `mailto` only, `rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"`), store `null` for an empty editor, limit a Board description to 500 visible characters, and a Card description to 20000 characters of HTML. THE Frontend SHALL sanitise again before display (DOMPurify, same allowlist) and SHALL show plain words (no tags) in Card tiles, Board tiles, and search. THE migration `d4a7e1c9b215` SHALL make `boards.description` TEXT and turn every existing plain-text description into one escaped paragraph with its line breaks. The Card description saves on blur (Requirement 25).
**Editor inside the Card modal, option rows, board edge (round 7 within V18)**
30. [DS-30] THE TinyMCE dialogs and menus (rendered outside every modal) SHALL work inside the Card modal: a click, a focus, or an Escape in them SHALL NOT close the Card modal, the modal's focus trap SHALL NOT pull focus out of their fields, and Cancel, the close button, Escape, and Save SHALL close only the TinyMCE dialog. THEIR look SHALL follow the system (the one radius, the app fonts, `page-title` for the dialog title, the accent primary button). THE editor frame SHALL show one focus ring (the ring colour on the border token width), and TinyMCE's own inner edit-area border SHALL be off.
31. [DS-31] EVERY option row of a list (`BaseCombobox`, command menu) SHALL be at least `--control-h` high with `px-3`, `py-2`, and `gap-3`, so a rich row (avatar, name, email, role tag) has room; the assignee list SHALL use the wide popover and the `md` avatar.
32. [DS-32] THE board's Column row SHALL be inset by `--page-x` on both sides as a scroll area, so the Columns are clipped at the same right edge as the toolbar above them; the Column width SHALL be computed from that inset area (`(100cqw - 3 x --gutter) / 3.5` from 960 pixels), and the public board SHALL do the same (amends DS-28).

### Requirement 39: App Shell

Note (V17): the navigation items, the Icon_Rail, the Top_Nav, and the Team_View destination are superseded by Requirement 47 (Decision BA); the App_Header, Board_Switcher, progress pill, Skip link, and Side_Panel look stand.
**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want the prototype's icon rail, pill navigation, and board dropdown, so that I can reach every screen and switch Boards from a familiar, balanced layout.

The prototype draws both an icon rail and a top pill nav, and places a logo in each. V16 keeps both because the owner chose the prototype shell (Open Question 39), but gives the logo one place, the App_Header, so the brand is not shown twice. The shell looks as the references in `design/reference/v2/` show it (canonical pages 1, 10, 11, 12, and 13) and is sized by Requirement 38. This requirement replaces the Sidebar (Decision AT). Behaviour of the Inbox, Activity, Categories, members, invites, and public link is unchanged; only where it is reached and how it looks changes.

#### Acceptance Criteria

**Navigation**

1. [SHELL-01] WHILE the viewport is 960 pixels or wider, THE Frontend SHALL show the Icon_Rail on every Protected_Route page as the prototype draws it: a white pill, `--rail-w` wide, floating at the left of the canvas with a `--page-x` margin above, below, and at its left, and a `--space-4` gap to the app frame (reference `01-board-desktop.png`). It holds, from the top: the Home circle (`--accent`, 44 pixels, white icon, links to `/boards`, accessible name "Home"), then the items Board (only while a Board is open; links to that Board), Dashboard, Inbox, Activity, Categories, and Team, in this order and with nothing at the bottom. Which items appear for which role is criterion 5.  (V17: superseded by NAV-01 to NAV-04, Requirement 47.)
2. [SHELL-02] THE Frontend SHALL draw each Icon_Rail item as a 40 pixel circle holding a `lucide-react` icon in `--ink-muted`, with an accessible name equal to its label and a `BaseTooltip` showing the label on hover and on keyboard focus only; WHEN the pointer leaves the item or it loses focus, THE Frontend SHALL hide the tooltip, and a click SHALL NOT leave it showing. THE active item SHALL be a filled `--accent` circle with a `--on-accent` icon (Requirement 38 criterion 18, Rail item) and, for a route item, `aria-current="page"`; the Inbox, Activity, and Team items are active while the Side_Panel shows their view.  (V17: superseded by NAV-02 for the Sidebar items; the tooltip rule stands.)
3. [SHELL-03] WHILE the viewport is 960 pixels or wider, THE Frontend SHALL show the Top_Nav in the App_Header, centred between the logo and the bell, with the items Boards, Dashboard, Inbox, Activity, Categories, and Team as text labels, drawn as the pill track of Requirement 38 criterion 18 (reference `01-board-desktop.png`): the active item is a `--surface-sunken` pill with semibold text; the Inbox item shows the unread count as a teal count badge while the count is above zero, capped at "9+".  (V17: superseded by NAV-04 and NAV-08; the Top_Nav is removed.)
4. [SHELL-04] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL hide the Icon_Rail and the Top_Nav and show in the persistent App_Header a menu button (44 pixels, accessible name "Open menu", `aria-expanded`) that opens a Nav_Drawer: an off-canvas panel from the left behind a scrim, built as `BaseDrawer` (a new shared wrapper over the shadcn/ui `Dialog`, added to the list of Requirement 14 criterion 10), listing the same items as 44 pixel rows with icon and label, then the Board_Switcher list of criterion 13. THE Nav_Drawer SHALL close on a route change, on a scrim click, and on Escape, trap focus while open, and return focus to the menu button.
5. [SHELL-05] THE Frontend SHALL show each item only to the roles that may use it: Boards (Top_Nav and drawer) and the Home circle (rail) to every signed-in user, and the rail's Board item to every user while a Board is open; Dashboard to a Supervisor and a Manager (Requirement 10); Inbox and Activity to every signed-in user; Categories to a Supervisor, a Manager, and an Admin, and only while a Board is open (Requirement 6 criterion 11); Team to every signed-in user while a Board is open, and to an Admin always (criterion 21). An item that is not shown takes no space.  (V17: superseded by NAV-01 and NAV-04, Requirement 47.)
6. [SHELL-06] WHEN a user activates Categories, THE Frontend SHALL open the Categories screen of the Board that is open, as before.  (V17: superseded by NAV-07: Categories is a Side_Panel view.)
7. [SHELL-07] WHEN a user activates Inbox, or the bell in the App_Header, THE Frontend SHALL toggle the Side_Panel on the Inbox view through the same handler (Requirement 9 criterion 6, with "Top_Nav or Icon_Rail" for "Sidebar"). THE bell SHALL show the unread dot of V12 while the unread count is above zero.  (V17: superseded by NAV-06: the bell is the only way to the Inbox.)
8. [SHELL-08] WHEN a user activates Activity or Team, THE Frontend SHALL open the Side_Panel on that view; the Side_Panel shows one view at a time, a second activation of the open view closes it, and Escape closes it and returns focus to the item that opened it (Requirement 8 criterion 6, with "Top_Nav or Icon_Rail" for "Sidebar").  (V17: superseded by NAV-04 and NAV-07: Activity and Members from the Top_Menu.)

**Header and layout**

9. [SHELL-09] THE Frontend SHALL render the App_Header on every Protected_Route page (HDR-01), inside the app frame, as its first row. WHILE the viewport is 960 pixels or wider, THE App_Header SHALL hold, left to right, the `BrandLogo` link to `/boards`, the Top_Nav, a flexible gap, the bell (a 40 pixel icon button with the teal unread dot), and the User_Menu trigger (a 32 pixel avatar and a chevron); it SHALL NOT hold the page context, a search button, or a global search field (exclusions 1). WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE App_Header SHALL hold the menu button, the `BrandLogo` link, the bell, and the User_Menu trigger, and on a Board SHALL show the Board name in place of the wordmark as V12 does.
10. [SHELL-10] THE Frontend SHALL show `BrandLogo` with the variant `full` in the App_Header while the viewport is 960 pixels or wider, standing where the prototype draws its logo tile and wordmark, and with the variant `mark` below 960 pixels (Requirement 21, BRAND-07 and BRAND-10 as V15 rewords them); THE Icon_Rail SHALL NOT show the logo. THE Frontend SHALL NOT draw the prototype's lightning tile or the word RevoTask.
11. [SHELL-11] THE Frontend SHALL lay a page out as the prototype does: a `--surface-100` canvas with a `--page-x` margin on every side, the Icon_Rail at the left, and one app frame (white, radius `frame`, `--shadow-card`) holding the App_Header on top and the page below it. On a Board the page itself does not scroll (Requirement 14 criterion 18); the Columns scroll inside the frame; on every other page the frame's content scrolls and keeps `--page-x` padding. WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE frame SHALL fill the screen with no canvas margin and no radius, and there is no Icon_Rail.
12. [SHELL-12] WHEN a Board page is open, THE Frontend SHALL show in the Board_Toolbar, as its first element, the eyebrow "ACTIVE BOARD" (`text-2xs`, upper case, `--ink-muted`, with an `--accent` dot), and under it the Board_Switcher trigger: the Board name in `text-xl` with a chevron, on the Board_Switcher trigger recipe, with the accessible name "Switch board: <name>", opening a `BasePopover` panel on the popover recipe (reference `12-board-switcher-desktop.png`). Under the trigger a line `text-sm` `--ink-muted` reads "<n> Columns · <m> Total Tasks · <description>", from the Board payload, with the description omitted when empty.

**Board_Switcher**

13. [SHELL-13] THE Board_Switcher panel SHALL list every Board the viewer may see (Requirement 22 criterion 4) and, for each: the name, a role tag (`BaseBadge`) reading "Owner" when the viewer is the Board_Owner and otherwise the viewer's role, the card count ("<n> cards"), and the percentage done (`done_count` over `card_count`, rounded, shown as "<p>%", and "0%" when there are no Cards), from the `GET /api/boards` payload; THE panel SHALL mark the open Board with `aria-current="true"`, show a search field when the list has six or more Boards (Requirement 14 criterion 23), show no visibility icon (ACC-14) and no Email, and navigate to `/boards/{id}` on selection, closing the panel and returning focus to the trigger.
14. [SHELL-14] THE Frontend SHALL wrap a long Board name onto further lines in the Board_Switcher panel and the Board_Toolbar instead of cutting it off.
15. [SHELL-15] WHERE the viewer may create a Board (Requirement 3), THE Board_Switcher panel SHALL end with a "Create new board" button that opens the existing New Board modal; WHEN a Board is created, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to it as it does today.

**Board_Toolbar**

16.
