[SHELL-16] THE Board_Toolbar SHALL show, inside the filter bar at its right, the progress pill "<done> of <total> done (<p>%)" for the open Board while it has at least one Card: a white pill with a trend icon, the text with the percentage in `--accent`, and a 100 pixel meter (Requirement 38 criterion 18, Progress meter) with `role="progressbar"`, `aria-valuenow` the percentage, and an accessible name; THE figures come from the Board payload's `done_count` and `card_count` and are never computed from another source.
17. [SHELL-17] THE Board_Toolbar SHALL hold, at the right of the Board_Switcher row, the "Public link" and "Invite" quiet pill buttons (`--size-pill`, with an icon; "Invite" shows the pending join-request count as a teal count badge when the viewer decides on requests) with the same visibility rules and behaviour as before (Requirements 23, 24, and 31), icon-only below 960 pixels, and SHALL hold no other action: there is no board-level "Add Task" button (exclusions 17).  (V17: superseded by SHR-01 and NAV-08: one Share item and modal, Requirement 55.)
18. [SHELL-18] WHILE the viewport is between 360 and 414 pixels wide, THE Board_Toolbar SHALL wrap onto a second row so that nothing is cut off or overlaps, and the page SHALL NOT scroll sideways.

**Team view**

19. [SHELL-19] THE Side_Panel Team_View SHALL list the members of the open Board, the Board_Owner first with the "Owner" tag, then the others in the order of `GET /api/boards/{id}/members`; each row SHALL show the avatar through `BaseAvatar`, the full name, the Username muted, and the role tag of Requirement 30. The row SHALL show no Email except where Requirement 3 criterion 31 allows it.  (V17: superseded by NAV-07: the Members view; the row content stands.)
20. [SHELL-20] WHERE the viewer is the Board_Owner, a Manager, or an Admin, THE Team_View SHALL show the member search field that adds a member and the remove action on each member, with the rules and confirmations of Requirements 3 and 22; a Member and a Supervisor who is not the owner see the list only.
21. [SHELL-21] WHEN an Admin activates Team, THE Frontend SHALL navigate to `/admin/users` instead of opening the Team_View, because an Admin has no Board access by default (Decision B, Open Question 40).  (V17: superseded by NAV-07 and Open Question 61: an Admin opens the Members view.)

**Removal and boundaries**

22. [SHELL-22] THE Frontend SHALL remove the Sidebar, its collapse toggle, the stored collapse state, and its Boards list and Team members section; THE Frontend SHALL NOT keep a second navigation component beside the Icon_Rail, the Top_Nav, and the Nav_Drawer.  (V17: superseded by NAV-08: the Sidebar is back with a new meaning; the Icon_Rail and Top_Nav are removed.)
23. [SHELL-23] THE Frontend SHALL show no Log out item and no Profile entry in the Icon_Rail, the Top_Nav, or the Nav_Drawer; both stay in the User_Menu (HDR-02 to HDR-05).
24. [SHELL-24] THE Frontend SHALL show no Icon_Rail, no Top_Nav, no Nav_Drawer, and no Side_Panel on the sign-in pages, the invite pages' signed-out states, the session-expired route, and the Shared_View (SHARE-21 extended); the Shared_View keeps its own light header with `BrandLogo` and "Sign in".
25. [SHELL-25] THE Frontend SHALL make the first focusable element of every Protected_Route page a "Skip to content" link that moves focus to the main region, and SHALL order focus: skip link, App_Header, Icon_Rail, main region.
26. [SHELL-26] THE Side_Panel SHALL keep its behaviour (right side, one shared panel, closes on Escape) and SHALL be drawn as the prototype does (references `11-inbox-desktop.png` and `13-activity-desktop.png`): a floating white panel `--size-panel` wide, inset `--page-x` from the top, right, and bottom of the viewport, radius `frame`, `--shadow-panel`, over a blurred `--scrim` that also covers the Icon_Rail and the App_Header; below 960 pixels it fills the screen width. Its header is a 36 pixel icon tile (`--accent-tint`, radius `card`), the title ("Inbox", "Activity", or "Team") in `text-title`, an optional teal count badge, and a close button.

### Requirement 40: Screen Restyle

**User Story:** As a user, I want every existing screen to look like the prototype, so that the product feels like one design while it keeps every feature it has today.

This requirement changes how existing screens look and where their controls sit. It adds no feature except where another V16 requirement says so. Every behaviour, permission, error message, and API call of Requirements 1 to 37 stands unless a criterion below says otherwise. Each screen is specified against its reference render in `design/reference/v2/` (Requirement 38 criterion 25), and all sizes, radii, fills, and type come from the recipe table of Requirement 38 criterion 18; this requirement fixes what goes where.

| Prototype page | Reference render | Screen |
|---|---|---|
| 1, 10, 12 | `01-board`, `11-create-board`, `13-board-switcher` | Board page, New Board modal, Board_Switcher |
| 2 | `02-landing` | Landing page (Requirement 41) |
| 3 | `03-dashboard` | Dashboard (Requirement 43) |
| 4 | `04-card-modal` | Card modal |
| 5 | `05-auth` | Sign-in and register |
| 6 | `06-invite-public-link` | Invite and Public link modals |
| 7 | `07-public-board` | Shared view |
| 8 | `08-user-management` | Admin users |
| 9 | `09-categories` | Categories (its render shows broken icon fonts; the layout is the intent) |
| 11 | `12-inbox` | Inbox |
| 13 | `13-activity` | Activity |

#### Acceptance Criteria

**All screens**

1. [SCR-01] THE Frontend SHALL restyle each screen below only through the Design_System (Requirement 38): the component recipes, the colour, type, radius, and shadow tokens, and `lucide-react` icons. A screen SHALL NOT keep a V15 or earlier value that Requirement 38 replaced (the 6 pixel card radius, the `#13226b` brand, the `#f3f4fa` page colour, the 16 and 17 pixel text sizes, the 40 pixel button height).
2. [SCR-02] THE Frontend SHALL keep each screen's content, order, empty state, loading state, error state, and Retry control, and SHALL restyle them with the same recipes; a state the prototype does not draw (empty, loading, error, confirm) is drawn from the same recipes and not left in its old style.
3. [SCR-03] THE Frontend SHALL keep every screen free of horizontal scroll and cut-off content between 360 and 414 pixels wide, with the 44 pixel hit area of Requirement 38 criterion 17 on every small control. The prototype's mobile renders (`*-mobile.png`) keep the 64 pixel rail and scroll sideways; V16 does not copy that: below 960 pixels the rail becomes the Nav_Drawer (SHELL-04) and the app frame fills the screen (SHELL-11).

**Sign-in and register [page 5, `05-auth`]**

4. [SCR-04] THE Frontend SHALL draw `/login` and `/register` as one auth card centred on the canvas, at most 1152 pixels wide (`--container-site` clamps it), radius `frame`, `--shadow-panel`, in two halves from 960 pixels. The left half is the auth brand panel (`--gradient-brand`, two soft `--accent-tint` glow circles behind the text): `BrandLogo` `full` on a translucent white chip at the top left, the `text-display` line "Work in flow, scale with clarity." under it, and at the bottom a thin divider and one `text-sm` line in `--on-brand-muted`. The right half holds, from the top: the segmented switch ("Sign in" and "Create account", each a link to its own route, Requirement 1), the form title in `text-xl`, one `text-sm` `--ink-muted` line, the fields, the submit button, and the security note row. Below 960 pixels the brand panel is a compact band above the form inside the same card.
5. [SCR-05] THE register form SHALL keep the separate full name, Username, Email, and password fields with the blur availability check and the 8 character rule (Requirement 1, USR-07 and USR-08). Each field is a labelled input (label `text-meta` semibold above, a leading `lucide-react` icon inside at the left) on the Input recipe; "Available" or the taken message shows as a status pill beside the Username and Email labels; the password field has a show or hide eye button inside at the right. THE form SHALL NOT show a Terms checkbox, an "Entropy score", a "Workspace Ready" chip, or a single "Username or work email" field (exclusions 7).
6. [SCR-06] THE sign-in form SHALL keep the Username-or-email field and the password field with the show or hide toggle, the 429 countdown, and the Safe_Return_Path, on the same recipes as criterion 5.

**Home and board [pages 1, 10, 12, `01-board`, `11-create-board`, `13-board-switcher`]**

7. [SCR-07] THE Home page (`/boards`) SHALL sit in the app frame under the App_Header with a `text-title` title "Boards", the analytics strip of Requirement 10 criterion 8 as KPI tiles in a row above (Supervisor and Manager only), then the Boards as Home tiles in a grid of one column below 960 pixels, two from 960, and three from 1280; each tile shows the name in `text-page-title`, up to two lines of description, the progress meter with the percentage, the card count, and the member avatars (up to four, then a "+N" count badge). It SHALL show no "Welcome Back" hero.
8. [SCR-08] THE Board page SHALL show, inside the app frame under the App_Header and in this order: the Board_Toolbar (SHELL-12, SHELL-16, SHELL-17) with the Board_Switcher row at the left and the "Public link" and "Invite" pills at the right; the filter bar (radius `frame`, `--surface-100`, padding 12) holding at its left the filter chips ("All Tasks" first, active chip filled `--brand`, then one chip per category) and at its right the progress pill and the search field (a white 40 pixel field with a leading search icon, "Search tasks..."), wrapping below 960 pixels; then the Columns in a row that scrolls sideways inside the frame. Each Column is a Column recipe: a header with the tone dot (`--size-dot-md`), the name in `text-page-title`, the card count badge, and the Column menu button at the right; the Cards stacked with `--gutter` between them; and the "Add Card" button at the foot. There is no "Add Task" button and no "Welcome Back" hero (exclusions 2 and 17).
9. [SCR-09] A board Card SHALL be laid out as the prototype draws it: a first row with the category tag at the left and the priority pill at the right; the title (`text-card-title`, up to three lines); up to two lines of description (`text-sm` `--ink-muted`); the progress bar and the subtask count only when the Card has subtasks (Requirement 27); and a footer row of `text-meta` `--ink-muted` items with a 14 pixel icon each (subtasks as done over total, the end date, and the comment and attachment counts when above zero) and the single assignee avatar (28 pixels) at the right. An overdue Card shows its end date in `--danger` with an alert icon and a `--danger` progress bar. A Card in the `Done` Column shows its title with a line through it in `--ink-muted`, a "Completed" pill (`--accent-tint`) in place of the priority pill, and its subtask count in `--accent`. It SHALL NOT show a Card identifier, an estimate, a second assignee, or text the Card does not store ("Re-opened", "Needs QA redo", "PR #842"; exclusions 4 to 6).
10. [SCR-10] THE Frontend SHALL keep drag and drop with its placeholder and the Column auto-scroll (Requirement 5) and SHALL draw a dragged Card with `--shadow-dragging` and the same radius as at rest; the drop placeholder is a `--surface-sunken` block of the Card's height with the Card radius.
11. [SCR-11] THE Card modal SHALL be laid out as the prototype draws it (`04-card-modal-desktop.png`): a `BaseModal` about 900 pixels wide with two panes divided by a one pixel `--border`. Its header holds a breadcrumb (the Board name, a slash, the Column name as a teal pill), an auto-save status pill ("Auto-saved just now" with a cloud icon), and the close button. The left pane holds the eyebrow "CARD TITLE" (`text-2xs`), the title field (`text-xl`), a row of attribute chips (category, priority, dates, assignee; each a `--surface-100` pill of `--size-control-sm` height with a label and a value, opening its popover), the Description section with its helper line, the Subtasks section (a title, the "<n> of <m> done (<p>%)" pill in `--accent-tint`, the progress bar, one `--surface-100` row of radius `field` per subtask with a checkbox and a drag handle, and "Add subtask"), and the Attachments section (file cards of radius `card` with a type tile, the name, the size and age, and a delete button; "Upload file" and "Add link" at the right of the section title). The right pane holds the Discussion title with the comment count, the thread, and the composer pinned at the foot of the pane; the Card's activity list sits under the thread under the eyebrow "ACTIVITY LOG". The footer holds "Created <date> by <name>" at the left and the Archive (quiet) and Delete (danger) buttons at the right. THE Card modal SHALL keep every behaviour of Requirements 4, 7, 25, 26, and 27. The comment marked as priority SHALL be labelled "Priority", not "Pinned"; the composer has no mention, emoji, or attachment control; the modal has no Save, Done, bookmark, or share button.
12. [SCR-12] THE Card modal SHALL show the assignee chip with one avatar and the full name, and the role tag beside each comment author (Requirement 30), and SHALL show a `--accent` check icon on a completed subtask row with the text in `--ink-muted` and struck through, as the prototype does.

**Side panel [pages 11 and 13, `12-inbox`, `13-activity`]**

13. [SCR-13] THE Inbox view SHALL keep the list of Notifications grouped by day, each row a link (Requirement 28), "Mark all as read", and "Load more", drawn as the prototype does: under the header a segmented switch (only "All" and "Unread", each with its count; the Mentions and Assignments tabs are excluded), then a day label (an `--accent` dot, the day in `text-2xs`, and the update count at the right), then the Notification rows on the Panel row recipe: a 36 pixel avatar with a small type badge at its corner, the actor name in `text-sm` semibold, the time at the right, the action line with the Card or Board name in semibold, an `--accent` unread dot, and, on a `board_join_requested` row where Requirement 24 allows it, the Approve (primary pill) and Decline (quiet pill) buttons. It SHALL NOT show quoted comment text, "Reply", "View Task", "Clear Read", or "Notification Settings" (exclusions 9); the whole row is the link.
14. [SCR-14] THE Activity view SHALL keep the Board activity feed with "Load more" and the existing wording of each action (Requirement 8), drawn as the prototype does: a day label ("TODAY", "EARLIER"), then one row per entry on the Panel row recipe with a 28 pixel actor avatar, the actor name semibold, the time at the right, the action line, and, for a move, the two Column names as pills with an arrow between them. It SHALL NOT show the "Live Stream" chip, the events, active-users, and system-state tiles, the filter field and filter chips, an export button, or a retention line (exclusions 10).

**Management screens [pages 6, 8, 9, `06-invite-public-link`, `08-user-management`, `09-categories`]**

15. [SCR-15] THE Categories screen (`/boards/{id}/categories`) SHALL stay a per-Board add, rename, recolour, and delete screen (Requirement 6), drawn in the prototype's layout: a title block (eyebrow, `text-xl` title "Categories", one line of `text-sm` copy) with the "New category" primary pill at its right, then one table card (radius `frame`, `--surface-200`) whose header row is `text-2xs` upper case labels and whose rows hold a colour swatch (the category's `--label-*` colour as a 16 pixel circle), the name in `text-sm` semibold, and Edit and Delete icon buttons at the right; the create and edit form is a `BaseModal` with a name field and a row of swatches. It SHALL NOT show KPI tiles, the quick-filter chips, "Associated boards", "Automated rules", "Import", an archived toggle, or a hex-code column (exclusions of V15).
16. [SCR-16] THE Invite modal and the Public link modal SHALL keep the controls and rules of Requirements 23 and 24, drawn in the prototype's layout (`06-invite-public-link-desktop.png`): a `BaseModal` with a 3 pixel `--accent` to `--brand` strip at its top edge, an icon tile and the title "Board access" with the Board name as a pill and one line of copy, a two-item segmented switch ("Invite link" with the pending count badge, and "Public link" with an `--accent` dot while it is on), then the active tab: the link card (`--surface-100`, radius `card`) with a status pill ("Active"), the read-only link field (a leading link icon and a monospace `text-sm` value) and a "Copy link" dark pill, then "Regenerate" (quiet) and "Disable" (danger) with the one-line warning; under it, for the Invite tab, the "Pending join requests" section with its count pill and one row per request (a 40 pixel avatar, the full name, the Username muted, the Email where Requirement 24 allows it, the request age, and the Approve and Reject pills). The footer holds a "Done" dark pill. It SHALL NOT show view counts, "CDN Cached", domain restrictions, a department tag, an online dot, a "Settings" link, an "Access logs audited" line, or an invite role picker (exclusions 12).
17. [SCR-17] THE Admin users screen (`/admin/users`) SHALL keep list, create, edit, and role change under the rules of Requirement 1 (criteria 7 and 8 and USR-12), drawn in the prototype's layout (`08-user-management-desktop.png`): a title block with the "Invite member" primary pill replaced by "Add user" (it opens the existing create form), a toolbar card (radius `card`, `--surface-100`) holding the search field and the role filter chips ("All", "Admins", "Members", and so on), then one table card whose header row is `text-2xs` upper case labels and whose rows hold the avatar, the full name and Email, the role as a role tag, and an Edit icon button; below 960 pixels the rows stack as cards. It SHALL NOT show the three summary tiles, seat quota, "Upgrade License", 2FA, Suspended and Invited states, "Last active", a "Board access" column, "Export Directory", or the multi-email invitation (exclusions 13).
18. [SCR-18] THE New Board modal SHALL be drawn as the prototype does (`11-create-board-desktop.png`): a `BaseModal` about 480 pixels wide with the title "Create new board" and one line of copy, a "Board name" field with a required mark, an optional "Description" textarea, a "Default workflow columns" row that shows the five Column names as white pills in a `--surface-100` bar (read-only), and a footer with the Cancel (quiet) and "Create board" (primary) pills. It SHALL NOT show an owner select (exclusions 14).

**Public and profile [page 7, `07-public-board`]**

19. [SCR-19] THE Shared_View (`/shared/{token}`) SHALL stay the read-only Board, drawn as the prototype does: inside the app frame, a banner row (`--accent-tint` fill, "Public read-only view" in `--on-accent-tint` and, at the right, the "Sign in to join" link when the viewer is signed out), a header with `BrandLogo` `full`, a "Public" pill, and a "Sign in" button; under it the Board title block (a lock tile, the Board name in `text-xl`, a "Public view" pill, the description), the filter chips and search field of Requirement 12 with the progress pill, and the Columns and Cards of criterion 8 and 9 without the assignee eye icon and with no drag handle. The Card modal opens read-only without comments and activity (Requirement 18). It SHALL NOT show the Icon_Rail, the Top_Nav, the Overview, Roadmap, and Team tabs, an export button, "Live synced", "Request Access", or card identifiers (exclusions 4 and 11).
20. [SCR-20] THE Profile page SHALL keep the two-column layout from 960 pixels (Requirement 29) inside the app frame, with each section a card of radius `frame` on `--surface-100`, the fields on the Input recipe, and the avatar section drawn with a 64 pixel avatar; the prototype draws no profile page.
21. [SCR-21] THE invite page (`/invite/{token}`) and the "link not valid", session-expired, and not-found pages SHALL be restyled as one centred card (radius `frame`, `--surface-200`, `--shadow-popover`, padding 32) on the `--surface-100` canvas with `BrandLogo` `full`, keeping their text and actions.

**Titles, brand, and states**

22. [SCR-22] THE Frontend SHALL keep every document title of Requirement 32 (`RevoBoard | <name>`) and SHALL name the product RevoBoard everywhere, never RevoTask.
23. [SCR-23] THE Frontend SHALL draw every empty, loading, and error state with the same recipes: an empty list as a centred icon tile (`--accent-tint`, radius `card`), a `text-page-title` line, and one `text-sm` `--ink-muted` line; a loading list as `--surface-strong` skeleton blocks with the radius of the thing they stand for; an error as the same block with the error text in `--danger` and a quiet "Retry" pill.
24. [SCR-24] THE Frontend SHALL draw every confirmation (`BaseConfirm`, Requirement 26) as a popover on the popover recipe with the question, a quiet "Cancel" pill, and a danger or primary confirm pill, and every toast and inline message as a `--surface-100` bar of radius `field` with an icon, with `--danger-soft` and `--accent-tint` as the error and success fills.

### Requirement 41: Landing Page

**User Story:** As a visitor who has not heard of RevoBoard, I want one clear page that says what it is, what it does, and how to start, so that I can decide to create an account.

The page follows prototype page 2 ("Web Landing Page") in structure, tone, and look; the references are `02-landing-desktop-1.png` to `02-landing-desktop-3.png` and `02-landing-mobile.png`. It is built from the same Design_System and the same `Base*` components as the app (Decision AQ), so it has no styles of its own. Where the prototype describes something the product does not do, the page says only what is true (exclusions 15). This requirement resolves Open Question 36 and withdraws its proposal that a signed-in visitor is sent to `/boards`: the Site_Host cannot read the session of the App_Host (Requirement 42), so the page looks the same for everyone.

#### Acceptance Criteria

**Scope and delivery**

1. [LAND-01] THE Frontend SHALL serve the Landing_Page at `/` on the Site_Host as a Server Component that makes no call to the Backend, reads no cookie, and uses no `localStorage`, and whose only client-side script is the menu drawer of criterion 3 (Requirement 14 criterion 11).
2. [LAND-02] THE Landing_Page SHALL hold these parts in this order: header, hero with product preview, Features, Workflow, Teams, Roles, FAQ, closing call to action, footer. Each part from Features to FAQ SHALL have an `id` (`features`, `workflow`, `teams`, `roles`, `faq`) so the header can link to it.

**Header and calls to action**

3. [LAND-03] THE header SHALL be a full-width white bar of `--size-header` height at the top (reference `02-landing-desktop-1.png`) holding, left to right, `BrandLogo` `full` linking to `/`, the anchors Features, Workflow, Teams, and FAQ as plain `text-sm` medium links in `--ink-muted` (the active anchor in `--ink`), a flexible gap, a "Sign in" text link, and a "Get started" dark pill button (`--brand`, `--size-button`, `--shadow-card`); below 960 pixels it holds a menu button that opens a `BaseDrawer` with the same anchors and the same two buttons. The header is sticky.
4. [LAND-04] THE "Sign in" button SHALL link to `{APP_URL}/login` and THE "Get started" button SHALL link to `{APP_URL}/register`, where `APP_URL` is the App_Host origin (Requirement 42 criterion 6). No other link on the page SHALL leave the page except these two and the footer links of criterion 12.

**Hero**

5. [LAND-05] UNDER the header SHALL sit an announcement bar (`--brand` fill, 40 pixels high, centred `text-sm` text in `--on-brand`) holding a teal count-style pill reading "RevoBoard", one line of true copy, "Drag-and-drop boards with roles, share links, and join requests.", and an arrow link "Get started" in `--accent-tint`. THE hero under it SHALL be centred: one `h1` in `text-display` reading "Your workflow, your way." with "Master tasks effortlessly." in `--accent` on the same line from 960 pixels and on the next line below, one paragraph of `text-base` `--ink-muted` copy of at most two sentences (the prototype has none; the page may omit it), the "Get started" dark pill enlarged to 44 pixels high with an arrow icon, and the product preview of criterion 6. It SHALL show no "NEW" chip, no rating, and no customer logos.
6. [LAND-06] THE product preview SHALL render a sample Board inside a window frame (radius `frame`, `--surface-200`, `--border`, `--shadow-float`, padding 24) with the app's own Column and Card components, from fixed sample data held in one typed file under `frontend/lib/` that uses the Backend's Card and Board types. The frame has a top row with three window dots (`--tone-red`, `--tone-orange`, `--tone-green`), the Board name, and at the right the pills "Private board" and "Public link on". Under it a grid of four columns from 960 pixels: a brand card (`--brand` with `--on-brand`, radius `card`) showing the derived board progress and the subtask count with a meter, and a quiet card for the pending join request with Approve and Reject icon buttons; two Columns (In Progress and Review) with their Cards, one Card with subtasks and its derived progress and one overdue Card; and a "Workflow columns" card listing the five Columns with the Card count of each. Every number shown (counts, subtask ratios, percentages) SHALL be computed from the sample data by the same functions the app uses (Requirement 27), so the preview cannot disagree with itself. The preview SHALL be inert (`inert` and `aria-hidden`), have a visible text alternative "Illustration of a RevoBoard board" for assistive technology, and below 960 pixels show the brand card and the first Column only.

**Content**

7. [LAND-07] THE Features part SHALL carry the eyebrow "WHAT YOU GET" (`text-2xs`, upper case, `--accent`), a centred `text-xl` title, one centred `text-base` line, and four feature cards in a row from 1280 pixels (two columns from 960, one below) on the Landing feature card recipe, each with a 48 pixel icon tile (radius `card`; `--accent-tint`, `--brand`, `--surface-sunken`, and `--accent-tint` in turn), a `text-page-title` heading, up to three lines of `text-sm` copy, and a footer line with a one pixel `--border` above it and an arrow link text in `--accent` or `--brand`: (a) drag and drop across five Columns, with the position kept after a refresh; (b) private Boards with a read-only public link and an invite link with join requests; (c) subtasks whose checklist sets the Card's progress; (d) roles, threaded comments, and an activity log. It MAY add a fifth and sixth card for categories with filters and for assignment and deadline notifications, because both exist.
8. [LAND-08] THE Workflow part SHALL show the five default Columns as a numbered row with one short line each: Todo (planned), In Progress (being worked on), Review (waiting for a check), Revision (changes requested), Done (finished). It SHALL NOT claim that a Card moves by itself.
9. [LAND-09] THE Teams part SHALL carry the heading "Private by default. Share when you need to." and these points, and no others: every Board is private to its owner and members; a public link shows the Board read-only, without comments and activity; an invite link sends a join request that the Board's owner, a Manager, or an Admin approves or rejects; every Card move, edit, and comment is recorded in an activity log. From 960 pixels the part is two columns as the prototype draws it (`02-landing-desktop-2.png`): at the left an inert sample card (`--surface-200`, radius `frame`, `--shadow-card`) showing two sample join requests with their Approve and Reject buttons, built from the app's own components and sample data; at the right the eyebrow "SECURE TEAM WORKFLOWS", the heading in `text-xl`, one `text-base` paragraph, and two small cards ("Join request queue" and "Activity history", radius `card`) with an icon, a `text-sm` semibold title, and one line. The prototype's sample card claims features the product does not have (a role-permission checklist and "Revision triggers") and SHALL NOT be copied.
10. [LAND-10] THE Roles part SHALL show one card per role with a line that matches Requirement 2: Member (move and edit Cards, comment), Supervisor (adds create, assign, archive, and delete Cards, Categories, and analytics for their Board), Manager (adds analytics across all Boards, creating Boards, and flagging priority comments), Admin (manages user accounts), and a fifth card "Guest" (reads a Board through a public link). It SHALL NOT name roles the product does not have (for example Owner, Viewer, or Enterprise).
11. [LAND-11] THE FAQ part SHALL show these four questions as native `<details>` elements, in this order: which columns a Board has; how private Boards and join requests work; how a Card's progress is calculated; which roles exist. Each answer SHALL agree with Requirements 2, 3, 24, and 27 and with criteria 8 to 10. The items sit in one centred column at most `--container-copy` wide on the FAQ item recipe, each with a chevron at the right that turns when it is open.
12. [LAND-12] THE closing call to action SHALL be a `--gradient-cta` band (the CTA band recipe) holding one `text-xl` line, "Ready to organize your team?", at the left and, at the right, a "Get started" pill in `--accent-tint` with `--on-accent-tint` text; a second centred call to action under the FAQ SHALL show a 64 pixel `--accent-tint` circle with a rocket icon, the line "Ready to master your workflow?", one `text-base` line, and the two buttons ("Get started" dark, "Sign in" quiet). THE footer SHALL be a `--surface-100` band holding `BrandLogo` `full` with one `text-sm` line, three link columns (Product: Features, Workflow, Teams; Roles: the four roles; Account: Sign in, Create account) that link to anchors and to the App_Host only, and a bottom row "© <current year> RevoBoard". The page SHALL NOT link to pricing, terms, privacy, blog, or contact pages, because none exists, and SHALL NOT show a "Web App Operational" status.

**Truth, search, and quality**

13. [LAND-13] EVERY claim on the Landing_Page SHALL be true of the product as specified in Requirements 1 to 43; a claim that names a feature listed in the exclusions SHALL NOT appear; and the page SHALL show no pricing, tier name, testimonial, usage statistic, or security certification.
14. [LAND-14] THE Landing_Page SHALL set the title `RevoBoard`, a meta description of at most 160 characters, `lang="en"`, a canonical link to `{SITE_URL}/`, and Open Graph tags (`og:title`, `og:description`, `og:type` `website`, `og:url`, `og:site_name`); and THE Site_Host SHALL serve `/robots.txt` allowing all and naming `{SITE_URL}/sitemap.xml`, and a `/sitemap.xml` listing `/` only. THE App_Host SHALL serve a `/robots.txt` that disallows all and every App_Host page SHALL send `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`.
15. [LAND-15] THE Landing_Page SHALL have one `h1`, headings in order without skipped levels, the landmarks `header`, `main`, and `footer`, a visible focus ring on every link and button, a "Skip to content" link as its first focusable element, and the contrast of Requirement 38 criterion 7; anchor jumps SHALL stop below the sticky header through a scroll margin equal to `--size-header`.
16. [LAND-16] WHILE the viewport is between 360 and 414 pixels wide, THE Landing_Page SHALL show every part stacked in one column without horizontal scroll or cut-off content, with touch targets of at least 44 pixels.
17. [LAND-17] THE Landing_Page SHALL load only the two self-hosted font families, the `BrandLogo` files, and the page's own script and style, SHALL contain no image other than the `BrandLogo` files, and SHALL make no third-party request.
18. [LAND-18] THE Landing_Page SHALL use only Design_System tokens and the `Base*` components, and SHALL pass the style checks of Requirement 44 (DS-24).

### Requirement 42: Site Host and App Host

Note (V17): superseded as a whole by Requirement 46 (Path_Topology, Decision AZ). HOST-01 to HOST-15 are history; the Site_Host and App_Host no longer exist.
**User Story:** As the product owner, I want the landing page on the main domain and the app on `app.` under it, so that visitors meet the promotion page first and the app has its own address.

This requirement amends Requirement 33 (DEP-05 and DEP-13) and Requirement 34 (the smoke test). One Next.js process serves both hosts; the `Host` header chooses the page. The Backend, the database, the scheduler, and the Compose or `supervisord` layout do not change. Open Question 38 holds the planned names.

#### Acceptance Criteria

**Hosts and routing**

1. [HOST-01] THE System SHALL serve two hosts from one Frontend process: the Site_Host, which serves the Landing_Page, and the App_Host, which serves the app, the sign-in pages, the shared and invite pages, and `/api` (through Nginx). The planned names are `revoboard.belajardeploy.web.id` (Site_Host) and `app.revoboard.belajardeploy.web.id` (App_Host) (Decision AV).
2. [HOST-02] THE Frontend SHALL route a request by its host and path as follows, in `frontend/proxy.ts` (Requirement 14 criterion 27 and Decision P):

| Host | Path | Result |
|---|---|---|
| Site_Host | `/` | serve the Landing_Page (an internal rewrite to `/site`; the address bar keeps `/`) |
| Site_Host | `/robots.txt`, `/sitemap.xml`, `/favicon.ico`, `/brand/*`, `/_next/*` | serve as usual |
| Site_Host | `/site` | HTTP 404 (the internal path is not public) |
| Site_Host | any other path | HTTP 308 to `{APP_URL}` plus the same path and query |
| App_Host | `/` | redirect to `/boards`, as today |
| App_Host | `/site` | HTTP 404 |
| App_Host | `/robots.txt` | disallow all (LAND-14) |
| App_Host | any other path | as today: Protected_Route guard, sign-in pages, shared and invite pages |

3. [HOST-03] THE Frontend SHALL read the request host from `x-forwarded-host` and, when that is absent, from `host`; compare its hostname only (case-insensitively, without the port and without a trailing dot) with the hostname of `SITE_URL`; and treat an absent, malformed, or non-matching host as the App_Host. THE Frontend SHALL build every redirect target from `APP_URL` or from a relative path, and SHALL NOT copy the request host into a redirect (Requirement 33, DEP-07).
4. [HOST-04] THE Frontend SHALL extend the `proxy.ts` matcher so that it runs for every page request except static assets, and SHALL keep the existing guard for the Protected_Routes `/boards`, `/profile`, `/admin`, and `/invite` on the App_Host unchanged. No Protected_Route page SHALL be reachable through the Site_Host.
5. [HOST-05] WHILE `SITE_URL` is not set, THE Frontend SHALL treat every host as the App_Host, which keeps access by the server IP and port 8082 and by `localhost` working (DEP-13); in that state `/site` SHALL answer HTTP 404 when `NODE_ENV` is `production`, and SHALL serve the Landing_Page for local preview otherwise.

**Configuration**

6. [HOST-06] THE Frontend SHALL read two server environment variables: `SITE_URL`, the Site_Host origin (for example `https://revoboard.belajardeploy.web.id`), and `APP_URL`, the App_Host origin (for example `https://app.revoboard.belajardeploy.web.id`). Each is an absolute origin (scheme, host, optional port) with no path; THE Frontend SHALL refuse to start with a clear message when one is set and the other is not, or when a value is not an absolute origin.
7. [HOST-07] THE Frontend SHALL read both variables at request time on the server and SHALL NOT inline them into the build (no `NEXT_PUBLIC_` prefix), so that one build serves the IP address and any domain, and the origin independence of Requirement 33 criterion 6 holds. WHILE `APP_URL` is not set, THE Landing_Page links SHALL be the relative paths `/login` and `/register`.
8. [HOST-08] THE Session_Cookie SHALL stay a host-only cookie of the App_Host: THE Frontend SHALL NOT set a `Domain` attribute, so that the Site_Host and any other subdomain never receive it. WHEN TLS is enabled, THE Frontend SHALL add `Secure` to the cookie (DEP-13).
9. [HOST-09] THE Backend SHALL stay reachable only through the App_Host. The browser calls `/api` on the current origin, so no cross-origin request exists: `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` SHALL list the App_Host origin only, and the Landing_Page SHALL make no browser request to the Backend (LAND-01). Public links, invite links, and every absolute URL the app builds SHALL use the App_Host origin.

**Nginx, TLS, and DNS**

10. [HOST-10] THE Nginx site file SHALL name both hosts in `server_name` and keep a `default_server` for the server IP; SHALL send `/api/` to the Backend and every other path to the Frontend for the App_Host and the default server; SHALL answer HTTP 404 for `/api/` on the Site_Host; SHALL preserve the request host in `Host` and `X-Forwarded-Host` (DEP-05); and SHALL keep the 3 MB body limit.
11. [HOST-11] WHEN TLS is configured, THE certificate SHALL cover both names (two names or a wildcard), plain HTTP SHALL redirect to HTTPS on both, and DNS SHALL have address records for both names. THIS requirement does not automate certificate issue; the runbook records the steps.
12. [HOST-12] THE deploy runbook (`deploy/README.md`), the server environment file template, the Compose file, and the `supervisord` configuration SHALL document and pass `SITE_URL` and `APP_URL` to the Frontend only, and SHALL record the DNS, Nginx, and certificate steps of criteria 10 and 11.

**Verification**

13. [HOST-13] THE smoke test (`deploy/smoke.mjs`) SHALL check, by sending `Host` headers to the Nginx port, that: the Site_Host `/` returns the Landing_Page containing the text "RevoBoard" and "Get started"; the Site_Host `/login` answers 308 to `{APP_URL}/login`; the Site_Host `/api/health` answers 404; the Site_Host `/site` answers 404; the App_Host `/` redirects to `/boards`; the App_Host `/site` answers 404; the App_Host `/robots.txt` disallows all; the App_Host `/api/health` is healthy; and a request by the server IP behaves as the App_Host.
14. [HOST-14] THE Frontend SHALL cover the table of criterion 2 with a property test (Property 64): for any host, path, and query, the result is exactly one row of the table, no redirect target is built from the request host, and a malformed host never reaches the Site_Host rows.

**Amendments**

15. [HOST-15] THIS requirement amends DEP-05 ("serves one origin") to "serves the Site_Host and the App_Host by name and the server IP as the App_Host", and DEP-13 so that the planned domain is the Site_Host and `app.` under it is the App_Host; the rest of Requirements 33 and 34 stands.

### Requirement 43: Throughput Analytics and Dashboard

Note (V17): DASH-08 and DASH-09 are superseded by Requirement 51; the Dashboard path is `/app/dashboard`; THR-03 is amended by COL-08.
**User Story:** As a Supervisor or Manager, I want to see how many Cards my team finishes each week, so that I can judge delivery over time and not only today's counts.

Requirement 10 returns counts for now (`total`, `done`, `in_progress`, `overdue`) and `done_delta`, which counts Cards in Done whose `updated_at` is within 7 days. Any later edit of a finished Card moves `updated_at`, so that number can count a Card twice or in the wrong week, and no per-week series can be built from it. V16 stores when a Card was completed (Decision AW). The prototype's Dashboard (page 3) also draws hour estimates, capacity points, and velocity percentages; none has data, so it is not built (exclusions 6).

#### Acceptance Criteria

**Data**

1. [THR-01] THE Backend SHALL add a nullable `cards.completed_at` timestamp with time zone, in one reversible migration that adds the column, backfills it, and drops it on downgrade. THE migration SHALL set `completed_at` to the Card's `updated_at` for every non-soft-deleted Card whose Column is named `Done`, SHALL leave it null for every other Card, SHALL delete and truncate nothing, and SHALL run on a production database with data (Decision AN does not apply, because the migration is not a reset migration).
2. [THR-02] WHEN a Card is created in a Column named `Done`, or moved into a Column named `Done` from a Column with another name, THE Backend SHALL set `completed_at` to the current time in the same transaction as the move; WHEN a Card moves out of a Column named `Done`, THE Backend SHALL set `completed_at` to null; WHEN a Card moves within `Done` (a reorder), THE Backend SHALL leave it unchanged.
3. [THR-03] THE Backend SHALL keep this invariant for every non-soft-deleted Card: `completed_at` is not null exactly when the Card's Column is named `Done`. THE Backend SHALL NOT add `completed_at` to any Card payload, shared payload, or Activity_Log metadata, and no existing payload SHALL change.  (V17: amended by COL-08 for archived Cards, which have no Column and keep `completed_at`.)
4. [THR-04] THE Backend SHALL compute `done_delta` of Requirement 10 criterion 5 as the number of non-soft-deleted Cards whose `completed_at` is within the last 7 days, instead of Cards in `Done` whose `updated_at` is within the last 7 days (Open Question 42). The field name, type, and the other Analytics_Summary fields are unchanged.

**Endpoints**

5. [THR-05] WHEN a user calls `GET /api/boards/{board_id}/analytics/throughput` with an optional `weeks` query parameter, THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 200 with the Envelope and `data` of `{ "board_id", "name", "weeks": [ { "week_start": "YYYY-MM-DD", "done": integer } ], "total": integer }`. THE `weeks` list SHALL be ordered oldest first, SHALL have exactly `weeks` entries ending with the current week, and SHALL list every week even when its `done` is 0. A week runs Monday 00:00 to the next Monday 00:00 in `APP_TIMEZONE`, and `week_start` is that Monday. `total` is the sum of `done`.
6. [THR-06] THE `done` of a week SHALL be the number of Cards of that Board whose `completed_at` falls in that week and which are not soft-deleted. Archived Cards SHALL count, because archiving does not undo that the work was finished.
7. [THR-07] THE `weeks` parameter SHALL be an integer from 1 to 26 and default to 8; IF it is not an integer or is outside that range, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 400 with the code `VALIDATION_ERROR` and an `error.details` entry for `weeks`.
8. [THR-08] WHEN a Manager calls `GET /api/analytics/throughput` with the same `weeks` parameter, THE Backend SHALL return one series per Board that is not soft-deleted, ordered by name and then id, in one pre-aggregated response of the shape of criterion 5 repeated in a list, using a number of database queries that does not depend on the number of Boards or Cards (Requirement 14 criterion 12).
9. [THR-09] THE Backend SHALL apply the role rules of Requirement 10 to both endpoints: a Supervisor may read the series of a Board it owns only; a Manager may read any Board and the cross-board list; a Member and an Admin receive HTTP 403; a request without a Token receives HTTP 401; a Board that does not exist or is soft-deleted returns HTTP 404 (Requirement 10 criteria 1 to 4 and 7).
10. [THR-10] THE Backend SHALL put the new routes in the `analytics` blueprint through `routes.py`, `service.py`, `repository.py`, and `schema.py` (Requirement 13), SHALL aggregate in one grouped query without returning Card rows, and SHALL regenerate `backend/openapi.json` and update `backend/API.md` so the committed documents match the generated ones (Requirement 17).

**Dashboard page**

11. [DASH-01] THE Frontend SHALL add the Protected_Route `/dashboard`, reachable from the Dashboard item of the Top_Nav and the Icon_Rail (SHELL-05), and SHALL add `/dashboard` to the `proxy.ts` guard. WHEN a Member or an Admin opens it, THE Frontend SHALL redirect to `/boards`. THE document title SHALL be `RevoBoard | Dashboard` (Requirement 32).  (V17: the path is `/app/dashboard`, PATH-01.)
12. [DASH-02] THE Dashboard SHALL show a `text-xl` title "Dashboard" and a Board selector built with `BaseCombobox`: for a Supervisor, the Boards it owns; for a Manager, "All boards" followed by every Board. The first option is selected on load.
13. [DASH-03] THE Dashboard SHALL draw its first row as the prototype's page 3 does (`03-dashboard-desktop.png`), as three cards of radius `frame` on `--surface-200` with padding 24, in a grid of one column below 960 pixels and three from 960: (a) "Board overview": the Board name, an inner `--gradient-brand` card (radius `card`) with the eyebrow "BOARD PROGRESS", the percentage done in `text-xl` on `--on-brand`, and the subtask-free count line "<done> of <total> Cards done", and under it "Completed in the last 7 days" with the `done_delta` value and a teal count badge; (b) the throughput chart of criterion 14; (c) "Open work": the open Cards count in `text-xl` (total minus done), and the In progress and Overdue figures as two rows with a tone dot each. The figures come from Requirement 10; FOR "All boards" the Frontend SHALL add the figures of `GET /api/analytics/boards`; no figure is computed from Card rows.
14. [DASH-04] THE throughput chart card SHALL be titled "Completed per week" with a 36 pixel icon tile, and SHALL show one pill-shaped bar per week of the last 8 weeks for the selected Board, or for "All boards" the sum of the weeks of every Board; the bars are `--surface-strong` pills of equal width with a `--accent` fill rising from the base, the current week's bar is solid `--accent` with its value in a dark `--ink` tooltip pill above it, and each bar is labelled below with the week start ("12 Oct", `text-2xs` upper case) and carries its value above it in `text-meta` on hover and focus; it is drawn with token colours as inline SVG or styled elements with no chart library, with `role="img"` and an accessible name that states the total, and a visually hidden table of the same values for assistive technology. A week with 0 shows an empty bar, not a gap. THE prototype's "Weekly or Monthly" switch is excluded (DASH-08).
15. [DASH-05] FOR a Manager, THE Dashboard SHALL show, as its second row, a Boards table card from `GET /api/analytics/boards` on the table card layout of SCR-15 (header row of `text-2xs` upper case labels; rows with a 40 pixel tone tile, the Board name as a link to the Board, and the figures Total, Done as a percentage, Overdue, and Completed in the last 7 days); below 960 pixels the rows stack as cards.
16. [DASH-06] FOR a single selected Board, THE Dashboard SHALL show, as its second row, a "Recent activity" card with the five newest rows of `GET /api/boards/{id}/activity?limit=5` through the existing `ActivityLine` on the Panel row recipe, with a "View all" quiet pill that opens the Activity view of the Side_Panel for that Board. For "All boards" it SHALL show no recent activity.
17. [DASH-07] THE Dashboard SHALL show a loading state (skeleton tiles and chart), an error state with a Retry button, and, for a viewer with no Board, an empty state "No boards to show yet." with a link to Home, each drawn from the Design_System recipes.
18. [DASH-08] THE Dashboard SHALL NOT show estimates, capacity, a velocity percentage, a "Weekly or Monthly" switch, or a claim that the API does not return (exclusions 6); and the Home analytics strip of Requirement 10 criterion 8 SHALL remain.  (V17: superseded by DASH-10 to DASH-22, Requirement 51.)
19. [DASH-09] THE Dashboard SHALL take the first row of criterion 13 and a second row of one full-width card, SHALL start with the title block of Requirement 40 (an eyebrow, the `text-xl` title "Dashboard", and the Board selector at the right), SHALL have no horizontal scroll between 360 and 414 pixels, and SHALL show no greeting, no date range, and no "Add New Card" button (exclusions 2 and 17).  (V17: superseded by DASH-10 to DASH-22, Requirement 51.)

### Requirement 44: V16 Verification

**User Story:** As the product owner, I want automated checks that the design system, the shell, the landing page, the host split, and the throughput numbers stay correct, so that a later change cannot quietly break the look or the data.

This requirement extends Requirement 15. The checks below are written with the implementation tasks of group 38 and run in CI with the existing ones (Requirement 34).

#### Acceptance Criteria

**Style checks (Requirement 38)**

1. [V16-T01] THE Frontend SHALL keep every check of `frontend/tests/styling.test.ts` (no hex, no arbitrary value, no numeric `z-`, `duration-`, `opacity-`, `ring-`, `outline-`, or `border-` width outside the allowed set, no raw `<button>` outside `components/ui` and `components/shared`, no `max-*` variant, shadcn/ui primitives imported only from `components/shared` and `components/ui`) and SHALL add checks that: the landing files (`app/site/` and `components/landing/`) are scanned like every other file; no source uses a `rounded-*` utility outside `sm`, `control`, `card`, `surface`, and `full`; no source uses `font-*` family utilities other than the two token families; no source or stylesheet names Material Symbols, `fonts.googleapis.com`, or `fonts.gstatic.com`; and `tokens.css` no longer defines `--text-heading`, `--text-lg`, `--radius-md`, or `--radius-lg`.
2. [V16-T02] THE Frontend SHALL include a contrast test (Property 67) that parses `tokens.css`, computes the ratio of every pair listed in Requirement 38 criterion 7, and fails below 4.5 to 1 for text and 3 to 1 for the boundary and focus pairs.
3. [V16-T03] THE Frontend SHALL include a test that every row of the component recipe table (Requirement 38 criterion 18) is met by its `Base*` component: control heights equal `--size-control` and `--size-control-sm`, the radius role matches, and the type utility matches (Property 63).

**Shell (Requirement 39)**

4. [V16-T04] THE Frontend SHALL include component tests that: the Icon_Rail and the Top_Nav show the items of SHELL-05 for each of the roles Member, Supervisor, Manager, and Admin, both with and without an open Board; a rail item has a tooltip on hover and focus and none after a click; the Inbox item shows "9+" for ten or more unread; the Nav_Drawer opens from the menu button, traps focus, and closes on Escape, on the scrim, and on a route change; the Board_Switcher shows the search field only for six or more Boards, shows "Owner" or the viewer's role, the percentage done, and the card count, and navigates on selection; the Board_Toolbar progress meter shows the Board payload's figures and is absent for a Board with no Card; the Team_View lists the owner first with the role tags and shows the add and remove controls only to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin; an Admin's Team item goes to `/admin/users`; no Log out or Profile entry exists in the rail, the Top_Nav, or the drawer; and the "Skip to content" link is first in focus order.
5. [V16-T05] THE Frontend SHALL update every existing Playwright and `vitest` test that queries the Sidebar (for example the sign-out checks AUTH-E2E-SO and the Team members tests of V12) so that it queries the Icon_Rail, the Top_Nav, the Board_Switcher, or the Team_View and asserts the same behaviour; no test SHALL be deleted to make the suite pass.

**Screens (Requirement 40)**

6. [V16-T06] THE Frontend SHALL include tests that the register form has separate Username and Email fields with the availability check and has no Terms checkbox, no entropy meter, and no combined identifier field; that a board Card shows no identifier and no estimate; that the priority comment label reads "Priority"; that the Inbox shows no Mentions or Assignments tab; that the Activity view shows no filter, live indicator, or export; and that the Categories, Invite, Public link, Admin users, New Board, and Shared view screens show none of the excluded items of `specs/v16/00-OVERVIEW.md` that apply to them.
7. [V16-T07] THE Frontend SHALL include a Playwright check at 360, 414, 960, and 1280 pixels wide that, for the login page, the register page, Home, a Board, the Card modal, the Side_Panel, the Categories screen, the Dashboard, and the Landing_Page, the document has no horizontal scroll (`scrollWidth` not above `clientWidth`) and every icon-only control is at least 44 pixels in both directions below 960 pixels.

**Landing page and hosts (Requirements 41 and 42)**

8. [V16-T08] THE Frontend SHALL include tests that the Landing_Page renders the parts of LAND-02 in order with their `id` values; that its two call-to-action links use `APP_URL` when set and the relative paths otherwise; that its source imports no `fetch`, `cookies`, `headers`, or `localStorage` use; that it contains exactly one `h1`; that its text contains none of the words of exclusions 15 ("Pricing", "Enterprise", "PRO", "Viewer", "testimonial") and no "RevoTask"; and that the product preview is `inert`, and every number it shows equals the value the app's own functions compute from the sample data (Property 68).
9. [V16-T09] THE Frontend SHALL include the host routing property test of HOST-14 (Property 64) and unit tests for the cases: `SITE_URL` unset; a host with a port; an upper-case host; a host with a trailing dot; a spoofed `x-forwarded-host` that is malformed; and a request that carries a Host equal to the Site_Host with the path `/boards/abc?x=1`, which must redirect to `{APP_URL}/boards/abc?x=1`.
10. [V16-T10] THE smoke test SHALL carry the checks of HOST-13, and the CI workflow SHALL run it against the throwaway stack with `SITE_URL` and `APP_URL` set to test names.

**Throughput and Dashboard (Requirement 43)**

11. [V16-T11] THE Backend SHALL include tests, on PostgreSQL where the existing migration tests use it, that: the migration adds `completed_at`, backfills it from `updated_at` for Cards in `Done` only, leaves other Cards null, removes nothing, and downgrades cleanly; creating a Card in `Done`, moving a Card into, out of, and within `Done` sets, clears, and keeps `completed_at` (Property 66); soft-deleting a Card removes it from every count; the throughput lists have exactly `weeks` entries, oldest first, with zero weeks present, with Monday boundaries in `APP_TIMEZONE` including a Card completed at 23:59 Sunday and at 00:00 Monday; `total` equals the sum (Property 65); `weeks` of 0, 27, and "abc" return 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details.weeks`; roles return 200, 403, 403, 401, and 404 as THR-09 says; the query count is the same for 1 and for 30 Boards; and `done_delta` follows THR-04.
12. [V16-T12] THE Backend SHALL include a property test (Property 65) that, for any set of Cards with arbitrary `completed_at` values and any `weeks` from 1 to 26, each non-deleted Card with `completed_at` inside the window is counted in exactly one week, and the weekly values sum to the number of such Cards.
13. [V16-T13] THE Frontend SHALL include tests that the Dashboard redirects a Member and an Admin to `/boards`; selects the first Board on load; sums the cross-board figures for "All boards"; draws one bar per week with the hidden table; shows the Boards table to a Manager only; shows "Recent activity" for one Board and not for "All boards"; and shows the loading, error with Retry, and empty states.

**Regression and review**

14. [V16-T14] THE System SHALL pass `pytest`, `tsc --noEmit`, `vitest run`, and `next build` unchanged in command, with `backend/openapi.json` current (`test_committed_openapi_json_is_current`), before group 38 is ticked.
15. [V16-T15] THE implementer SHALL put each of the 13 prototype pages (the images of `design/reference/v2/`) beside the built screen for the owner at 1440 and 390 pixels and record the owner's decision on every difference in `specs/CHANGELOG.md` before group 38 is ticked; a difference the owner accepts becomes a V16 note, and one the owner rejects becomes a task.

16. [V16-T16] THE Frontend SHALL include a Playwright test of the recipe table (Requirement 38, DS-27) that renders each shared component once in a test page at 1440 pixels wide and asserts its computed height, border radius, padding, font size, font weight, and background colour against the token values of `tokens.css` for every row of criterion 18 that has them (for example: a primary button is 36 pixels high, fully rounded, `--accent` on `--on-accent`; a Board Card has radius `--radius-card`, padding 16 pixels, and `--surface-200`; a Column has radius `--radius-frame` and `--surface-100`; the Icon_Rail is 64 pixels wide and fully rounded; the app frame has radius `--radius-frame`). It SHALL also assert the hit area of Requirement 38 criterion 17: below 960 pixels every icon-only control and every pill shorter than 44 pixels has an interactive area of at least 44 by 44 pixels.
17. [V16-T17] THE Frontend SHALL include a Playwright run that opens the Board page, the Card modal, the New Board modal, the Board_Switcher, the Inbox, the Activity view, the auth page, and the Landing_Page at 1440 by 900 pixels, saves a screenshot of each to a git-ignored folder, and fails when a screenshot is blank or when the page shows an unstyled element (a computed font family other than the two token families, or a computed background and radius equal to the browser default on a recipe component). The owner compares these screenshots with `design/reference/v2/` in V16-T15; the test does not compare pixels, because the sample content differs.
### Requirement 45: UI Fixes
**User Story:** As a user, I want the sign-in pages, forms, selects, and confirmations to behave consistently, so that the product feels finished and I never lose work to an accidental click.
This requirement fixes seven defects the owner listed and adds the one shared confirmation dialog (Decision AY). It changes no data and no endpoint. Every visual value comes from Design_System tokens (Requirement 38); the references are `design/reference/v2/05-auth-desktop.png`, `05-auth-mobile.png`, `01-board-desktop.png`, and `13-activity-desktop.png`.
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Sign-in and register pages**
1. [FIX-01] THE Frontend SHALL draw `BrandLogo` on the login and register pages with a transparent background: no tile, fill, or border behind it on the brand panel or on the form side. THE logo files in `public/brand/` SHALL carry an alpha channel; IF the supplied PNG inside either SVG has an opaque background, THEN the implementer SHALL ask the owner for a transparent file and SHALL NOT paint over the image (Open Question 21, V11). A test SHALL read the corner pixels of the embedded PNG and assert that they are fully transparent.
2. [FIX-02] THE login and register pages SHALL use the same canvas and surface tokens as the Boards page: the page background is `--surface-100`, and the form card uses the white surface, radius role, and shadow token of the app frame (SHELL-11), with no second colour or radius of their own. THE brand panel keeps `--gradient-brand` (DS-06). A test SHALL assert that the three class sets resolve to the same token names on the auth layout and on the app shell.
3. [FIX-03] WHEN the user edits a field, THE Frontend SHALL clear that field's error at once, both a client validation error and an error from `error.details` of a previous submit; the error SHALL NOT come back until the next blur or submit finds the new value invalid. Clearing one field SHALL NOT clear another field's error.
4. [FIX-04] WHEN the user submits the login or register form, THE Frontend SHALL validate every field before it sends a request, SHALL show the error of every invalid field at once (not only the first), SHALL move focus to the first invalid field, and SHALL send no request while any field is invalid. EACH invalid field SHALL have `aria-invalid="true"` and its error linked through `aria-describedby`. WHEN the Backend answers `400 VALIDATION_ERROR`, THE Frontend SHALL show every key of `error.details` beside its field in the same pass.
**Selects, comboboxes, and dropdowns**
5. [FIX-05] IN every select, combobox, and dropdown menu (`BaseCombobox`, `BaseMenu`, and every `components/ui` primitive behind them), THE Frontend SHALL show an option's hover highlight while the pointer is over it and SHALL remove it when the pointer leaves, so that at most one option shows a highlight from the pointer at a time and none shows it after the pointer left the list. THE keyboard highlight (arrow keys, Home, End, type-ahead) SHALL keep working and SHALL move the highlight independently of the pointer; WHEN the pointer then moves over another option, the highlight SHALL follow the pointer. A test SHALL fire `pointermove` and `pointerleave` on options and assert the highlighted attribute each time.
**Team panel**
6. [FIX-06] THE Members view of the Side_Panel (the Team panel of V16) SHALL leave `--space-4` between its header and the member search input. IN the build of Phase 1 the view is still called Team_View; Requirement 47 renames it.
**Confirmation**
7. [FIX-07] THE Frontend SHALL provide one `ConfirmDialog` (`components/shared/ConfirmDialog.tsx`), built on `BaseModal`, and SHALL open it before these actions run: (a) create: a Board, a Column, a Category, a Card, a user (Admin), an Invite_Link, and a Public_Link; (b) delete, remove, disable, or regenerate: a Board (soft delete), a Column, a Category, a Card, a Subtask, a comment, a reply, an attachment, a Board member, an Invite_Link, a Public_Link, and a profile picture; (c) the toggles and changes named in Requirement 53 criterion 8. THE comment, reply, Subtask, attachment, and Join_Request creates are not in the list (Open Question 51).
8. [FIX-08] THE dialog SHALL name the item and the action in its title and body ("Delete column “Review”?", "Create board “Q3 plan”?"), SHALL have a primary action whose label repeats the verb ("Delete", "Create") and a Cancel action, SHALL use the `danger` button recipe for a delete and the primary recipe for a create, SHALL close on Escape, on a scrim click, and on Cancel without running the action, and SHALL return focus to the control that opened it. THE primary action SHALL show a busy state while the request runs and SHALL keep the dialog open with the error message when the request fails.
9. [FIX-09] THE Frontend SHALL trap focus inside the dialog while it is open, move initial focus to Cancel for a delete and to the primary action for a create, and SHALL announce the title through `aria-labelledby` and the body through `aria-describedby`. WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE dialog SHALL fit between 360 and 414 pixels with its two actions on one row or stacked, never cut off.
10. [FIX-10] THE Frontend SHALL replace every use of `BaseConfirm` (the `popover` and `modal` variants of V12) with `ConfirmDialog` and SHALL remove `BaseConfirm` once none is left. THE `structure.md` list of shared primitives SHALL name `ConfirmDialog` in place of `BaseConfirm` (task 39.51).
11. [FIX-11] THE Frontend SHALL keep the existing behaviour of each confirmed action (endpoints, permissions, activity rows, notifications); only the confirmation step changes. A test SHALL, for each action of FIX-07, assert that no request is sent before the dialog is confirmed and exactly one is sent after.
### Requirement 46: Path Topology
**User Story:** As the product owner, I want the landing page and the app on one origin, with the app under `/app`, so that one domain, one certificate, and one deploy serve everything.
This requirement supersedes Requirement 42 (HOST-01 to HOST-15) and amends Requirement 33 (DEP-05 and DEP-13) and Requirement 34 (the smoke test). One Next.js process serves `/` and `/app/...`. The Backend, the database, and the Flask routes do not change; only nginx and the public URL prefix do (Decision AZ).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Routes**
1. [PATH-01] THE Frontend SHALL serve the Landing_Page at `/` and every other page under `/app`: `/app/boards`, `/app/b/{slug}`, `/app/b/{slug}/list`, `/app/b/{slug}/calendar`, `/app/b/{slug}/archived`, `/app/dashboard`, `/app/my-tasks`, `/app/users`, `/app/profile`, `/app/login`, `/app/register`, `/app/verify`, `/app/invite/{token}`, `/app/shared/{token}`, `/app/session-expired`, and `/app/boards/{id}` (the redirect-only route of PATH-08). THE Frontend SHALL implement this with route folders (`app/app/...`), not with Next.js `basePath`, because the Landing_Page lives outside `/app`.
2. [PATH-02] THE production build SHALL set `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` to `/app/api`. THE Frontend SHALL build every Backend call as `{NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL}/...` (for example `/app/api/boards`). IN development, `next.config.ts` SHALL rewrite `/app/api/:path*` to the Backend's `/api/:path*`. Server-side calls keep using the internal Backend URL (Requirement 33).
3. [PATH-03] nginx SHALL proxy `/app/api/` to the Flask `/api/` routes with the prefix removed (`proxy_pass` with a trailing slash), SHALL send every other `/app` path and `/` to the Frontend, SHALL answer HTTP 404 for `/api/` on the public side so that the Backend is reachable at one path only, and SHALL keep the request body limit of Requirement 33. THE Flask routes SHALL NOT change.
4. [PATH-04] THE Backend SHALL build every absolute path it returns with the configured public prefix `API_PUBLIC_PREFIX` (default empty in development and testing, `/app/api` in production): the `file_url` and `avatar_url` of signed files (Requirement 19, the attachment rules of `backend.md`), and the `path` of the Invite_Link and the Public_Link, which become `/app/invite/{token}` and `/app/shared/{token}`. THE signature of a Signed_File_URL SHALL cover the file, the scope, and the expiry, and SHALL NOT cover the prefix, so a change of prefix invalidates no valid URL.
**Host routing removed**
5. [PATH-05] THE Frontend SHALL remove `lib/host.ts`, `lib/origins.ts`, the host branch of `proxy.ts`, the environment variables `SITE_URL` and `APP_URL`, and `app/site/`; THE Landing_Page SHALL move to `app/page.tsx`. THE `proxy.ts` matcher SHALL cover `/app/:path*`. THE guard SHALL protect every path under `/app` except `/app/login`, `/app/register`, `/app/verify`, `/app/session-expired`, the signed-out states of `/app/invite/{token}` (which show the sign-in prompt), and `/app/shared/{token}`. A request without a valid Session_Cookie to a guarded path SHALL redirect to `/app/login?next=<safe path>` and remove the cookie. THE `next` value SHALL be used only when it is a local path under `/app/b/`, `/app/boards`, `/app/dashboard`, `/app/my-tasks`, `/app/users`, `/app/profile`, or `/app/invite/`.
6. [PATH-06] THE Session_Cookie SHALL stay host-only (no `Domain`) and SHALL gain `Path=/app`, so the Landing_Page and every other path never receive it. WHEN TLS is enabled the cookie SHALL be `Secure`. The Landing_Page SHALL still read no cookie and make no Backend call (LAND-01).
**Search engines**
7. [PATH-07] THE `robots.txt` and `sitemap.xml` routes SHALL name `/` only; THE sitemap SHALL list no `/app` URL. EVERY response under `/app` SHALL carry `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`, set in `proxy.ts`. THE `robots.txt` SHALL NOT disallow `/app`, because a disallowed page is never fetched and its `noindex` header would never be read (Open Question 67). THE V12 headers `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` and `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` of the Shared_View and the invite page stay.
**Old paths**
8. [PATH-08] THE Frontend SHALL answer these paths with HTTP 308 to the `/app` equivalent, keeping the query string: `/boards` to `/app/boards`, `/boards/{id}` to `/app/boards/{id}`, `/dashboard` to `/app/dashboard`, `/admin/users` to `/app/users`, `/profile` to `/app/profile`, `/login` to `/app/login`, `/register` to `/app/register`, `/invite/{token}` to `/app/invite/{token}`, `/shared/{token}` to `/app/shared/{token}`, and `/session-expired` to `/app/session-expired`. THE route `/app/boards/{id}` SHALL read the Board through `GET /api/boards/{id}` and redirect to `/app/b/{slug}` (with the same query), and SHALL show the Not_Found_Page when the Backend answers 404 and the Board_Unavailable page when it answers 403 (Open Question 65).
9. [PATH-09] THE Frontend SHALL NOT redirect an old Board slug (Requirement 48, OQ46).
**Internal links**
10. [PATH-10] THE Frontend SHALL build every internal link and every `router.push` from one module, `lib/routes.ts`, which exports `appPath(...)` helpers (`boardPath(slug)`, `boardViewPath(slug, view)`, `cardPath(slug, cardId)`, `sharedPath(token)`, `invitePath(token)`, and so on). No component SHALL write the string `/app/` or `/boards` by hand. A test SHALL scan `components/` and `app/` for hand-written route strings and fail on a match outside `lib/routes.ts`. THE Inbox `hrefFor` function SHALL use these helpers.
**Deployment**
11. [PATH-11] THE deployment files SHALL change as follows (tasks 39.9 and 39.48): `deploy/nginx/revoboard.conf` (one server block, `/app/api/` proxy, `/api/` 404, no host names beyond the optional `server_name`), `compose.yaml` and `deploy/bare/supervisor.conf` (remove `SITE_URL` and `APP_URL`; add `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` at build time and `API_PUBLIC_PREFIX` for the Backend), `deploy/deploy.sh`, `deploy/seed.sh`, `deploy/setup-server.sh`, `deploy/bare/*.sh`, `deploy/smoke.sh` and `deploy/smoke.mjs` (check `/` returns the Landing_Page, `/app/login` returns 200, `/app/api/health` returns 200, `/api/health` returns 404, `/boards` answers 308 to `/app/boards`, and an unknown `/app/xyz` returns 404 with the Not_Found_Page), and `deploy/README.md` (the new topology, the 308 table, the cookie path). THE access by server IP and port 8082 SHALL keep working, because no host name is required (DEP-13).
12. [PATH-12] THE CORS allow-list (`ALLOWED_ORIGINS`) SHALL stay and list the one public origin; the browser makes only same-origin calls, so CORS applies to development only. THE Landing_Page SHALL keep the rules of Requirement 41 (static, no API call) with its two call-to-action links pointing to `/app/register` and `/app/login`.
### Requirement 47: Navigation
> Amended by Requirements 62, 70, and 71, V18 (logo, active state, back arrow, Members and Categories panels).
**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want global places in a left sidebar and the places of the open Board in a menu at the top, so that I always know where I am and what belongs to the Board.
This requirement supersedes Requirement 39 for the navigation items (SHELL-01 to SHELL-08 and SHELL-17 to SHELL-21), keeps its App_Header (HDR rules), Board_Switcher (SHELL-12 to SHELL-15), progress pill (SHELL-16), Skip link (SHELL-25), and Side_Panel look (SHELL-26), and amends Requirements 8, 9, and 6 where they say where a view opens (Decision BA). The Sidebar here is new; it is not the V1 to V15 Sidebar. Reference: `design/reference/v2/01-board-desktop.png` for the frame, the Side_Panel references `11-inbox-desktop.png`, `13-activity-desktop.png`, and `09-categories-desktop.png` for the panel views.
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Sidebar (global)**
1. [NAV-01] ON every Protected_Route page, THE Frontend SHALL show a left Sidebar with these items, in this order, each only to the roles named: Dashboard (Supervisor and Manager, Requirement 10), My Tasks (Member, Supervisor, and Manager; not an Admin, who cannot be an assignee, TASK-04), Boards (every signed-in user), and Users (Admin only). An item that is not shown takes no space. THE items come from one list, `shell/navItems.ts` (NAV-05).
2. [NAV-02] THE Sidebar SHALL have a toggle with the accessible name "Collapse sidebar" or "Expand sidebar" and `aria-expanded`. EXPANDED, it shows each item's icon and label; COLLAPSED, it shows icons only, and each item has a `BaseTooltip` with its label that shows on hover and on keyboard focus only (never after a click). THE default is expanded (OQ48). THE state SHALL be stored per browser under one `localStorage` key and read safely: a missing key, an unknown value, a storage error, or a server render SHALL give expanded. A change of the state SHALL NOT move focus.
3. [NAV-03] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE Frontend SHALL hide the Sidebar and show in the App_Header a menu button (44 pixels, accessible name "Open menu", `aria-expanded`) that opens the Nav_Drawer: an off-canvas panel from the left, built as `BaseDrawer`, behind a scrim, listing the Sidebar items as 44 pixel rows and, while a Board is open, a second group "This board" with the Top_Menu items (NAV-04). THE Nav_Drawer SHALL trap focus, close on Escape, on a scrim click, and on a route change, and return focus to the menu button.
**Top_Menu (board only)**
4. [NAV-04] WHILE a Board is open (`/app/b/{slug}` and its views), THE App_Header SHALL show the Top_Menu with these items in this order: Board, List, Calendar, Activity, Members, Categories, and Share. Board, List, and Calendar are routes (`/app/b/{slug}`, `/list`, `/calendar`), have `aria-current="page"` when active, and keep the search and filter query of the current view where it applies. Activity, Members, and Categories open the right Side_Panel on that view; a second activation of the open view closes it. Share opens the Share_Modal (Requirement 55). WHICH roles see which item: Board, List, Calendar, Activity, and Members, every user with Board_Access; Categories, a Supervisor, a Manager, and an Admin (Requirement 6); Share, the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin. THE Top_Menu SHALL NOT appear on any page that is not a Board view, and the Archived page (`/app/b/{slug}/archived`) SHALL be reached from the Board toolbar's "Archived" link and show the Top_Menu with Board active.
5. [NAV-05] THE Top_Menu is the main menu of a Board; THE Sidebar SHALL NOT repeat a Top_Menu item. THE Frontend SHALL keep both lists in one module, `shell/navItems.ts`, exporting `sidebarItems` and `topMenuItems`, each with an `isVisible({ role, boardOpen, isOwner })` predicate and an action (a route or a Side_Panel view or the Share_Modal), so the Sidebar, the Top_Menu, and the Nav_Drawer never disagree. A test SHALL assert that no id appears in both lists.
**Bell**
6. [NAV-06] THE Frontend SHALL reach notifications only from the bell in the App_Header. THE bell opens the Side_Panel on the Inbox view, drawn as `11-inbox-desktop.png`, and shows the unread dot (V12) and the count badge capped at "9+" while the unread count is above zero. THERE SHALL be no Inbox item in the Sidebar, the Top_Menu, or the Nav_Drawer.
**Side_Panel views**
7. [NAV-07] THE Side_Panel SHALL show one view at a time from `inbox | activity | members | categories`. THE Categories view replaces the Categories screen (`/boards/{id}/categories`) and holds the same add, rename, recolor, and delete controls and the same role rules (Requirement 6), with creates and deletes through ConfirmDialog; THE Members view is the Team_View of V16 (SHELL-19 and SHELL-20) with the member search, remove action, role tags, and the "Owner" tag unchanged, and is shown to an Admin too (Open Question 61). THE panel keeps SHELL-26 (floating, scrim, Escape closes and returns focus to the opener). Opening a view SHALL set and Escape or close SHALL restore `document.title` (Requirement 32). THE `ShellContext` open view type becomes `"inbox" | "activity" | "members" | "categories" | null`.
**Removal**
8. [NAV-08] THE Frontend SHALL remove `IconRail.tsx`, `TopNav.tsx`, the Home circle, and the Inbox, Dashboard, Categories, and Team items of the V16 lists, and SHALL NOT keep a second navigation component beside the Sidebar, the Top_Menu, and the Nav_Drawer. THE V16 Board_Toolbar "Public link" and "Invite" buttons are removed (Share is in the Top_Menu, SHR-01); THE progress pill, the Board_Switcher, and the "Archived" link stay.
**Layout and shell rules kept**
9. [NAV-09] THE Frontend SHALL lay a page out as SHELL-11 does with the Sidebar in the place of the Icon_Rail: a `--surface-100` canvas with a `--page-x` margin, the Sidebar at the left as a white panel of the `frame` radius role, and one app frame holding the App_Header and the page. THE Sidebar width SHALL be two new tokens, `--sidebar-w` (expanded) and `--sidebar-w-collapsed`, added to `tokens.css` and the Tailwind theme (`w-sidebar`, `w-sidebar-collapsed`); their values are chosen against `01-board-desktop.png` and the 44 pixel hit area, and the Board page keeps its viewport-locked layout (Requirement 14 criterion 18). WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE frame SHALL fill the screen and there is no Sidebar.
10. [NAV-10] THE App_Header SHALL hold, at 960 pixels and wider: `BrandLogo` (variant `full`, link to `/app/boards`), the Top_Menu (only on a Board), a flexible gap, the bell, and the User_Menu trigger; and below 960 pixels: the menu button, `BrandLogo` (variant `mark`), the Board name on a Board, the bell, and the User_Menu trigger. THE header SHALL show no page context, no search button, and no global search (exclusion 1 of V16). THE Top_Menu SHALL wrap or collapse so that no item is cut off between 960 and 1280 pixels; IF its items do not fit, THEN labels SHALL be hidden and icons with tooltips shown, never a horizontal scroll.
11. [NAV-11] THE Frontend SHALL show no Sidebar, no Top_Menu, no Nav_Drawer, and no Side_Panel on `/app/login`, `/app/register`, `/app/verify`, the signed-out states of `/app/invite/{token}`, `/app/session-expired`, `/app/shared/{token}`, and the Not_Found_Page when reached signed out. THE Shared_View keeps its own light header (SHELL-24). THE first focusable element of every Protected_Route page SHALL be the Skip link, then the App_Header, then the Sidebar, then the main region (SHELL-25).
12. [NAV-12] THE Profile and Log out SHALL stay in the User_Menu only (HDR-02 to HDR-05); the Sidebar and the Nav_Drawer SHALL NOT repeat them.
13. [NAV-13] THE Inbox view SHALL show at the top of its list an "All" and "Unread" switch (a two-option segmented control on the pill track recipe, `role="radiogroup"`, keyboard operable), drawn as `11-inbox-desktop.png`, defaulting to All. "Unread" SHALL load `GET /api/notifications?unread=true`, which THE Backend SHALL add to the existing endpoint: only rows with `read_at` null, the same paging (`limit`, `before`) and order, and `unread_count` unchanged. WHEN a row is marked read while "Unread" is shown, it SHALL stay visible until the next load so the list does not jump. "Mark all as read" keeps its behaviour. THE empty state under "Unread" SHALL read "No unread notifications" (Open Question 70).
### Requirement 48: Board Slug and Not Found Page
**User Story:** As a user, I want a Board's address to read like its name, and a clear page when an address is wrong, so that I can share and recognise links and never land on a blank error.
This requirement adds `boards.slug` and the URL `/app/b/{slug}` (Decision BB). Every other endpoint keeps the Board UUID (Requirement 13). Public links and invite links use their own tokens.
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Data and generation**
1. [SLUG-01] THE Backend SHALL add `boards.slug` (text, not null) with a unique index over every Board row, including archived and soft-deleted Boards, in one reversible migration that backfills every existing Board from its name in `created_at` then `id` order (so the earliest Board keeps the plain slug) and drops the column on downgrade.
2. [SLUG-02] THE Backend SHALL generate a slug from the Board name by one pure function `slugify(name)` in `app/common/slug.py`: lower-case the name, split it on white space, remove from each word every character that is not `a` to `z` or `0` to `9`, drop the words that are then empty, and join the rest with `-`. A name that gives an empty slug SHALL use `board`. Examples: "Q3 Launch Plan" gives `q3-launch-plan`; "Design & Dev" gives `design-dev`; "Café Board" gives `caf-board` (Open Question 66); "!!!" gives `board`.
3. [SLUG-03] WHEN the slug is already taken by another Board, THE Backend SHALL append `-2`, `-3`, and so on, using the lowest number from 2 that is free, compared case-sensitively against the stored lower-case slugs.
