IF two requests create the same slug at once, THEN the unique index SHALL make one fail and the service SHALL retry with the next free suffix (at most 5 times) before it returns HTTP 409.
4. [SLUG-04] WHEN a Board is created, THE Backend SHALL set its slug in the same transaction. WHEN a Board is renamed, THE Backend SHALL regenerate the slug from the new name in the same transaction, ignoring the Board's own row when it checks for a taken slug; a rename that yields the same slug SHALL keep it (no suffix change). THE Backend SHALL NOT keep the old slug (Open Question 46).
5. [SLUG-05] THE Backend SHALL NOT accept a `slug` in any request body or query; the slug is derived and read-only. A request that carries `slug` SHALL receive HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details.slug`.
**Endpoint**
6. [SLUG-06] WHEN a signed-in user calls `GET /api/boards/slug/{slug}`, THE Backend SHALL return the Board exactly as `GET /api/boards/{id}` does, with the same role rules and payload, and the same status codes: 401 without a Token, 403 without Board_Access (Open Question 52), and 404 when no non-soft-deleted Board has that slug. THE slug in the path SHALL be matched in lower case and SHALL be rejected with 404 when it contains a character outside `a-z`, `0-9`, and `-`.
7. [SLUG-07] THE Board payloads of `GET /api/boards`, `GET /api/boards/{id}`, `GET /api/boards/slug/{slug}`, and `POST` and `PATCH /api/boards` SHALL carry the read-only `slug`. THE Notification payload SHALL carry `board_slug` beside `board_id` (Requirement 28), and THE `board` object of `GET /api/invite/{token}` SHALL carry `slug`. NO `/api/shared` payload SHALL carry a slug (Requirement 18). THE Backend SHALL regenerate `backend/openapi.json` and `backend/API.md`.
**Frontend**
8. [SLUG-08] THE Board URL SHALL be `/app/b/{slug}`, with the views `/list`, `/calendar`, and `/archived` under it. THE Frontend SHALL load the Board with `GET /api/boards/slug/{slug}` and use the returned `id` for every other call.
9. [SLUG-09] WHEN a Board is renamed in the open page, THE Frontend SHALL replace the URL (not push) with `/app/b/{new slug}`, keep the current view segment and the query string, and refresh the document title (Requirement 32) and the Board_Switcher entry from the response. A second tab on the old slug SHALL show the Not_Found_Page on its next load or request.
10. [SLUG-10] THE public link and the invite link SHALL keep working after a rename, because they carry their own tokens and resolve the Board by it (Requirements 18 and 24).
**Not found page**
11. [SLUG-11] THE Frontend SHALL provide one on-brand Not_Found_Page, `app/not-found.tsx`, used for: any unknown path under `/` and `/app`, any `notFound()` call, an unknown or old Board slug, and an unknown `/app/boards/{id}`. IT SHALL show `BrandLogo`, a heading ("Page not found"), one short message ("The page may have moved, or the link is old."), a decorative illustration made only of Design_System tokens (a large `--accent-tint` numeral "404" or an inline SVG that uses token fills, with no raster file and no hard-coded colour), and two buttons: "Go to boards" (to `/app/boards`) and "Go home" (to `/`). WHEN the visitor is signed out, "Go to boards" SHALL lead to the sign-in page through the guard.
12. [SLUG-12] THE Not_Found_Page SHALL answer HTTP 404, carry `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`, set the document title `RevoBoard | Page not found`, keep one `h1`, expose the buttons as links, work with the keyboard, and fit 360, 414, 960, and 1280 pixels with no horizontal scroll. UNDER `/app` it SHALL show inside the app shell when the visitor is signed in, and bare otherwise.
13. [SLUG-13] THE Not_Found_Page SHALL leak nothing: it SHALL NOT show the requested slug, a Board name, or a hint of whether the Board exists (Open Question 52).
**Rules that stay**
14. [SLUG-14] THE ActivityLog metadata, notifications, logs, and error messages SHALL NOT carry `share_token` or `invite_token` (Requirements 18 and 24); a slug is not a credential and MAY appear in them.
### Requirement 49: Column CRUD
> Amended by Requirement 67, V18 (Column drag).
**User Story:** As a Board_Owner, I want to create, rename, reorder, and delete the Columns of my Board, so that its workflow fits my team, without losing finished work.
This requirement amends Requirement 3 criteria 4 and 5 and Requirement 2 criterion 6, which let a Manager, an Admin, or a Supervisor with Board_Access create Columns: from V17 the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin manage Columns, and a Supervisor who is only a BoardMember does not (Decision BC, Open Question 55, answered by the owner in round 2). The Default_Columns of Requirement 3 criterion 1 stay. Reference: the Column header of `01-board-desktop.png`; the new controls use the Component_Recipes.
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Permissions**
1. [COL-01] THE Backend SHALL allow only the Board_Owner of a Board, a Manager, and an Admin to create, rename, reorder, and delete its Columns, with `@require_role("supervisor", "manager", "admin")` plus an ownership check in the service for a Supervisor. EVERY other user, a Supervisor who is only a BoardMember and a Member included, SHALL receive HTTP 403, and a request without a Token HTTP 401 (Requirement 13). A Board with a null `owner_id` is managed by a Manager or an Admin (Open Question 55).
**Endpoints**
2. [COL-02] THE Backend SHALL provide: `POST /api/boards/{board_id}/columns` (body `name`; appends with the next `order`), `PATCH /api/columns/{id}` (body `name`), `PATCH /api/columns/{id}/move` (body `position`, a 0-based integer index among the Board's Columns), and `DELETE /api/columns/{id}`, each with `@blp.arguments` and `@blp.response` and the Envelope, in the `columns` blueprint (`routes.py`, `service.py`, `repository.py`, `schema.py`). A `name` is trimmed, 1 to 255 characters (the existing column length), and unique within the Board when compared case-insensitively; a duplicate SHALL receive HTTP 409 `CONFLICT` with `details.name`. A `position` outside `0..n-1` SHALL receive HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
3. [COL-03] WHEN a Column is reordered, THE Backend SHALL renumber the Board's Columns `0.0, 1.0, 2.0, ...` in the new order in one transaction, so no gap or tie remains. THE Done_Column MAY be reordered (criterion 5).
4. [COL-04] EVERY Column create, rename, reorder, and delete SHALL write one ActivityLog row in the same transaction (`column.created`, `column.renamed` with the old and new name, `column.moved` with the old and new position, `column.deleted` with the name and no Card text), and SHALL NOT notify anyone.
**Deleting**
5. [COL-05] IF a Column to delete holds one or more Open_Cards or other active Cards (not archived and not soft-deleted), THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `COLUMN_NOT_EMPTY` and `error.details` of `{ "count": n }`, and SHALL delete nothing. THE Frontend SHALL show "Move or archive its N cards first." (singular "its 1 card") in the ConfirmDialog's error area and in an `aria-live="polite"` region.
6. [COL-06] WHEN a Column to delete holds only soft-deleted Cards, THE Backend SHALL set their `column_id` to null in the same transaction and then delete the Column row. Archived Cards have no Column (COL-07) and never block a delete. A Column delete SHALL be a hard delete of the Column row; Boards and Cards stay soft-deleted only (Requirement 3 criterion 8).
**Archived Cards and the Card's Board**
7. [COL-07] WHEN a Card is archived, THE Backend SHALL set `cards.column_id` to null and `archived_at` in the same transaction, and SHALL record the Column the Card left in the `card.archived` ActivityLog metadata (`from_column`, a name, no id needed). THE migration SHALL make `cards.column_id` nullable, set it to null for every already archived Card, and add `cards.board_id` (not null, indexed, foreign key to `boards.id`) backfilled from the Card's Column before it is nulled (Open Question 53). A Card keeps one `board_id` for its life; a Card never moves between Boards.
8. [COL-08] THE Backend SHALL keep the `completed_at` invariant of THR-03 for every Card that has a Column: not null exactly when that Column is the Done_Column, and SHALL amend it for archiving (Open Question 57): a Card in the Done_Column cannot be archived (COL-14), so a Card archived from V17 on never has a `completed_at`, and an already archived Card that has one from before V17 keeps it (throughput still counts it, THR-06); restoring (VIEW-07) treats the Card as entering its new Column, clearing `completed_at` or setting it to now when that Column is the Done_Column. THE invariant SHALL hold for every Column change, including a reorder, a rename, and the delete of an emptied Column.
9. [COL-09] THE Backend SHALL treat a Card with a null `column_id` as outside every board view except Archived: it is excluded from `card_count`, `done_count`, and `overdue_count`, from the Board payload's Columns, from search, from Overdue_Card, and from the analytics of Requirement 10, and it SHALL NOT be movable (HTTP 409 `CARD_ARCHIVED`).
**The Done_Column is locked**
10. [COL-10] IF a request renames the Done_Column, or renames any other Column to `Done` in any letter case, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `COLUMN_LOCKED`. IF a request deletes the Done_Column, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 `COLUMN_LOCKED`. A request that creates a Column named `Done` in any letter case SHALL receive HTTP 409 `COLUMN_LOCKED`, because every Board already has one. THE Done_Column MAY be reordered. THE Frontend SHALL show the Done_Column's rename and delete controls disabled with the tooltip "The Done column is locked", and SHALL NOT offer to delete it.
**Frontend**
11. [COL-11] THE Frontend SHALL show, to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin only, at the end of the Column row an "Add column" control and, on each Column header, a menu (`BaseMenu`) with Rename, Move left, Move right, and Delete; every other user sees none of them (the absence of the controls follows the permission, and the Backend still returns 403). Renaming SHALL save on Enter or blur with the V12 auto-save status (Requirement 25). A reorder SHALL update the order at once and reconcile with the response.
12. [COL-12] EVERY Column create and delete SHALL go through ConfirmDialog (FIX-07, Requirement 45). A rename and a reorder SHALL NOT.
13. [COL-13] THE Column header SHALL stay within the Design_System: no new radius, colour, or size; the "Add column" tile uses the Column recipe of Requirement 38 criterion 18 (`--surface-sunken`) at the Column width token. A Board with up to 12 Columns SHALL scroll sideways inside the board area and never widen the page (Requirement 14 criterion 18).
14. [COL-14] IF a Card in the Done_Column is archived (the archive route of Requirement 4 criterion 6), THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `CARD_IN_DONE` and change nothing (Open Question 57, resolved by the owner); a Card must leave `Done` by a move before it can be archived. THE Frontend SHALL show Archive disabled with the tooltip "Move the card out of Done to archive it" in the Card modal footer for a Card in `Done`. THE bulk or Column-level paths SHALL NOT archive a Done Card either.
### Requirement 50: Board Views and Due Badges
**User Story:** As a Board member, I want to read a Board's Cards as a list, on a calendar, or as an archive, and see which Cards are late or due soon, so that I can plan without opening each Card.
The Board, List, Calendar, and Archived pages share the Board_Toolbar, the filters of Requirement 12, and the Card modal (`?card={id}`). They read the Cards through the existing list endpoint; the only new endpoint is the restore action. References: `01-board-desktop.png` for the toolbar; the List, Calendar, and Archived pages have no reference image and use the Component_Recipes (Open Question 63).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**List view**
1. [VIEW-01] THE Frontend SHALL provide `/app/b/{slug}/list` showing the Board's Cards (not archived, not soft-deleted) grouped by Column in Column order, one table section per Column with a header (Column name, count) and one row per Card: title, assignee (name and avatar), priority badge, category pill, and due date with its due badge. THE view SHALL apply the same search and filters as the Board view (Requirement 12) and the same query string, and a selection of a row SHALL open the Card modal. A Column with no Card matching shows "No cards".
2. [VIEW-02] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE List rows SHALL stack as cards with the same fields, with no horizontal scroll between 360 and 414 pixels, and each row's hit area SHALL be at least 44 pixels high.
**Calendar view**
3. [VIEW-03] THE Frontend SHALL provide `/app/b/{slug}/calendar`: a month grid (Monday first, matching the throughput week of THR-05) with each Card on its `end_date`, the month and year as a heading, "Previous month" and "Next month" controls and a "Today" control, and the same search and filters. A Card without an `end_date` SHALL NOT be shown. A day with more Cards than fit shows the first ones and a "+N more" control that opens that day's Cards in a `BasePopover`. A selection of a Card SHALL open the Card modal; the Calendar SHALL NOT support drag-and-drop in V17. THE grid SHALL open on the month of the browser's current date and mark that date (a known limit: near midnight the server's `APP_TIMEZONE` date may differ).
4. [VIEW-04] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE Calendar SHALL show the month grid with Cards as count dots and the selected day's Cards as a list below it, with no horizontal scroll between 360 and 414 pixels.
**Archived Cards**
5. [VIEW-05] THE Frontend SHALL provide `/app/b/{slug}/archived` showing the Board's archived Cards (Cards with `archived_at`, not soft-deleted) as rows with title, priority, category, archive date (`archived_at`), and a Restore action for a viewer who may archive (Requirement 4 criterion 6). THE Board toolbar SHALL link to it with an "Archived" control that shows the count. THE Backend SHALL return them from `GET /api/boards/{id}/cards?archived=true` (same access as the Board's Card list; the default stays `archived=false`).
6. [VIEW-06] THE Backend SHALL provide `POST /api/cards/{id}/restore`, for a Card_Creator with Board_Staff_Access (the rule of archiving), returning the Card. IF the Card is not archived, THEN it SHALL return HTTP 409 `CARD_NOT_ARCHIVED`. (If an archive-reversal route already exists, the task keeps that one route and documents it, not two.)
7. [VIEW-07] WHEN a Card is restored, THE Backend SHALL clear `archived_at`, set `column_id` to the Board's first Column by `order` (OQ47), place the Card at the end of that Column (`max_order + 1`, Requirement 6 ordering rules), apply COL-08 to `completed_at`, write one `card.restored` ActivityLog row in the same transaction, and notify nobody. THE Frontend SHALL ask no confirmation for Restore (it is not a create or a delete) and SHALL show "Card restored to <Column name>".
**Due badges**
8. [VIEW-08] THE Backend SHALL add a read-only `due_state` to every Card payload (list, detail, create, update, and the shared card): `overdue` when `end_date` is before today, `due_tomorrow` when `end_date` is tomorrow, and null otherwise, with "today" and "tomorrow" taken in `APP_TIMEZONE`, and always null for a Card in the Done_Column or without `end_date`. A posted `due_state` SHALL be HTTP 400. THE rule SHALL be one function, `due_state(end_date, column_name, today)`, used by the schema, My Tasks, and the Dashboard, so the three agree with the Overdue_Card definition of Requirement 10 (Property 79).
9. [VIEW-09] THE Frontend SHALL show a due badge on every Card tile, List row, and Calendar item from `due_state`: "Overdue" on the `danger` tone and "Due tomorrow" on the `warning` tone (`BaseBadge`, Component_Recipes), beside the date. A Card in Done shows none. THE badge SHALL carry text, not colour alone, and the existing "Overdue" red date chip of the Card modal (CardAttributes) SHALL read the same field. THE Frontend SHALL never compute the state from the browser date.
10. [VIEW-10] THE Backend SHALL keep a constant number of queries for the list endpoint with `archived=true` and with the default (Requirement 13, `selectinload` rules), and SHALL add the `board_id` index (COL-07) so the Archived list reads by Board directly.
### Requirement 51: Dashboard
> Amended by Requirement 65, V18 (Admin access, filter bar).
**User Story:** As a Supervisor or Manager, I want a greeting, filters, and a few clear widgets about late, upcoming, and finished work, so that I see what needs attention before I open a Board.
This requirement supersedes DASH-08 and DASH-09 (no greeting, limited widgets) and amends Requirement 43 (DASH-01 to DASH-07 stand, with the path `/app/dashboard`). The existing Overview card and the throughput chart stay. Reference: `design/reference/v2/03-dashboard-desktop.png` and `03-dashboard-mobile.png`; new widgets use the same card recipe. The Dashboard keeps the audience of Requirement 10: a Supervisor sees the Boards it owns, a Manager sees every Board; a Member and an Admin get HTTP 403 (Decision B).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Greeting and filters**
1. [DASH-10] THE Frontend SHALL show at the top of the Dashboard a greeting "Good morning", "Good afternoon", or "Good evening" followed by the signed-in user's first name (the first white-space-separated word of `full_name`, "there" when empty): morning from 00:00 to 11:59, afternoon from 12:00 to 17:59, evening from 18:00 to 23:59, by the browser's local time. THE greeting SHALL be one pure function, `greeting(date, fullName)`, rendered in a Client Component after mount (the server render shows "Hello"), so there is no hydration mismatch. THE greeting SHALL replace the plain "Dashboard" eyebrow; the document title stays `RevoBoard | Dashboard`.
2. [DASH-11] THE Dashboard SHALL offer three filters in the title block: Board (the existing `BaseCombobox`; a Supervisor sees its owned Boards, a Manager "All boards" and every Board), Member (a `BaseCombobox` of "Anyone" and the people in the selected scope), and Date range (two date fields and presets "Last 7 days", "Last 30 days", "This month"). THE date range SHALL default to the last 30 days (OQ49): `to` is today and `from` is 29 days earlier, in `APP_TIMEZONE`. A change of a filter SHALL refetch once and show the loading state (DASH-07). The filters SHALL be kept in the query string (`?board=&member=&from=&to=`) so a link reproduces the view.
3. [DASH-12] THE filters SHALL apply to the widgets as this table says (Open Question 54). Board and Member apply to every widget. THE date range applies to the widgets that have a date inside the range:
| Widget | Board and Member | Date range applies to |
|---|---|---|
| Overview (existing counts) | yes | no ("Not limited by the date range") |
| Completed per week (existing throughput) | yes | `completed_at` |
| Overdue cards | yes | `end_date` |
| Due tomorrow | yes | no: it has its own "Due on" date filter (default tomorrow), independent of the range (DASH-15) |
| Top 5 priority cards | yes | no ("Not limited by the date range") |
| Finished on time | yes | `completed_at` |
| Finished overdue | yes | `completed_at` |
THE Overview and Top 5 priority widgets, which ignore the range, SHALL show "Not limited by the date range" in `text-xs` `--ink-muted` text. THE Due tomorrow widget has its own filter (DASH-15) and shows no such note.
**Widgets**
4. [DASH-13] THE Dashboard SHALL keep the Overview card and the "Completed per week" chart (DASH-03 and DASH-04) and the Boards table (DASH-05) and Recent activity (DASH-06) as they are, now fed by the filters where the table says so.
5. [DASH-14] THE "Overdue cards" widget SHALL show the count and the first 10 Cards: Open_Cards whose `end_date` is before today (in `APP_TIMEZONE`) and inside the date range, sorted by `end_date` ascending, then priority (high first), then `id`. EACH row shows the title (a link that opens `/app/b/{slug}?card={id}`), the Board name, the priority badge, the assignee name, and the date with the "Overdue" badge. WHEN there are more than 10, THE widget SHALL show "and N more" (no link; the Board's List view is the place to see all).
6. [DASH-15] THE "Due tomorrow" widget (the H-1 list) SHALL have its own date filter, a "Due on" date field in the widget header that defaults to tomorrow and is independent of the global date range (Open Question 54, answered by the owner); it SHALL show the count and the first 10 Open_Cards whose `end_date` equals that date, in the same row layout, sorted by priority (high first), then `id`. WHEN the chosen date is not tomorrow, THE title SHALL read "Due on <date>". THE Board and Member filters still apply; the choice is kept in the query string as `due_on`.
7. [DASH-16] THE "Top 5 priority cards" widget SHALL show up to 5 Open_Cards, sorted by priority (`high`, `medium`, `low`), then the nearest `end_date` (a Card without `end_date` last), then `id`.
8. [DASH-17] THE "Finished on time" and "Finished overdue" widgets SHALL show a count each and, together, a stacked bar with a hidden data table: a Card is finished on time when its `completed_at`, converted to a date in `APP_TIMEZONE`, is on or before its `end_date`, and finished overdue when it is after. A Card without `end_date` SHALL be excluded from both figures and from the bar. ARCHIVED Cards SHALL count (the work was finished, THR-06); soft-deleted Cards SHALL NOT. THE two widgets SHALL show "On time N%" computed from the two counts, and "No finished cards in this period" when both are zero.
9. [DASH-18] THE Dashboard SHALL NOT show estimates, capacity, velocity percentages, or any claim the API does not return (exclusion 6 of V16).
**Endpoint**
10. [DASH-19] THE Backend SHALL provide `GET /api/analytics/dashboard?board=&member=&from=&to=&due_on=` in the `analytics` blueprint (`routes.py`, `service.py`, `repository.py`, `schema.py`), with the role rules of Requirement 10: a Supervisor reads only Boards it owns (a `board` it does not own is 403; no `board` means every Board it owns), a Manager reads any Board (no `board` means every Board), a Member and an Admin receive 403, no Token 401, and an unknown or soft-deleted `board` 404. `member` is a user id and filters Cards by `assignee_id`; an id that is not a UUID is 400. `due_on` is a `YYYY-MM-DD` date for the Due tomorrow widget and defaults to tomorrow in `APP_TIMEZONE` (an invalid date is 400); `from` and `to` are `YYYY-MM-DD`; `from` after `to`, a range longer than 182 days (26 weeks, the throughput limit of THR-07), or an invalid date is 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details`. WHEN `from` and `to` are absent, THE Backend SHALL use the last 30 days.
11. [DASH-20] THE response SHALL be one pre-aggregated Envelope `data` of: `range` (`from`, `to`), `today` (the date in `APP_TIMEZONE`), `overview` (`total`, `done`, `in_progress`, `overdue`), `throughput` (`weeks` and `total`, as THR-05, for the range, the whole weeks that touch it), `overdue` (`count`, `cards`), `due_tomorrow` (`count`, `cards`), `top_priority` (`cards`), `finished` (`on_time`, `overdue`), and `members` (`id`, `full_name`, and `avatar_url` of the people in scope, no email). A `cards` entry has `id`, `title`, `board_id`, `board_slug`, `board_name`, `priority`, `end_date`, `due_state`, `assignee_name`. NO other Card field and no raw dump of Cards SHALL be returned (Requirement 10 criterion 5).
12. [DASH-21] THE Backend SHALL answer with a number of database queries that does not depend on the number of Cards or Boards (a fixed budget stated in the design: at most 10), using grouped queries and `LIMIT`, and SHALL compute `due_state`, Overdue_Card, and the `completed_at` date with the same functions as the Board views (VIEW-08) and THR-05 (Property 78).
**States**
13. [DASH-22] THE Dashboard SHALL keep the loading, error with Retry, and empty states of DASH-07 for the new widgets; each widget with no rows SHALL show a short empty line ("Nothing overdue", "Nothing due tomorrow", "No cards to rank"); charts follow the chart rules (token colours, an accessible name, a hidden data table). WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE widgets SHALL stack in one column with no horizontal scroll between 360 and 414 pixels, and the filters SHALL wrap.
### Requirement 52: My Tasks
> Amended by Requirement 66, V18 (My Tasks for Member and Supervisor only).
**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want one list of the Cards assigned to me across my Boards, grouped by when they are due, so that I know what to do next.
My Tasks has no reference image and is built from the Component_Recipes (Open Question 63).
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [TASK-01] THE Frontend SHALL provide `/app/my-tasks`, in the Sidebar for a Member, a Supervisor, and a Manager (an Admin does not see the item, and an Admin who opens the path is redirected to `/app/boards`), listing the Cards assigned to the signed-in user on every Board the user can see (Requirement 22), excluding Cards in the Done_Column, archived Cards, soft-deleted Cards, and Cards of a soft-deleted Board. THE document title SHALL be `RevoBoard | My tasks`.
2. [TASK-02] THE page SHALL group the rows in this order: Overdue, Today, Tomorrow, This week, Later, No due date, with a heading and a count per group, and SHALL omit an empty group. THE groups in `APP_TIMEZONE`: Overdue is `end_date` before today; Today is today; Tomorrow is tomorrow; This week is every later day up to and including the Sunday of the current Monday-first week (empty when tomorrow is already in the next week); Later is every day after that; No due date is a null `end_date`. EVERY Card falls in exactly one group (Property 80).
3. [TASK-03] EACH row SHALL show the Card title, the Board name, the priority badge, and the due badge (VIEW-09), and SHALL be a link to `/app/b/{slug}?card={id}`, which opens the Board with the Card modal. WITHIN a group, rows are sorted by `end_date` ascending, then priority (high first), then `id`. THE page SHALL show an empty state "Nothing assigned to you" when no row exists, and loading and error-with-Retry states from the Design_System. WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, rows stack with a 44 pixel hit area.
4. [TASK-04] THE Backend SHALL provide `GET /api/me/cards`, for any signed-in user (an Admin gets an empty list, because an Admin cannot be an assignee, Requirement 22), returning one Envelope `data` of `{ "today": "YYYY-MM-DD", "groups": [ { "key", "cards": [...] } ] }` in the order of criterion 2, where a card has `id`, `title`, `board_id`, `board_slug`, `board_name`, `priority`, `end_date`, `due_state`, and `category` (`id`, `name`, `color`) or null. THE Backend SHALL compute the groups from `APP_TIMEZONE`, SHALL use a number of queries that does not depend on the number of Cards, SHALL return no other user's Card, and SHALL return no Email or description.
5. [TASK-05] THE Frontend SHALL read the groups from the API and SHALL NOT group by the browser date.
### Requirement 53: Users, Active, and Availability
> Amended by Requirement 63, V18 (order, paging, width).
**User Story:** As an Admin, I want to deactivate an account or mark a person unavailable instead of deleting them, so that history and assignments stay intact and work is never orphaned.
This requirement supersedes the Admin user delete (there is none in the specs of Requirement 1; the task removes any route or button that exists), amends Requirement 1 (sign-in) and Requirement 4 (assignee), and keeps Decision B. Reference: `design/reference/v2/08-user-management-desktop.png` and `08-user-management-mobile.png` for the table frame; the table layout itself is built from the Component_Recipes (Open Question 63).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Data**
1. [ADM-01] THE Backend SHALL add `users.is_active` (boolean, not null, default true) and `users.is_available` (boolean, not null, default true) in the migration of Requirement 59 (migration 3), which sets both to true for every existing user and drops both on downgrade. THE `user` payload (login, register, `GET /api/auth/me`, `GET /api/users`) SHALL carry `is_active` and `is_available`; a payload for another signed-in viewer (comments, members, candidates, assignees) SHALL carry neither flag except `assignee_is_active` and `assignee_is_available` on a Card (criterion 9).
**Admin screen**
2. [ADM-02] THE Frontend SHALL provide `/app/users` (Admin only; any other role is redirected to `/app/boards`; the Sidebar item is shown to an Admin only) as a table with the columns Name (avatar, full name, username), Email, Role (a `BaseCombobox`), Active (a switch), Available (a switch), and Update (a button), one row per user, and a "New user" button above the table. WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, rows SHALL stack as cards with the same fields, 44 pixel controls, and no horizontal scroll between 360 and 414 pixels. THE page SHALL keep the search of the V16 Admin users screen (SCR-17) and load users from `GET /api/users`. The document title SHALL be `RevoBoard | Users`. THE Email is shown here because the Admin may see it (Requirement 1 criteria on Admin users).
3. [ADM-03] THERE SHALL be no user delete: THE Backend SHALL NOT expose `DELETE /api/users/{id}` (a call receives HTTP 405), and THE Frontend SHALL show no delete control. THE task SHALL remove the route and the control if they exist.
4. [ADM-04] WHEN an Admin changes a role, Active, or Available switch in a row, THE Frontend SHALL stage the change in that row and enable the row's Update button; WHEN the Admin presses Update, THE Frontend SHALL open a ConfirmDialog naming the user and listing each staged change ("Role: Member to Supervisor", "Active: on to off", "Available: on to off"), and on confirm SHALL send one `PATCH /api/users/{id}` with only the changed fields and update the row from the response (Open Question 62). Cancel SHALL keep the staged values. The "New user" button SHALL open a form modal (full name, username, email, password, role) and the submit SHALL open a ConfirmDialog ("Create user “ada”?") before `POST /api/users`.
**Deactivation rules**
5. [ADM-05] IF an Admin sets `is_active` to false for a user who has Open_Cards assigned, or who owns a Board that has Open_Cards (any assignee), THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 with the code `USER_HAS_OPEN_WORK` and `error.details` of `{ "cards": [ { "id", "title", "board_id", "board_slug", "board_name" } ], "boards": [ { "id", "slug", "name", "open_card_count" } ], "cards_total", "boards_total" }`, listing at most 50 of each (the totals are exact), and SHALL change nothing. Cards in the Done_Column, archived Cards, and soft-deleted Cards SHALL NOT block. THE Frontend SHALL show the lists in a modal with a link to each Board (`/app/b/{slug}`) and each Card (`/app/b/{slug}?card={id}`) and the text "Reassign or finish this work first." (Property 74).
6. [ADM-06] IF an Admin sets `is_active` to false on their own account, THEN THE Backend SHALL return HTTP 409 `CANNOT_DEACTIVATE_SELF` (Open Question 59). THE Backend SHALL allow `is_active` and `is_available` to be set by an Admin only; a `PATCH /api/users/{id}` or `PATCH /api/auth/me` that sends `is_active` from a non-Admin SHALL receive HTTP 403, and a non-Admin's `is_available` change is allowed on their own account only (ADM-10).
7. [ADM-07] WHEN a user becomes inactive, THE Backend SHALL end every session of that user at once (Open Question 60): every authenticated request reads `is_active` from the user row and, when it is false, SHALL return HTTP 403 with the code `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` and the message "This account is disabled"; no revocation list is added. THE Frontend SHALL treat `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` on any call like a lost session: remove the Session_Cookie and go to `/app/session-expired`. AN inactive user SHALL NOT be able to sign in: `POST /api/auth/login` with the right password SHALL return HTTP 403 `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, and with a wrong password the usual 401 (so the response never says whether a disabled account exists), and the failure still counts for the rate limiter (Requirement 20). WHEN both `is_active` is false and the email is not verified, `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` wins.
**Pickers and markers**
8. [ADM-08] THE Backend SHALL list only users who are both active and available in the member picker (`GET /api/boards/{id}/candidates`) and the assignee picker (`GET /api/boards/{id}/members?assignable=true`; the Frontend's assignee popover SHALL send it), and SHALL refuse with HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (`details.assignee_id` or `details.user_id`) an assignment or a direct `POST /api/boards/{id}/members` for a user who is not (Property 73). THE Members view SHALL still list every member of the Board, with an "Inactive" or "Unavailable" tag beside such a person. A Join_Request approval SHALL NOT be blocked by the flags.
9. [ADM-09] A Card in the Done_Column SHALL keep its assignee after the assignee is deactivated (nothing clears it). THE Card payload SHALL carry `assignee_is_active` and `assignee_is_available` beside `assignee_name` (the batched name lookup, no extra query per row; no Email), and `BaseAvatar` SHALL show an "Inactive" marker (a muted ring and the text in its tooltip and `aria-label`) when the assignee is inactive and an "Unavailable" marker when the assignee is unavailable; where both hold, "Inactive" wins. THE Shared_View card carries neither flag.
10. [ADM-10] AN Admin SHALL be able to set both toggles of any user (ADM-04), and a user SHALL be able to set their own `is_available` through `PATCH /api/auth/me` (PRF-04). EVERY toggle change SHALL go through ConfirmDialog (ADM-04 and PRF-04). Making a user unavailable SHALL NOT change their open Cards or sessions.
### Requirement 54: Profile
**User Story:** As a signed-in user, I want a profile page where I manage who I am, how I sign in, and what reaches me, so that my account stays correct and secure.
This requirement supersedes Requirement 29 and Decision AF (two-column layout), PROF-01 (username and email read-only), and PROF-04 (the `PATCH /api/auth/me` body is limited to `full_name`) of Requirement 19 (Decision BG). The page has no reference image; it is drawn from the Component_Recipes and checked in the owner's review (Open Question 63).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Layout**
1. [PRF-01] THE Frontend SHALL draw `/app/profile` like a social profile page: a cover band (a `--gradient-brand` band of the `card` radius role across the top of the frame), a large avatar (the `size-icon-lg` family or a new `--size-avatar-xl` token) overlapping the lower edge of the band, then the full name (`text-title`) and the username (`--ink-muted`, prefixed with `@`), then a tab list with About, Security, and Notifications (`role="tablist"`, arrow-key navigation, `aria-selected`). THE active tab SHALL be kept in the query (`?tab=about|security|notifications`, default About). THE tab primitive is a new `BaseTabs` in `components/shared/` over the shadcn/ui `Tabs` (the dependency `@radix-ui/react-tabs` is recorded in the design). THE document title SHALL be `RevoBoard | Profile`. THE page SHALL fit 360 to 1280 pixels, the cover band and avatar scaling with token sizes and no horizontal scroll.
**About**
2. [PRF-02] THE About tab SHALL edit: full name; username, with the live availability check (Requirement 1, the debounced `GET /api/auth/availability`, the same format rules 3 to 30 of `a-z 0-9 _ .`); email; and the avatar (choose, preview, upload, remove, as Requirement 19, with the remove asking through ConfirmDialog). THE user saves changed fields with one Save button that is disabled until a field changed and valid; a username or full name change SHALL be a `PATCH /api/auth/me` with only the changed keys. A username that is taken SHALL show the field error "Username is taken" from `details.username`. THE signed-in user's own Email MAY be shown here (it is their own).
3. [PRF-03] WHEN the user changes the email, THE Backend SHALL NOT replace `users.email`: it SHALL store the new address (lower-cased, validated by Requirement 1 criterion 48, not equal to any user's `email` or any other user's `pending_email`) in `users.pending_email`, create an Email_Token of purpose `change_email` (Requirement 59), and send a confirmation link to the NEW address after the commit. The old email SHALL stay the sign-in and notification address until the link is confirmed. THE response SHALL return the `user` with `pending_email`, and HTTP 409 `CONFLICT` with `details.email` when the address is taken. THE About tab SHALL show "Waiting for confirmation: <pending email>" with a "Resend link" action (`POST /api/auth/me/email/resend`, rate limited under the scope of Requirement 59) and a "Cancel change" action (`DELETE /api/auth/me/email/pending`, which clears `pending_email` and invalidates its tokens; asked through ConfirmDialog). WHEN the link is confirmed (`POST /api/auth/verify`), THE Backend SHALL replace `email` with `pending_email`, clear `pending_email`, mark the token used, and fail with HTTP 409 `CONFLICT` if the address was taken in the meantime. A second change request before confirmation SHALL replace the pending address and invalidate the earlier tokens. `pending_email` SHALL be visible to its owner only and SHALL never appear in another user's payload, an activity row, or a notification.
4. [PRF-04] THE About tab SHALL show an "Available for work" switch bound to `users.is_available`; changing it SHALL open a ConfirmDialog and, on confirm, send `PATCH /api/auth/me { "is_available" }`. A user SHALL NOT be able to change their own `is_active` (ADM-06).
**Security**
5. [PRF-05] THE Security tab SHALL hold the change-password form: current password (required), new password (at least 8 characters, Requirement 1), and a confirmation field that must match (client check only). THE Backend SHALL require `current_password` on `POST /api/auth/change-password` and return HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` (never 401) for a wrong one, with the rate limit of Requirement 20 (scope `change_password`). WHEN the password is changed, THE Backend SHALL, after the commit, send the email "Your password was changed" to the account's email (the confirmed `email`, not `pending_email`); a failed send SHALL be logged and SHALL NOT fail the request (ACT-09). THE email SHALL carry the time of the change and no password or token. Other sessions stay valid (exclusion 8).
**Notifications**
6. [PRF-06] THE Notifications tab SHALL show one switch per type of Requirement 57, with its label and a one-line description, loaded from `GET /api/me/notification-preferences`. EACH switch SHALL save at once through `PUT /api/me/notification-preferences` with the V12 field status (Saving, Saved, or an error with Retry in an `aria-live="polite"` region), roll back on failure, and need no ConfirmDialog (a preference is neither a create nor a delete). The tab SHALL say "These control in-app notifications only."
**Rules that stay**
7. [PRF-07] THE avatar rules (Requirement 19, AVT-01 to AVT-16: PNG, JPEG, or WebP up to 2 MB, signed URL, initials fallback) and the `BaseAvatar` use SHALL stay unchanged. THE User_Menu keeps Profile above Log out (HDR-02 to HDR-05).
8. [PRF-08] THE Backend SHALL validate `username` on change by Requirement 1 criterion 47 and its uniqueness case-insensitively, and SHALL keep PROF-08: a successful profile change (name, username, email request, availability) or password change writes one Activity_Log row (`user.profile_updated` or `user.password_changed`, target type `user`) in the same transaction, with no password, hash, Token, or email address in `metadata`. A username change takes effect for sign-in at once.
9. [PRF-09] THE `PATCH /api/auth/me` body SHALL accept `full_name`, `username`, `email`, and `is_available` only; any other key (including `role`, `is_active`, `pending_email`, `email_verified_at`) SHALL be HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`.
10. [PRF-10] THE availability check for the profile's username SHALL exclude the caller's own current username, so keeping the name is "available"; THE check stays rate limited (Requirement 20).
### Requirement 55: Share Modal
**User Story:** As a Board_Owner, Manager, or Admin, I want one place to manage how people reach my Board, so that I do not hunt for two buttons and two modals.
This requirement supersedes SHELL-17, PUB-09, and INV-19 to INV-21 for layout only (Decision BH). Behaviour, endpoints, permissions, and visibility rules of Requirements 23, 24, and 31 stay unchanged: the Public_Link is managed by a Manager and an Admin only (PUB-01, PUB-02), the Invite_Link by the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin (INV-02 to INV-05), join requests are decided by a Manager and an Admin only (INV-14 to INV-16). Reference: `design/reference/v2/06-invite-public-link-desktop.png` and `06-invite-public-link-mobile.png`.
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [SHR-01] THE Frontend SHALL replace the separate "Public link" and "Invite" buttons and modals with one Share item in the Top_Menu (NAV-04) that opens one Share_Modal (`BaseModal`), drawn as `06-invite-public-link-desktop.png` with the Component_Recipes, titled "Share board". THE item SHALL be shown to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin, and to nobody else. WHERE the viewer decides on requests (a Manager or an Admin) and the Board has pending Join_Requests, THE item SHALL show their count as a teal count badge, capped at "9+".
2. [SHR-02] THE Share_Modal SHALL hold, in this order: (a) the Invite link section: the state, "Create invite link" when off, the full link (the origin plus the `path` of the API, with a copy button, `invite_token` never shown elsewhere), Regenerate, and Disable; (b) the pending Join_Requests list: avatar or initials, full name, username, email, and the time, with Approve and Reject for a Manager and an Admin and no action for the Board_Owner (INV-20, INV-27); (c) the Public link section, with the same controls and read-only wording. EACH section SHALL show only to the roles that may use it: (a) and (b) to the Board_Owner, a Manager, and an Admin; (c) to a Manager and an Admin only (the owner who is neither sees no Public link section and no hint of one).
3. [SHR-03] EVERY create, regenerate, and disable in the Share_Modal SHALL go through ConfirmDialog (FIX-07; the V12 popover forms are replaced). Copy, Approve, and Reject SHALL NOT. A Join_Request decision SHALL keep its V12 behaviour (INV-14, INV-15) and show the result inline.
4. [SHR-04] THE modal SHALL open on `?invite=1` (the Inbox link of a `board_join_requested` notification, Requirement 28) and on the Share item, SHALL keep a copied link out of the document title and the URL, and SHALL send no token to a log. THE Frontend SHALL build the link from `window.location.origin` and the API's `path` (`/app/invite/{token}`, `/app/shared/{token}`, PATH-04). Closing the modal SHALL return focus to the Share item.
5. [SHR-05] THE modal SHALL fit 360 to 414 pixels as a full-width sheet with 44 pixel actions and no horizontal scroll, keep a focus trap and Escape (BaseModal), and show loading and error-with-Retry states for each section independently. THE Shared_View, the invite page, and every API route stay as they are.
### Requirement 56: Card Modal
> Amended by Requirements 68 and 69, V18 (layout, paging, permissions).
**User Story:** As a user, I want the Card modal to look like the owner's new design, with the key attributes in one row, so that I can read and edit a Card at a glance.
This requirement rebuilds the layout of the Card modal (Requirement 14 criterion 8, Requirement 25, Requirement 40 criteria for the modal) to match `design/reference/v2/04-card-modal-desktop.png` and `04-card-modal-mobile.png`. Behaviour, permissions, auto-save, and payloads do not change (Decision BH).
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [CM-01] THE Frontend SHALL draw the Card modal to match `04-card-modal-desktop.png` with the Component_Recipes: the same header, panes, and footer structure as today (a fixed header, two panes that scroll on their own, and a fixed footer) with the reference's spacing, radii, and type, and no value that is not a token. THE footer SHALL hold Archive and Delete (the Delete through ConfirmDialog, FIX-07) and no Save changes or Close button (V12). Below 960 pixels it SHALL follow `04-card-modal-mobile.png` as a full-screen sheet with one scrolling column.
2. [CM-02] THE modal SHALL show under the title one **attribute chip row** with, in this order: status, priority, assignee, dates, and category. THE status chip SHALL show the Card's Column name (a `BaseBadge` with the Column's dot colour) and SHALL be read-only (Open Question 58, exclusion 5); a Card changes Column only by drag-and-drop. THE priority, assignee, dates, and category chips SHALL keep their popovers, their one-field auto-save (Requirement 25), and their permissions (Requirement 4); the assignee popover SHALL list only active and available users (ADM-08); the dates chip shows the due badge (VIEW-09).
3. [CM-03] THE modal SHALL keep the cards of the left and right panes (Details, Attachments, Subtasks; Comments, Activity), the comment rules (Requirement 7), the Subtask rules (Requirement 27), the attachment rules (the file and link behaviour), and the rule that comments and activity render only when the API sends them. IT SHALL add nothing the reference draws that has no spec basis (exclusions of V15 and V16: estimate, `status` dropdown, `@mention`, emoji, bookmark, share and Done buttons, a second assignee).
4. [CM-04] THE modal SHALL open from `/app/b/{slug}?card={id}` and from the List, Calendar, and Archived views, SHALL set and restore `document.title` (Requirement 32), SHALL show an archived Card read-only with a Restore action (VIEW-07) when opened from the Archived view, and SHALL show the not-available message of Requirement 28 when the Card is archived (outside the Archived view), deleted, or inaccessible.
5. [CM-05] THE Frontend SHALL keep the fixed-size rules (every pane scrolls on its own, `lg:grid-rows-1`), the `aria-live="polite"` field status, the focus trap, Escape to close, and the "closing waits for a save in flight" rule of Requirement 25. THE existing tests of the Card modal SHALL be updated, not deleted (task 39.12 rule).
### Requirement 57: Notification Preferences
**User Story:** As an assignee, I want to choose which events about my Cards reach my Inbox, so that I see what matters and nothing else.
This requirement adds five Notification types and per-user switches (Decision BI). It amends Requirement 9 (types and the Inbox), Requirement 28 (the type list and the Inbox links), and the `Notification` rule of `data-model.md`. The `assigned`, `board_join_requested`, `board_added`, and `board_join_rejected` types of V12 are not preference-controlled and keep their behaviour. In-app notifications only: email notifications are out of scope (exclusion 1).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Data**
1. [NPF-01] THE Backend SHALL add the table `notification_preferences` with `id` (UUID), `user_id` (foreign key, not null), `type` (string, not null), `enabled` (boolean, not null), `created_at`, and `updated_at`, with a unique index on `(user_id, type)`, in migration 4. A missing row for a user and type means enabled, so no backfill is needed.
2. [NPF-02] THE Backend SHALL define five preference types: `comment_on_assigned` (another user comments or replies on a Card assigned to me), `due_today` (a Card assigned to me is due today), `due_tomorrow` (a Card assigned to me is due tomorrow, the H-1 reminder), `assigned_card_changed` (a Card assigned to me is moved to another Column, archived, or deleted), and `subtask_on_assigned` (a Subtask on a Card assigned to me is created, its title or done state changes, or it is deleted; a reorder alone does not count). EACH is also a value of `Notification.type`; migration 4 SHALL extend the type check or enum and keep `assigned`, `deadline`, and the three board types.
**Creation rules**
3. [NPF-03] THE Backend SHALL create a notification of a preference type only when the recipient has that type enabled, in the same transaction as the triggering action (backend steering rule), with `type`, `card_id`, `board_id`, `actor_id` (null for the due types), and `notify_date`. THE recipient is the Card's assignee at that moment; a Card with no assignee notifies no one. THE actor SHALL never notify themselves: when the actor is the assignee, no row is created. NO row is created for an inactive user (ADM-07).
4. [NPF-04] WHEN a comment or a reply is created on a Card, THE Backend SHALL create one `comment_on_assigned` notification for the assignee (one per comment, even when a reply also targets another author). WHEN a Card is moved to another Column, archived, or soft-deleted by a user who is not its assignee, THE Backend SHALL create one `assigned_card_changed` notification; a reorder inside the same Column, a field edit, and a restore SHALL NOT. WHEN a Subtask of a Card is created, changed (title or `is_done`), or deleted by a user who is not the assignee, THE Backend SHALL create one `subtask_on_assigned` notification.
5. [NPF-05] THE notification SHALL carry no comment text, no Subtask title, no Email, and no token: the Inbox row is rebuilt from `type`, `actor_name`, `card_title`, and `board_name` (Requirement 28, criterion on Notification fields), so a deleted comment or Subtask leaves no copy of its text (V12 comment deletion rule). THE Backend SHALL add `board_slug` to the payload (SLUG-07).
6. [NPF-06] THE dedupe indexes of V12 SHALL be changed so that event notifications are never refused: THE card-row unique index `uq_notifications_card` SHALL apply only to the types `assigned`, `deadline`, `due_today`, and `due_tomorrow` (`postgresql_where` and `sqlite_where` on `card_id IS NOT NULL AND type IN (...)`), so a second comment, move, or Subtask change on the same day on the same Card creates a second row (Open Question 56). THE index for join rows and the index for other board rows stay.
**Endpoints**
7. [NPF-07] THE Backend SHALL provide `GET /api/me/notification-preferences`, returning `{ "preferences": [ { "type", "enabled" } ] }` with exactly the five types in the order of NPF-02, `enabled` true where no row exists; and `PUT /api/me/notification-preferences` with the body `{ "preferences": [ { "type", "enabled" } ] }` of one to five distinct entries, which upserts the listed types in one transaction and returns the same shape as `GET`. AN unknown type, a duplicate type, an empty list, or a non-boolean `enabled` SHALL receive HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details`. BOTH routes are scoped to the signed-in user (no `user_id` in the path or body), return 401 without a Token, and live in the `notifications` blueprint with `@blp.arguments` and `@blp.response`.
8. [NPF-08] THE Frontend SHALL show the switches in the Notifications tab of the Profile (PRF-06) and SHALL show each type's Inbox row text: "<Actor> commented on “<Card>”", "“<Card>” is due today", "“<Card>” is due tomorrow", "<Actor> <action> “<Card>”" where `action` is `moved`, `archived`, or `deleted` (NPF-09), and "<Actor> updated a subtask on “<Card>”". THE `hrefFor` function SHALL open the Card (`cardPath(slug, id)`) for `comment_on_assigned`, `due_today`, `due_tomorrow`, `subtask_on_assigned`, and `assigned_card_changed` of a moved Card, and the Board for an archived or deleted Card with the not-available message of Requirement 28.
9. [NPF-09] THE `assigned_card_changed` Notification payload SHALL carry `action` (`moved`, `archived`, or `deleted`), stored in a nullable `Notification.action` column added by migration 4; no other type carries it.
### Requirement 58: Due-Date Job
**User Story:** As an assignee, I want a reminder on the day a Card is due and the day before, so that I do not miss a deadline.
This requirement supersedes Requirement 9 criteria 2 and 3 (the daily `deadline` job and its idempotence) and the scheduler of Requirement 33 and 37 (Decision BI, Open Question 56). Reference for the Inbox row: `11-inbox-desktop.png`.
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [DUE-01] THE Backend SHALL provide the Flask CLI command `flask notify-due` in `app/jobs/due.py`, registered in the app factory, run every 15 minutes (OQ50). IT SHALL take "today" and "tomorrow" as dates in `APP_TIMEZONE` at the time of the run, with no per-user timezone, and SHALL print one line of counts (`due_today=N due_tomorrow=M skipped=K`) and exit with 0, or non-zero when an unexpected error occurs.
2. [DUE-02] THE command SHALL create a `due_today` notification for the assignee of each Card whose `end_date` is today and a `due_tomorrow` notification for each whose `end_date` is tomorrow, for Cards that are not soft-deleted, not archived, not in the Done_Column, and have an assignee who is active, respecting the assignee's preference (NPF-03). THE `notify_date` is today's date in `APP_TIMEZONE`.
3. [DUE-03] THE command SHALL be idempotent: it SHALL insert with `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` against the unique key `(user_id, card_id, type, notify_date)` of the partial index of NPF-06, so a second run on the same date inserts nothing and an overlapping run cannot create a duplicate (Property 76). IT SHALL also take a PostgreSQL advisory lock for its duration and exit 0 at once when another run holds it.
4. [DUE-04] A Card whose `end_date` is changed during the day SHALL be picked up by the next run: a Card moved to today after the first run gets its `due_today` at the next 15 minute run; a Card that was due today and is moved to a later date keeps the notification it already received. THE first run after local midnight creates the day's notifications; the spec does not hold them for a working hour.
5. [DUE-05] THE command SHALL create no `deadline` notification (the V12 type is no longer produced; existing rows still display). THE `app/jobs/deadlines.py` module and its scheduler hook SHALL be replaced by `due.py`; the tests of `test_scheduler.py` SHALL be updated for the new command, not deleted.
6. [DUE-06] THE deploy scripts SHALL install the schedule `*/15 * * * *` running `flask notify-due` with the application environment, and `deploy/README.md` SHALL document it, its log file, and how to run it by hand. WHERE the server cannot run cron (the unprivileged container of Open Question 31), THE bare-mode `supervisord` configuration SHALL run a program that loops `flask notify-due` then `sleep 900`, and the Docker mode SHALL run the same loop as a service named `scheduler`. THE scripts SHALL NOT start both a cron entry and the loop.
7. [DUE-07] THE command SHALL log counts and ids only (no Card titles, no Email), SHALL finish a run over 10000 candidate Cards with a number of queries that does not depend on the number of Cards (one select for candidates joined to preferences, one bulk insert), and SHALL be covered by tests on PostgreSQL for the unique key and the advisory lock and on SQLite for the logic (Requirement 15).
### Requirement 59: Account Activation and Email
**User Story:** As the product owner, I want every new account to prove it owns its email address, and email changes to be confirmed, so that accounts are real and an address cannot be taken over by typing it.
This requirement amends Requirement 1 (register and login) and Requirement 19, and adds the mail transport used by Requirements 54 and 59 (Decision BJ). `email_verified_at` is separate from `is_active` (Requirement 53): the two have different codes and messages. Activation uses the Email_Token table for both the verification link and the email-change link.
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Data and rules**
1. [ACT-01] THE Backend SHALL add `users.email_verified_at` (nullable timestamp with time zone) in migration 3. A user with a null value SHALL NOT sign in: `POST /api/auth/login` SHALL return HTTP 403 with the code `ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVATED` and the message "Confirm your email address first" only after the identifier and password have both matched (a wrong identifier or password SHALL return the usual 401, so the code never reveals that an account exists). THE two states SHALL differ: `ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVATED` (403, "Confirm your email address first") and `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` (403, "This account is disabled"); WHEN both apply, `ACCOUNT_DISABLED` wins (ADM-07). A login refused with either code SHALL add no failure row to the rate limiter (the password was correct).
2. [ACT-02] WHEN a visitor calls `POST /api/auth/register`, THE Backend SHALL create the user with `email_verified_at` null, create an Email_Token of purpose `verify_email` that expires 24 hours later, send the verification email after the commit, and return HTTP 201 with `data` of `{ "user": {...}, "token": null }` (amends the shared shape of the auth rule: register returns no Token). NO Token SHALL be issued before verification (Property 77).
3. [ACT-03] THE Backend SHALL provide `POST /api/auth/verify` with `{ "token": string }` (no Token needed): it SHALL look up the SHA-256 hash of the value among unused tokens, and WHEN the token is valid and unexpired, mark it used in the same transaction, and for `verify_email` set `email_verified_at` to now, and for `change_email` replace `users.email` with `pending_email` and clear it (PRF-03), and return HTTP 200 with `data` of `{ "purpose" }`. IF the value is unknown or already used, THEN it SHALL return HTTP 400 `TOKEN_INVALID`; IF it is expired, THEN HTTP 400 `TOKEN_EXPIRED`. A token SHALL work once; the second call receives `TOKEN_INVALID`. THE request SHALL NOT sign the user in.
4. [ACT-04] THE Backend SHALL provide `POST /api/auth/resend-verification` with `{ "email": string }` (no Token needed). IT SHALL always return HTTP 200 with the same message ("If that address needs confirming, a new link was sent") whether or not the address exists or is already verified, so it reveals nothing; IT SHALL invalidate the user's earlier unused `verify_email` tokens and create a new one only when the user exists and is not verified. IT SHALL be rate limited under a new scope `resend_verification` of `RateLimitAttempt` (Requirement 20, the shared Postgres limiter, key = a keyed hash of the lower-cased email plus the client address, and a per-address bucket), with thresholds from environment variables (`RATE_LIMIT_RESEND_VERIFICATION_MAX`, default 3 per email and address and 10 per address in `RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW_SECONDS`), returning HTTP 429 `RATE_LIMITED` with `retry_after` and `Retry-After`. THE same limiter protects `POST /api/auth/me/email/resend`.
5. [ACT-05] THE migration SHALL set `email_verified_at` to the migration time for every existing user, so no one is locked out. A user created by an Admin through `POST /api/users` SHALL be verified at creation, and `flask seed-users` and `flask seed-data` SHALL create verified users (Requirement 36).
6. [ACT-06] THE Backend SHALL store tokens only as hashes in the `email_tokens` table: `id` (UUID), `user_id` (foreign key, not null), `purpose` (`verify_email` or `change_email`), `token_hash` (string, unique, the SHA-256 hex digest of the token), `expires_at` (not null), `used_at` (nullable), and `created_at`. A token SHALL be at least 256 bits from `secrets.token_urlsafe(32)`, SHALL expire 24 hours after creation for both purposes, SHALL appear only in the email body and the one link, and SHALL NOT appear in a log, an Activity_Log row, a notification, an error message, or an API response (the `token` of the register response is the sign-in Token and is null). THE `email_tokens` table is append and mark-used; rows are not updated otherwise, and expired rows MAY be deleted by `flask purge-email-tokens` (optional, not scheduled).
**Frontend**
7. [ACT-07] THE Frontend SHALL, after a successful register, go to `/app/verify` showing "Check your email" with the address (kept in `sessionStorage`, not the URL), the sentence "We sent a link to confirm your address. It expires in 24 hours.", a "Resend email" button (disabled with a mm:ss countdown on a 429, as the login form does), and a link to sign in. WHEN `/app/verify?token=...` loads, THE Frontend SHALL call `POST /api/auth/verify` once and show "Email confirmed" with a "Sign in" button (for `verify_email`) or "Email address updated" (for `change_email`), or an error state with "Send a new link" (for `TOKEN_EXPIRED` and `TOKEN_INVALID`). THE page SHALL send `Referrer-Policy: no-referrer` and `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` (the token must not leak) and SHALL remove the token from the address bar after reading it. WHEN login returns `ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVATED`, THE login form SHALL show the message and a "Resend confirmation email" action; WHEN it returns `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, the form SHALL show the message and no resend.
**Mail transport**
8. [ACT-08] THE Backend SHALL send mail through one module, `app/common/mail.py`, with two backends chosen by `MAIL_BACKEND`: `smtp` and `console` (the default in development and testing; `smtp` is required when `APP_ENV` is production). THE `smtp` backend SHALL use Gmail SMTP (`smtp.gmail.com`, port 587, STARTTLS, an app password) configured only by `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASSWORD`, and `MAIL_FROM`, and `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` for the links (for example `https://revoboard.example/app/verify?token=...`). THE `console` backend SHALL write the message to the application log without the SMTP credentials, and tests SHALL use an in-memory outbox. NO credential SHALL appear in code, a committed file, a log, or an error message; `backend/.env.example` SHALL list the variables empty (Requirement 33 and `tech.md`). THE startup SHALL fail with a clear message when `MAIL_BACKEND=smtp` and any of the five variables is empty.
9. [ACT-09] THE Backend SHALL send each email after the database commit (never inside the transaction) from a small bounded worker so the request does not wait for SMTP; a failed send SHALL be logged (user id, purpose, error class; no address, no token) and SHALL NOT fail the request or roll anything back, with one retry after 5 seconds. EACH message SHALL be plain text with a simple HTML part, carry the product name, state what happened, and contain no password. THE messages are: "Confirm your RevoBoard email" (verification), "Confirm your new RevoBoard email" (email change, sent to the new address), and "Your RevoBoard password was changed" (PRF-05, sent to the confirmed address).
10. [ACT-10] THE `.env.example` files, `compose.yaml`, `deploy/bare/supervisor.conf`, the server environment template, and `deploy/README.md` SHALL list `MAIL_BACKEND`, `SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, `SMTP_USER`, `SMTP_PASSWORD`, `MAIL_FROM`, `PUBLIC_BASE_URL`, and `RATE_LIMIT_RESEND_VERIFICATION_MAX`, with a note that a Gmail account needs 2-step verification and an app password (Open Question 64).
11. [ACT-11] THE Backend SHALL document the new routes and codes (`ACCOUNT_NOT_ACTIVATED`, `ACCOUNT_DISABLED`, `TOKEN_INVALID`, `TOKEN_EXPIRED`) in `openapi.json` and `API.md`, SHALL keep `GET /api/auth/availability` and the login rate limit as they are, and SHALL regenerate both documents.
12. [ACT-12] THE Frontend SHALL keep the visitor's destination through register, verification, and sign-in, so an invite link still leads to its Join_Request (Open Question 69): the register and login pages accept `?next=<safe path>` under the PATH-05 rules; WHEN register succeeds, THE Frontend SHALL store `next` in `sessionStorage` with the address and go to `/app/verify`; THE "Sign in" button after "Email confirmed" SHALL go to `/app/login?next=<stored next>` when the verification happens in the same browser, and to `/app/login` otherwise; after sign-in THE Frontend SHALL go to `next` when it is a safe path. THE signed-out invite page's "Create account" and "Sign in" links SHALL pass `next=/app/invite/{token}`. THE verification email link SHALL NOT carry `next` (no invite token in an email the Backend writes), and `next` SHALL never be sent to the Backend.
### Requirement 60: Toasts
**User Story:** As a user, I want every action to answer me in one place, so that I always know whether it worked without scanning the page.
This requirement adds a Toast channel (Decision BK). It supersedes the inline success messages and the non-field error banners of earlier versions for **action results**. It does not change the page states (loading, error with Retry, empty), the field errors of forms, the V12 auto-save status, or the error area of an open ConfirmDialog (Open Question 80).
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [TOAST-01] THE Frontend SHALL show a Toast at the bottom right of the viewport for the result of every user action that writes data (create, update, delete, move, invite, join, restore, upload, mark read in bulk, sign out excepted): a success Toast after a 2xx answer and an error Toast after a non-2xx answer or a network failure. THE Toast library SHALL be `sonner`, wrapped once in `components/shared/BaseToaster.tsx` and called only through `lib/toast.ts` (`toast.success(message)`, `toast.error(message)`), so no component imports `sonner` directly.
2. [TOAST-02] THE error Toast SHALL show the API Envelope `message` (or "Something went wrong. Try again." for a network failure or an empty message); IT SHALL NOT show a stack, a code, or a raw status. THE success Toast SHALL use a short past-tense sentence naming the thing ("Column added", "Card restored to To Do", "User updated").
3. [TOAST-03] WHILE the viewport is below 960 pixels, THE Toasts SHALL appear at the bottom centre, full width minus the page gutter, above the mobile safe area. AT most 3 Toasts SHALL be visible; a fourth pushes out the oldest.
4. [TOAST-04] A success Toast SHALL close after 4 seconds and an error Toast after 6 seconds; both SHALL pause while hovered or focused and SHALL have a close control with the name "Dismiss". THE Toaster region SHALL be an `aria-live` region (`polite` for success, `assertive` for error) so a screen reader announces it, and a Toast SHALL NOT take focus.
5. [TOAST-05] THE Toast SHALL use Design_System tokens only: `--surface-raised`, `--ink`, `--border`, the `success` and `danger` tones for the icon, `--radius-md`, `--shadow-lg`, the spacing scale of Requirement 61, and the light and dark themes.
6. [TOAST-06] A field error of a form (a 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details`) SHALL stay next to its field (Requirement 45) and SHALL NOT also raise a Toast; a 400 without `details` raises an error Toast. WHILE a ConfirmDialog is open, its action error SHALL stay in the dialog (COL-05) and SHALL NOT raise a Toast; a success closes the dialog and raises a success Toast.
7. [TOAST-07] Auto-saved edits (the Card modal fields of Requirement 25) SHALL keep the inline "Saving / Saved" status and SHALL raise only error Toasts, not a success Toast per keystroke save.

### Requirement 61: Design Consistency
**User Story:** As the product owner, I want the same space, size, and colour rules on every screen, so that the product looks like one product.
This requirement adds scales to the Design_System (Decision BL) and applies them to every screen of V1 to V18. It supersedes any per-component spacing written before V18 that conflicts with it.
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Scales**
1. [DS-01] THE Design_System SHALL define these tokens in `styles/tokens.css` and map them in `@theme` so Tailwind utilities use them: `--space-label` 8px (label to control), `--space-field` 16px (between form fields and between controls in a toolbar), `--space-section` 24px (between sections and between cards of a grid), `--space-page` 24px below 960 pixels and 32px from 960 pixels (page gutter and the space under the page title), `--control-h` 40px (input, select, button, search, date field), `--control-h-sm` 32px (compact controls in a Column header and table rows), `--chip-h` 24px (badge, pill, chip, filter chip), and `--progress-h` 8px (progress bars).
2. [DS-02] EVERY input, select, combobox, search field, date field, and button of default size SHALL be `--control-h` high, and every compact control `--control-h-sm`; the text size inside follows the existing type scale (`text-sm`). No component SHALL set its own height with a number.
3. [DS-03] EVERY chip-like element (priority badge, due badge, category pill, column badge, role badge, filter chip, count pill, "Overdue" date chip) SHALL be `--chip-h` high with the same horizontal padding, radius (`--radius-full`), and text size (`text-xs`, `font-medium`). THE Board_Toolbar progress pill and its bar SHALL use `--chip-h` and `--progress-h`; the toolbar search field SHALL use `--control-h`, so the toolbar has two heights only.
4. [DS-04] EVERY labelled field SHALL put `--space-label` between the label and the control, and a hint or error line SHALL sit `--space-label` below the control. Forms SHALL stack fields with `--space-field`, and form sections with `--space-section`.
5. [DS-05] EVERY page SHALL use `--space-page` as its gutter and below its title block, and SHALL separate its top-level blocks (filters, widgets, tables) with `--space-section`.
6. [DS-06] A grep in CI (`frontend/scripts/check-tokens.mjs`, already used for colours) SHALL also fail on an arbitrary spacing, height, or width value (`p-[`, `gap-[`, `h-[`, `w-[`, `m-[` with a number) outside `components/ui/`.
**Lists and options**
7. [DS-07] A list of options (`BaseCombobox`, `BaseSelect`, `BaseMenu`, the command menu) SHALL NOT show any option as highlighted when it opens. AN option SHALL be highlighted only while the pointer is over it or after the first arrow key, and the highlight SHALL clear when the pointer leaves the list. THE chosen value SHALL be marked with a check icon and `aria-selected="true"`, never with the highlight colour alone. This supersedes the cmdk default (first item selected on open) everywhere.
8. [DS-08] EVERY select in the product SHALL be a `BaseCombobox` that can be searched by typing, with the same trigger look (`--surface`, `--border`, `--control-h`, chevron), the same popover (`--surface-raised`, `--shadow-lg`), and the same empty line ("No matches"). A plain native `<select>` SHALL NOT be used.
**Date and check controls**
9. [DS-09] THE Frontend SHALL have one date field, `BaseDateField` (the field already used for the Card modal dates, over `react-day-picker`), and every date input (Card modal start and end, Dashboard range and "Due on", any other) SHALL use it: a trigger that shows the date in the user's locale format, a calendar popover, a Clear control where the date is optional, and the keyboard rules of `react-day-picker`. A native `<input type="date">` SHALL NOT be used.
10. [DS-10] EVERY checkbox SHALL be `BaseCheckbox` (one size, `--radius-sm`, the accent tone when checked, a visible focus ring); EVERY on/off setting SHALL be `BaseSwitch`. THE Card modal's "Start date" and "End date" toggles SHALL use `BaseCheckbox` with a label, aligned to the date field it enables.
**Surfaces**
11. [DS-11] A form control SHALL be visually distinct from the surface it sits on: on `--canvas` a control uses `--surface`, on `--surface` it uses `--surface-sunken`, and every control has a `--border` that meets 3:1 against its background in both themes.
12. [DS-12] A focus ring SHALL never be clipped: a scroll container that holds focusable controls SHALL have inner padding at least `--border-width-focus` plus the ring offset, or the control SHALL use an inset ring.
**Verification**
13. [DS-13] THE owner's visual review (Requirement 72) SHALL check every screen against DS-01 to DS-12 at 360, 414, 960, and 1280 pixels.
### Requirement 62: Sidebar Logo, Active State, and Back Arrow
**User Story:** As a user, I want the brand in the Sidebar, to see where I am, and to go up one level with one click, so that I never get lost.
This requirement amends Requirement 47 (NAV) and the App_Header of Requirement 39.
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [NAVB-01] THE Sidebar SHALL show the RevoBoard logo at its top, and THE logo SHALL be the Sidebar toggle (round 2 within V18): a `button` with `aria-expanded` and the name "Collapse sidebar" or "Expand sidebar" that shows the logo with the word mark while the Sidebar is expanded and the logo mark only while it is collapsed. THE separate expand and collapse icon button SHALL be removed. THE logo SHALL be separated from the first Sidebar item by `--space-section`. BELOW 960 pixels, where the Sidebar is a drawer, THE drawer SHALL show the logo with the word mark (not a toggle), and the App_Header SHALL show the logo mark beside the menu button, linking to the role's home page (Dashboard for Supervisor, Manager, Admin; My Tasks for Member). THE App_Header SHALL no longer show a logo from 960 pixels.
2. [NAVB-02] THE logo SHALL use the existing transparent assets (`public/brand/`) at the `sm` logo height, with empty `alt` text inside the toggle (the button carries the name); the mark-only logo in the App_Header link SHALL have the accessible name "RevoBoard home".
3. [NAVB-03] A Sidebar item SHALL be active WHEN the current path equals its `href` or starts with its `href` followed by `/`, and THE Boards item SHALL also be active on every Board path (`/app/b/...` and `/app/boards/...`). THE active item SHALL carry `aria-current="page"` and the existing active recipe. AT most one Sidebar item SHALL be active.
4. [NAVB-04] A Top_Menu item that is a route SHALL follow the same rule within the open Board (Board is active on `/app/b/{slug}` only; List, Calendar on their path; Archived activates no Top_Menu item and shows the toolbar "Archived" control as pressed).
5. [NAVB-05] THE App_Header SHALL show, at its top left, a back arrow (`ArrowLeft`, `BaseIconButton`, name "Back to <parent>") on every page that is not a Sidebar destination, linking to the parent page in this table (Open Question 81). IT SHALL NOT use browser history.
| Page | Parent |
|---|---|
| `/app/b/{slug}`, `/list`, `/calendar`, `/archived` | `/app/boards` ("Boards") |
| `/app/profile` | the role's home page |
| `/app/invite/{token}` | `/app/boards` |
| `/app/dashboard`, `/app/my-tasks`, `/app/boards`, `/app/users` | none (Sidebar destinations) |
6. [NAVB-06] THE back arrow SHALL keep the parent's query string when the parent is the page the user came from inside the app in this tab (for example `/app/boards?q=sales&sort=updated_at`), read from a `sessionStorage` key set by the parent page; otherwise it SHALL link to the bare parent path.
7. [NAVB-07] THE App_Header SHALL be separated from the page content below it by a 1 pixel `--border` line across the full width of the app frame (`border-b border-line`), at every width (round 2 within V18).
8. [NAVB-08] FROM 960 pixels, THE Top_Menu SHALL be centred in the App_Header between the back arrow on the left and the bell and User_Menu on the right (a three-part row: left and right parts of equal flexible width, the Top_Menu in the middle), and SHALL stay centred whether or not the back arrow shows. BELOW 960 pixels the Top_Menu stays in the drawer (NAV-03).

### Requirement 63: Users Page
**User Story:** As an Admin, I want the Users page to use the whole page, list people in a fixed order, and page through them, so that a long list stays readable.
This requirement amends Requirement 53.
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [USRP-01] THE Users page SHALL use the full page content width (the shell content area minus `--space-page` on each side), with no narrower `max-width`; the table SHALL scroll inside its card, not the page, below 960 pixels.
2. [USRP-02] THE Backend SHALL return `GET /api/users` sorted by role in the order `admin`, `manager`, `supervisor`, `member`, then by `full_name` ascending (case-insensitive), then by `id`.
3. [USRP-03] `GET /api/users` SHALL accept `page` (integer from 1, default 1) and `per_page` (integer 1 to 100, default 10) and SHALL return `data` of `{ "items": [...], "page", "per_page", "total" }`. A `page` beyond the last page SHALL return an empty `items` with the real `total`. An invalid value SHALL be HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR` with `details`. THE number of queries SHALL not depend on the number of users (one count, one page).
4. [USRP-04] THE Frontend SHALL show, below the table, a pagination bar: "Showing 21 to 40 of 57", Previous and Next buttons (disabled at the ends), and page numbers with an ellipsis when there are more than 7 pages; the page SHALL be kept in the query string (`?page=`). AFTER a save, THE table SHALL reload the same page.
5. [USRP-05] EVERY save, error, and refusal of the Users page (role change, active and available toggles, create) SHALL use the Toasts of Requirement 60; `USER_HAS_OPEN_WORK` keeps its list in the ConfirmDialog (TOAST-06).

### Requirement 64: Boards List Search and Sort
**User Story:** As a user with many Boards, I want to search and sort the Boards page, so that I find a Board fast.
#### Acceptance Criteria
1. [BRDL-01] THE Boards page SHALL have, in its title block, a search field (`--control-h`, placeholder "Search boards", name "Search boards") that matches the Board name case-insensitively as the user types (debounced 250 ms), and a sort `BaseCombobox` with "Last updated", "First updated", "Newest", "Oldest" (`updated_at` desc, `updated_at` asc, `created_at` desc, `created_at` asc). THE default SHALL be "Last updated" (Open Question 79).
2. [BRDL-02] THE Backend SHALL accept on `GET /api/boards` the parameters `q` (string, trimmed, at most 100 characters; matched with `ILIKE` on `name`, with `%` and `_` escaped) and `sort` (`updated_at` or `created_at`) and `order` (`asc` or `desc`), with ties broken by `name` then `id`. An invalid `sort` or `order` SHALL be HTTP 400 `VALIDATION_ERROR`. THE access rules of Requirement 11 and Requirement 22 SHALL not change.
3. [BRDL-03] THE search and sort SHALL be kept in the query string (`?q=&sort=&order=`) so a link and the back arrow (NAVB-06) restore them. WHEN no Board matches, THE page SHALL show "No boards match \"<q>\"" with a "Clear search" button.
4. [BRDL-04] A Board's `updated_at` SHALL change on a rename, a description, visibility, or owner change, and a Column create, rename, move, or delete; it SHALL NOT change on a Card change (so the sort reflects the Board, not its traffic).
### Requirement 65: Dashboard Access and Filter Bar
**User Story:** As an Admin, Manager, or Supervisor, I want a clear filter bar, so that I can narrow the Dashboard without guessing what each control does.
This requirement amends Requirement 51 (DASH-11, DASH-19) and Decision B (Decision BM).
#### Acceptance Criteria
**Access**
1. [DASH-23] THE Backend SHALL allow an Admin to call `GET /api/analytics/dashboard` with the scope of a Manager (every Board; any `board`). A Supervisor SHALL keep the scope of DASH-19 (only the Boards it owns, as the owner confirmed). A Member SHALL still receive 403. THE other analytics routes of Requirement 10 SHALL follow the same rule.
2. [DASH-24] THE Sidebar SHALL show Dashboard to Supervisor, Manager, and Admin (NAV-01 amended), and the role's home page of an Admin SHALL be `/app/dashboard`.
**Filter bar**
3. [DASH-25] THE filters SHALL sit in one filter card under the greeting (`--surface-raised`, `--border`, `--radius-frame`, padding `--space-section`), not inline with the title. EVERY filter SHALL have a visible label above it (`text-xs`, `font-medium`, `--ink-muted`, `--space-label` gap): "Board", "Member", "From", "To". THE controls SHALL be `--control-h` high and use `--surface`, so they never match the card background.
4. [DASH-26] Board and Member SHALL be searchable `BaseCombobox` controls (DS-08) with "All boards" and "Anyone" as their first option, which is the default and is shown as the value, not as a highlighted row (DS-07).
